backend/NexusRAG.Domain/Operations/OperationsEntities.cs
csharp
using System.Text.Json;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Common;

namespace NexusRAG.Domain.Operations;

/// <summary>Append-only audit trail. TenantId is nullable so platform-level events can be recorded.</summary>
public sealed class AuditLog : Entity
{
    public Guid? TenantId { get; set; }
    public Guid? UserId { get; set; }
    public required string Action { get; set; }
    public required string ResourceType { get; set; }
    public string? ResourceId { get; set; }
    public string? DetailsJson { get; set; }

    public static AuditLog Create(Guid? tenantId, Guid? userId, string action, string resourceType,
        string? resourceId = null, object? details = null) => new()
    {
        TenantId = tenantId,
        UserId = userId,
        Action = action,
        ResourceType = resourceType,
        ResourceId = resourceId,
        DetailsJson = details is null ? null : JsonSerializer.Serialize(details),
    };
}

public sealed class UsageRecord : Entity, ITenantOwned
{
    public Guid TenantId { get; set; }
    public Guid? UserId { get; set; }
    public required string Operation { get; set; }       // query | ingest | embed | verify
    public required string Provider { get; set; }
    public required string Model { get; set; }
    public int InputTokens { get; set; }
    public int OutputTokens { get; set; }
    public int EmbeddingTokens { get; set; }
    public bool CacheHit { get; set; }
    public int LatencyMs { get; set; }
}

/// <summary>Daily aggregate per provider/model, used for free-quota warnings.</summary>
public sealed class ProviderUsage : Entity
{
    public required string Provider { get; set; }
    public required string Model { get; set; }
    public DateOnly Day { get; set; }
    public int Requests { get; set; }
    public int Failures { get; set; }
    public int RateLimitedCount { get; set; }
    public long InputTokens { get; set; }
    public long OutputTokens { get; set; }
}

/// <summary>Transactional outbox: written in the same transaction as business data, published by Workers.</summary>
public sealed class OutboxMessage : Entity
{
    public required string Type { get; set; }
    public required string Payload { get; set; }
    public DateTimeOffset? ProcessedAt { get; set; }
    public int Attempts { get; set; }
    public string? LastError { get; set; }
    public DateTimeOffset? NextAttemptAt { get; set; }
}

All 15 tables from your spec are defined now, plus RefreshTokens and OutboxMessages. The initial migration therefore covers the full schema, and the later phases only add behavior.

Part B — Application layer (Phases 3–5)

backend/NexusRAG.Application/Common/ValidationRules.cs
csharp
using FluentValidation;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Common;

public static class ValidationRules
{
    public static IRuleBuilderOptions<T, string> StrongPassword<T>(this IRuleBuilder<T, string> rule) =>
        rule.NotEmpty()
            .MinimumLength(12).MaximumLength(128)
            .Matches("[A-Z]").WithMessage("Password must contain an uppercase letter.")
            .Matches("[a-z]").WithMessage("Password must contain a lowercase letter.")
            .Matches("[0-9]").WithMessage("Password must contain a digit.")
            .Matches("[^a-zA-Z0-9]").WithMessage("Password must contain a symbol.");
}
backend/NexusRAG.Application/Security/AccessControl.cs
csharp
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using FluentValidation;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Documents;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Identity;
using NexusRAG.Domain.KnowledgeBases;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Security;

public static class NexusClaimTypes
{
    public const string Subject = "sub";
    public const string TenantId = "tid";
    public const string Email = "email";
    public const string Name = "name";
    public const string Role = "role";
    public const string Group = "grp";
}

/// <summary>
/// Everything needed to decide what a user may see. Built by the backend from the JWT,
/// later sent to the Python service as the retrieval scope (it can never be widened there).
/// </summary>
public sealed record AccessScope(Guid TenantId, Guid UserId, bool IsTenantAdmin, IReadOnlyCollection<string> Groups)
{
    public static AccessScope From(ICurrentUser user) => new(
        user.TenantId,
        user.UserId,
        user.IsInRole(RoleNames.TenantAdmin) || user.IsInRole(RoleNames.PlatformAdmin),
        user.AccessGroups);
}

public static partial class AccessGroupRules
{
    [GeneratedRegex("^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{0,63}$")]
    private static partial Regex GroupPattern();

    public static bool IsValid(string group) => GroupPattern().IsMatch(group);

    public static string[] Normalize(IEnumerable<string>? groups) =>
        groups is null
            ? []
            : groups.Select(g => g.Trim().ToLowerInvariant()).Where(g => g.Length > 0).Distinct().Order().ToArray();

    public static string[] NormalizeAndValidate(IEnumerable<string>? groups)
    {
        var normalized = Normalize(groups);
        var invalid = normalized.Where(g => !IsValid(g)).ToArray();
        if (invalid.Length > 0)
        {
            throw new ValidationException(
                $"Invalid access group(s): {string.Join(", ", invalid)}. Use lowercase letters, digits, '-' or '_'.");
        }
        return normalized;
    }
}

/// <summary>EF-translatable authorization filters (Npgsql turns the group checks into array overlap `&&`).</summary>
public static class AccessQueryExtensions
{
    public static IQueryable<KnowledgeBase> WhereAccessible(this IQueryable<KnowledgeBase> query, AccessScope scope)
    {
        query = query.Where(kb => kb.TenantId == scope.TenantId);   // defense in depth on top of query filters
        if (scope.IsTenantAdmin) return query;

        var groups = scope.Groups.ToArray();
        return query.Where(kb => kb.AllowedGroups.Length == 0 || kb.AllowedGroups.Any(g => groups.Contains(g)));
    }

    public static IQueryable<Document> WhereAccessible(this IQueryable<Document> query, AccessScope scope)
    {
        query = query.Where(d => d.TenantId == scope.TenantId);
        if (scope.IsTenantAdmin) return query;

        var groups = scope.Groups.ToArray();
        return query.Where(d =>
            (d.KnowledgeBase.AllowedGroups.Length == 0 || d.KnowledgeBase.AllowedGroups.Any(g => groups.Contains(g))) &&
            (d.AccessGroups.Length == 0 || d.AccessGroups.Any(g => groups.Contains(g))));
    }
}

using FluentValidation;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Auth;

public sealed class AuthOptions

    public const string SectionName = "Auth";
    public bool AllowTenantRegistration { get; set; }
}

public sealed record RegisterTenantRequest(
    string TenantName, string TenantSlug, string AdminEmail, string AdminDisplayName, string AdminPassword);

public sealed record LoginRequest(string Email, string Password);

public sealed record RefreshRequest(string RefreshToken);

public sealed record UserSummary(
    Guid Id, Guid TenantId, string Email, string DisplayName,
    IReadOnlyList<string> Roles, IReadOnlyList<string> AccessGroups);

public sealed record AuthResponse(
    string AccessToken, DateTimeOffset AccessTokenExpiresAt, string RefreshToken, UserSummary User);

public interface IAuthService
{
    Task<AuthResponse> RegisterTenantAsync(RegisterTenantRequest request, CancellationToken ct);
    Task<AuthResponse> LoginAsync(LoginRequest request, CancellationToken ct);
    Task<AuthResponse> RefreshAsync(RefreshRequest request, CancellationToken ct);
    Task LogoutAsync(RefreshRequest request, CancellationToken ct);
}

internal sealed class RegisterTenantRequestValidator : AbstractValidator<RegisterTenantRequest>
{
    public RegisterTenantRequestValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.TenantName).NotEmpty().MaximumLength(200);
        RuleFor(x => x.TenantSlug).NotEmpty()
            .Matches("^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{1,48}[a-z0-9]$")
            .WithMessage("Slug must be 3-50 lowercase letters, digits or hyphens.");
        RuleFor(x => x.AdminEmail).NotEmpty().EmailAddress().MaximumLength(320);
        RuleFor(x => x.AdminDisplayName).NotEmpty().MaximumLength(200);
        RuleFor(x => x.AdminPassword).StrongPassword();
    }
}

internal sealed class LoginRequestValidator : AbstractValidator<LoginRequest>
{
    public LoginRequestValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.Email).NotEmpty().MaximumLength(320);
        RuleFor(x => x.Password).NotEmpty().MaximumLength(128);
    }
}
backend/NexusRAG.Application/Auth/AuthService.cs
csharp
using FluentValidation;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Identity;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Operations;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Tenants;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Auth;

internal sealed class AuthService(
    IApplicationDbContext db,
    IPasswordService passwords,
    ITokenService tokens,
    TimeProvider time,
    IOptions<AuthOptions> authOptions,
    IValidator<RegisterTenantRequest> registerValidator,
    IValidator<LoginRequest> loginValidator,
    ILogger<AuthService> logger) : IAuthService
{
    public async Task<AuthResponse> RegisterTenantAsync(RegisterTenantRequest request, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        if (!authOptions.Value.AllowTenantRegistration)
            throw new ForbiddenException("Tenant self-registration is disabled.");

        await registerValidator.ValidateAndThrowAsync(request, ct);

        var slug = request.TenantSlug.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        var email = NormalizeEmail(request.AdminEmail);

        if (await db.Tenants.AnyAsync(t => t.Slug == slug, ct))
            throw new ConflictException("Tenant slug is already taken.");
        if (await db.Users.IgnoreQueryFilters().AnyAsync(u => u.Email == email, ct))
            throw new ConflictException("E-mail is already registered.");

        var adminRole = await db.Roles.SingleAsync(r => r.Name == RoleNames.TenantAdmin, ct);

        var tenant = new Tenant { Name = request.TenantName.Trim(), Slug = slug };
        var user = new User { TenantId = tenant.Id, Email = email, DisplayName = request.AdminDisplayName.Trim() };
        user.PasswordHash = passwords.Hash(user, request.AdminPassword);
        user.UserRoles.Add(new UserRole { UserId = user.Id, RoleId = adminRole.Id });

        db.Tenants.Add(tenant);
        db.Users.Add(user);
        db.AuditLogs.Add(AuditLog.Create(tenant.Id, user.Id, "tenant.registered", "Tenant", tenant.Id.ToString()));

        var response = IssueTokens(user, [RoleNames.TenantAdmin]);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);

        logger.LogInformation("Tenant {TenantId} registered", tenant.Id);
        return response;
    }

    public async Task<AuthResponse> LoginAsync(LoginRequest request, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        await loginValidator.ValidateAndThrowAsync(request, ct);
        var email = NormalizeEmail(request.Email);

        // Anonymous request: no tenant yet, so tenant filters are bypassed explicitly for this lookup only.
        var user = await db.Users.IgnoreQueryFilters()
            .Include(u => u.UserRoles).ThenInclude(ur => ur.Role)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(u => u.Email == email, ct);

        if (user is null)
        {
            passwords.SimulateVerification(request.Password);
            throw new UnauthorizedException();
        }

        var tenantActive = await db.Tenants.AnyAsync(t => t.Id == user.TenantId && t.IsActive, ct);
        if (!passwords.Verify(user, user.PasswordHash, request.Password) || !user.IsActive || !tenantActive)
        {
            db.AuditLogs.Add(AuditLog.Create(user.TenantId, user.Id, "auth.login_failed", "User", user.Id.ToString()));
            await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
            throw new UnauthorizedException();
        }

        user.LastLoginAt = time.GetUtcNow();
        db.AuditLogs.Add(AuditLog.Create(user.TenantId, user.Id, "auth.login", "User", user.Id.ToString()));

        var response = IssueTokens(user, RolesOf(user));
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        return response;
    }

    public async Task<AuthResponse> RefreshAsync(RefreshRequest request, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.RefreshToken)) throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid refresh token.");

        var hash = tokens.HashRefreshToken(request.RefreshToken);
        var stored = await db.RefreshTokens.IgnoreQueryFilters()
            .Include(t => t.User).ThenInclude(u => u.UserRoles).ThenInclude(ur => ur.Role)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(t => t.TokenHash == hash, ct)
            ?? throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid refresh token.");

        var now = time.GetUtcNow();

        if (stored.RevokedAt is not null)
        {
            // A rotated token was reused: treat as theft and revoke every active token of this user.
            var active = await db.RefreshTokens.Where(t => t.UserId == stored.UserId && t.RevokedAt == null).ToListAsync(ct);
            active.ForEach(t => t.RevokedAt = now);
            db.AuditLogs.Add(AuditLog.Create(stored.User.TenantId, stored.UserId, "auth.refresh_reuse_detected", "User", stored.UserId.ToString()));
            await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
            logger.LogWarning("Refresh token reuse detected for user {UserId}", stored.UserId);
            throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid refresh token.");
        }

        if (stored.ExpiresAt <= now || !stored.User.IsActive)
            throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid refresh token.");

        var response = IssueTokens(stored.User, RolesOf(stored.User));
        stored.RevokedAt = now;
        stored.ReplacedByTokenHash = tokens.HashRefreshToken(response.RefreshToken);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        return response;
    }

    public async Task LogoutAsync(RefreshRequest request, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.RefreshToken)) return;
        var hash = tokens.HashRefreshToken(request.RefreshToken);
        var stored = await db.RefreshTokens.FirstOrDefaultAsync(t => t.TokenHash == hash, ct);
        if (stored is { RevokedAt: null })
        {
            stored.RevokedAt = time.GetUtcNow();
            await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        }
    }

    private AuthResponse IssueTokens(User user, IReadOnlyList<string> roles)
    {
        var access = tokens.CreateAccessToken(user, roles);
        var rawRefresh = tokens.GenerateRefreshToken();
        db.RefreshTokens.Add(new RefreshToken
        {
            UserId = user.Id,
            TokenHash = tokens.HashRefreshToken(rawRefresh),
            ExpiresAt = time.GetUtcNow().Add(tokens.RefreshTokenLifetime),
        });
        return new AuthResponse(access.Token, access.ExpiresAt, rawRefresh, ToSummary(user, roles));
    }

    internal static UserSummary ToSummary(User user, IReadOnlyList<string> roles) =>
        new(user.Id, user.TenantId, user.Email, user.DisplayName, roles, user.AccessGroups);

    private static IReadOnlyList<string> RolesOf(User user) => user.UserRoles.Select(ur => ur.Role.Name).ToList();

    internal static string NormalizeEmail(string email) => email.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
}

How authentication works:

Access tokens are short-lived JWTs (15 minutes by default).
Refresh tokens are random, and only their hash is stored.
Rotation: every refresh issues a new refresh token and revokes the old one.
Theft detection: reusing an old refresh token revokes all of that user's sessions.
Timing equalization: a login with an unknown e-mail still runs a password verification, so it takes as long as a wrong password.
backend/NexusRAG.Application/Users/UserContracts.cs
csharp
using FluentValidation;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;
using NexusRAG.Application.Security;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Identity;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Users;

public sealed record CreateUserRequest(
    string Email, string DisplayName, string Password, string[] Roles, string[]? AccessGroups);

public sealed record UpdateUserRequest(
    string DisplayName, string[] Roles, string[]? AccessGroups, bool IsActive);

public sealed record UserDto(
    Guid Id, string Email, string DisplayName, IReadOnlyList<string> Roles, string[] AccessGroups,
    bool IsActive, DateTimeOffset CreatedAt, DateTimeOffset? LastLoginAt);

public interface IUserService
{
    Task<PagedResult<UserDto>> ListAsync(int page, int pageSize, CancellationToken ct);
    Task<UserDto> CreateAsync(CreateUserRequest request, CancellationToken ct);
    Task<UserDto> UpdateAsync(Guid id, UpdateUserRequest request, CancellationToken ct);
    Task<Auth.UserSummary> GetMeAsync(CancellationToken ct);
}

internal sealed class CreateUserRequestValidator : AbstractValidator<CreateUserRequest>
{
    public CreateUserRequestValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.Email).NotEmpty().EmailAddress().MaximumLength(320);
        RuleFor(x => x.DisplayName).NotEmpty().MaximumLength(200);
        RuleFor(x => x.Password).StrongPassword();
        RuleFor(x => x.Roles).NotEmpty();
        RuleForEach(x => x.Roles).Must(r => RoleNames.TenantAssignable.Contains(r))
            .WithMessage("Role must be one of: TenantAdmin, Editor, Viewer.");
        RuleForEach(x => x.AccessGroups).Must(g => AccessGroupRules.IsValid(g.Trim().ToLowerInvariant()))
            .WithMessage("Invalid access group.");
    }
}

internal sealed class UpdateUserRequestValidator : AbstractValidator<UpdateUserRequest>
{
    public UpdateUserRequestValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.DisplayName).NotEmpty().MaximumLength(200);
        RuleFor(x => x.Roles).NotEmpty();
        RuleForEach(x => x.Roles).Must(r => RoleNames.TenantAssignable.Contains(r))
            .WithMessage("Role must be one of: TenantAdmin, Editor, Viewer.");
        RuleForEach(x => x.AccessGroups).Must(g => AccessGroupRules.IsValid(g.Trim().ToLowerInvariant()))
            .WithMessage("Invalid access group.");
    }
}
backend/NexusRAG.Application/Users/UserService.cs
csharp
using FluentValidation;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Auth;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;
using NexusRAG.Application.Security;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Identity;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Operations;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Users;

internal sealed class UserService(
    IApplicationDbContext db,
    ICurrentUser currentUser,
    IPasswordService passwords,
    TimeProvider time,
    IValidator<CreateUserRequest> createValidator,
    IValidator<UpdateUserRequest> updateValidator) : IUserService
{
    public async Task<PagedResult<UserDto>> ListAsync(int page, int pageSize, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        page = Math.Max(1, page);
        pageSize = Math.Clamp(pageSize, 1, 100);

        var query = db.Users.AsNoTracking();        // tenant query filter applies automatically
        var total = await query.CountAsync(ct);
        var items = await query.OrderBy(u => u.Email)
            .Skip((page - 1) * pageSize).Take(pageSize)
            .Select(u => new UserDto(u.Id, u.Email, u.DisplayName,
                u.UserRoles.Select(ur => ur.Role.Name).ToList(),
                u.AccessGroups, u.IsActive, u.CreatedAt, u.LastLoginAt))
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        return new PagedResult<UserDto>(items, page, pageSize, total);
    }

    public async Task<UserDto> CreateAsync(CreateUserRequest request, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        await createValidator.ValidateAndThrowAsync(request, ct);
        var email = AuthService.NormalizeEmail(request.Email);

        if (await db.Users.IgnoreQueryFilters().AnyAsync(u => u.Email == email, ct))
            throw new ConflictException("E-mail is already registered.");

        var roles = await LoadRolesAsync(request.Roles, ct);
        var user = new User
        {
            TenantId = currentUser.TenantId,
            Email = email,
            DisplayName = request.DisplayName.Trim(),
            AccessGroups = AccessGroupRules.NormalizeAndValidate(request.AccessGroups),
        };
        user.PasswordHash = passwords.Hash(user, request.Password);
        user.UserRoles.AddRange(roles.Select(r => new UserRole { UserId = user.Id, RoleId = r.Id }));

        db.Users.Add(user);
        db.AuditLogs.Add(AuditLog.Create(currentUser.TenantId, currentUser.UserId, "user.created", "User",
            user.Id.ToString(), new { roles = request.Roles }));
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);

        return ToDto(user, roles.Select(r => r.Name).ToList());
    }

    public async Task<UserDto> UpdateAsync(Guid id, UpdateUserRequest request, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        await updateValidator.ValidateAndThrowAsync(request, ct);

        var user = await db.Users.Include(u => u.UserRoles).ThenInclude(ur => ur.Role)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(u => u.Id == id, ct) ?? throw new NotFoundException("User", id);

        if (user.Id == currentUser.UserId)
        {
            if (!request.IsActive) throw new ValidationException("You cannot deactivate your own account.");
            if (!request.Roles.Contains(RoleNames.TenantAdmin) && currentUser.IsInRole(RoleNames.TenantAdmin))
                throw new ValidationException("You cannot remove your own TenantAdmin role.");
        }

        var roles = await LoadRolesAsync(request.Roles, ct);
        user.DisplayName = request.DisplayName.Trim();
        user.AccessGroups = AccessGroupRules.NormalizeAndValidate(request.AccessGroups);
        user.UserRoles.Clear();
        user.UserRoles.AddRange(roles.Select(r => new UserRole { UserId = user.Id, RoleId = r.Id }));

        if (user.IsActive && !request.IsActive)
        {
            var now = time.GetUtcNow();
            var tokens = await db.RefreshTokens.Where(t => t.UserId == user.Id && t.RevokedAt == null).ToListAsync(ct);
            tokens.ForEach(t => t.RevokedAt = now);
        }
        user.IsActive = request.IsActive;

        db.AuditLogs.Add(AuditLog.Create(currentUser.TenantId, currentUser.UserId, "user.updated", "User",
            user.Id.ToString(), new { roles = request.Roles, request.IsActive }));
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);

        return ToDto(user, roles.Select(r => r.Name).ToList());
    }

    public async Task<UserSummary> GetMeAsync(CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var user = await db.Users.AsNoTracking().Include(u => u.UserRoles).ThenInclude(ur => ur.Role)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(u => u.Id == currentUser.UserId, ct)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException("User", currentUser.UserId);
        return AuthService.ToSummary(user, user.UserRoles.Select(ur => ur.Role.Name).ToList());
    }

    private async Task<List<Role>> LoadRolesAsync(IEnumerable<string> names, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var distinct = names.Distinct().ToArray();
        var roles = await db.Roles.Where(r => distinct.Contains(r.Name)).ToListAsync(ct);
        if (roles.Count != distinct.Length) throw new ValidationException("One or more roles do not exist.");
        return roles;
    }

    private static UserDto ToDto(User u, IReadOnlyList<string> roles) =>
        new(u.Id, u.Email, u.DisplayName, roles, u.AccessGroups, u.IsActive, u.CreatedAt, u.LastLoginAt);
}

public interface IKnowledgeBaseService

internal sealed class CreateKnowledgeBaseRequestValidator : AbstractValidator<CreateKnowledgeBaseRequest>
{
    public CreateKnowledgeBaseRequestValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.Name).NotEmpty().MaximumLength(200);
        RuleFor(x => x.Description).MaximumLength(2000);
        RuleForEach(x => x.AllowedGroups).Must(g => AccessGroupRules.IsValid(g.Trim().ToLowerInvariant()))

backend/NexusRAG.Application/KnowledgeBases/KnowledgeBaseService.cs
csharp
using FluentValidation;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;
using NexusRAG.Application.Security;
using NexusRAG.Domain.KnowledgeBases;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Operations;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.KnowledgeBases;

internal sealed class KnowledgeBaseService(
    IApplicationDbContext db,
    ICurrentUser currentUser,
    IValidator<CreateKnowledgeBaseRequest> validator) : IKnowledgeBaseService
{
    public async Task<KnowledgeBaseDto> CreateAsync(CreateKnowledgeBaseRequest request, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        await validator.ValidateAndThrowAsync(request, ct);
        var scope = AccessScope.From(currentUser);
        var groups = AccessGroupRules.NormalizeAndValidate(request.AllowedGroups);

        // Prevent editors from creating a KB they would immediately lose access to.
        if (!scope.IsTenantAdmin && groups.Length > 0 && !groups.Intersect(scope.Groups).Any())
            throw new ValidationException("You must belong to at least one allowed group, or leave the groups empty.");

        var name = request.Name.Trim();
        if (await db.KnowledgeBases.AnyAsync(k => k.Name == name, ct))
            throw new ConflictException($"A knowledge base named '{name}' already exists.");

        var kb = new KnowledgeBase
        {
            TenantId = scope.TenantId,
            Name = name,
            Description = request.Description?.Trim(),
            AllowedGroups = groups,
            CreatedByUserId = scope.UserId,
        };
        db.KnowledgeBases.Add(kb);
        db.AuditLogs.Add(AuditLog.Create(scope.TenantId, scope.UserId, "knowledge_base.created", "KnowledgeBase", kb.Id.ToString()));
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);

        return new KnowledgeBaseDto(kb.Id, kb.Name, kb.Description, kb.AllowedGroups, kb.ContentVersion, 0, kb.CreatedAt);
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<KnowledgeBaseDto>> ListAsync(CancellationToken ct) =>
        await Project(db.KnowledgeBases.AsNoTracking().WhereAccessible(AccessScope.From(currentUser)))
            .OrderBy(k => k.Name).ToListAsync(ct);

    public async Task<KnowledgeBaseDto> GetAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct) =>
        await Project(db.KnowledgeBases.AsNoTracking().WhereAccessible(AccessScope.From(currentUser)).Where(k => k.Id == id))
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct) ?? throw new NotFoundException("Knowledge base", id);

    private IQueryable<KnowledgeBaseDto> Project(IQueryable<KnowledgeBase> query) =>
        query.Select(k => new KnowledgeBaseDto(
            k.Id, k.Name, k.Description, k.AllowedGroups, k.ContentVersion,
            db.Documents.Count(d => d.KnowledgeBaseId == k.Id), k.CreatedAt));
}

backend/NexusRAG.Application/Documents/FileTypes.cs
csharp
namespace NexusRAG.Application.Documents;

public static class SupportedFileTypes
{
    public static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> ContentTypes =
        new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
        {
            [".pdf"] = "application/pdf",
            [".docx"] = "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",
            [".xlsx"] = "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
            [".pptx"] = "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation",
            [".txt"] = "text/plain",
            [".md"] = "text/markdown",
            [".csv"] = "text/csv",
        };
}

/// <summary>
/// Checks magic bytes so a renamed executable cannot pass as a PDF. The Python parser
/// performs deep structural validation later (Phase 7).
/// </summary>
public static class FileSignatureValidator
{
    private static ReadOnlySpan<byte> PdfMagic => "%PDF-"u8;
    private static ReadOnlySpan<byte> ZipMagic => [0x50, 0x4B, 0x03, 0x04];

    public static bool IsValid(string extension, ReadOnlySpan<byte> header) => extension.ToLowerInvariant() switch
    {
        ".pdf" => header.StartsWith(PdfMagic),
        ".docx" or ".xlsx" or ".pptx" => header.StartsWith(ZipMagic),
        ".txt" or ".md" or ".csv" => IsLikelyText(header),
        _ => false,
    };

    private static bool IsLikelyText(ReadOnlySpan<byte> header) =>
        header.IndexOf((byte)0) < 0 && !header.StartsWith(PdfMagic) && !header.StartsWith(ZipMagic)
        && !header.StartsWith("MZ"u8);   // Windows executables
}
backend/NexusRAG.Application/Documents/BufferedUpload.cs
csharp
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using FluentValidation;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Documents;

/// <summary>
/// Streams an upload into a temp file (deleted on dispose) while computing SHA-256 and enforcing
/// the size limit on the actual bytes, not the client-declared length. Never loads the file into memory.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class BufferedUpload : IAsyncDisposable
{
    private const int BufferSize = 81_920;

    private BufferedUpload(FileStream stream, string sha256Hex, long length, byte[] header)
    {
        Stream = stream;
        Sha256Hex = sha256Hex;
        Length = length;
        Header = header;
    }

    public FileStream Stream { get; }
    public string Sha256Hex { get; }
    public long Length { get; }
    public byte[] Header { get; }

    public static async Task<BufferedUpload> CreateAsync(Stream source, long maxBytes, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var path = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"nexusrag-{Guid.NewGuid():N}.upload");
        var file = new FileStream(path, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.ReadWrite, FileShare.None, BufferSize,
            FileOptions.DeleteOnClose | FileOptions.Asynchronous);
        try
        {
            using var hash = IncrementalHash.CreateHash(HashAlgorithmName.SHA256);
            var buffer = new byte[BufferSize];
            long total = 0;
            int read;
            while ((read = await source.ReadAsync(buffer.AsMemory(0, BufferSize), ct)) > 0)
            {
                total += read;
                if (total > maxBytes)
                    throw new ValidationException($"File exceeds the maximum size of {maxBytes / (1024 * 1024)} MB.");
                hash.AppendData(buffer, 0, read);
                await file.WriteAsync(buffer.AsMemory(0, read), ct);
            }

            if (total == 0) throw new ValidationException("File is empty.");

            await file.FlushAsync(ct);
            file.Position = 0;
            var header = new byte[(int)Math.Min(8192, total)];
            await file.ReadExactlyAsync(header, ct);
            file.Position = 0;

            return new BufferedUpload(file, Convert.ToHexStringLower(hash.GetHashAndReset()), total, header);
        }
        catch
        {
            await file.DisposeAsync();
            throw;
        }
        finally
        {
            await source.DisposeAsync();
        }
    }

    public ValueTask DisposeAsync() => Stream.DisposeAsync();
}
backend/NexusRAG.Application/Documents/DocumentService.cs
csharp
using System.Text.Json;
using FluentValidation;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;
using NexusRAG.Application.Security;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Documents;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Ingestion;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Operations;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Documents;

internal sealed class DocumentService(
    IApplicationDbContext db,
    IObjectStorage storage,
    ICurrentUser currentUser,
    TimeProvider time,
    IOptions<DocumentUploadOptions> options,
    ILogger<DocumentService> logger) : IDocumentService
{
    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new(JsonSerializerDefaults.Web);
    private static readonly IngestionStatus[] TerminalStatuses =
        [IngestionStatus.Indexed, IngestionStatus.Failed, IngestionStatus.Cancelled];

    private readonly DocumentUploadOptions _options = options.Value;
    private AccessScope Scope => AccessScope.From(currentUser);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ upload
    public async Task<UploadResult> UploadAsync(UploadDocumentCommand command, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var scope = Scope;
        var fileName = SanitizeFileName(command.File.FileName);
        var extension = Path.GetExtension(fileName).ToLowerInvariant();

        if (!SupportedFileTypes.ContentTypes.TryGetValue(extension, out var contentType))
            throw new ValidationException(
                $"Unsupported file type '{extension}'. Allowed: {string.Join(", ", SupportedFileTypes.ContentTypes.Keys)}.");
        if (command.File.Length > _options.MaxFileSizeBytes)
            throw new ValidationException($"File exceeds the maximum size of {_options.MaxFileSizeBytes / (1024 * 1024)} MB.");

        var groups = AccessGroupRules.NormalizeAndValidate(command.AccessGroups);

        var kb = await db.KnowledgeBases.WhereAccessible(scope)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(k => k.Id == command.KnowledgeBaseId, ct)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException("Knowledge base", command.KnowledgeBaseId);

        await using var upload = await BufferedUpload.CreateAsync(command.File.OpenReadStream(), _options.MaxFileSizeBytes, ct);

        if (!FileSignatureValidator.IsValid(extension, upload.Header))
            throw new ValidationException("File content does not match its extension.");

        Document document;
        if (command.DocumentId is { } existingId)
        {
            // New version of an existing document
            document = await db.Documents.Include(d => d.Versions).WhereAccessible(scope)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(d => d.Id == existingId, ct)
                ?? throw new NotFoundException("Document", existingId);

            if (document.KnowledgeBaseId != kb.Id)
                throw new ValidationException("The document belongs to a different knowledge base.");
            if (!string.Equals(document.FileExtension, extension, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                throw new ValidationException("A new version must have the same file type as the original.");

            var current = document.Versions.FirstOrDefault(v => v.VersionNumber == document.CurrentVersion);
            if (current?.ContentHash == upload.Sha256Hex)
                throw new ConflictException("This file is identical to the current version.");

            document.FileName = fileName;
            document.CurrentVersion += 1;
            if (groups.Length > 0) document.AccessGroups = groups;
        }
        else
        {
            // Duplicate detection inside the knowledge base (current versions only)
            var duplicateId = await db.Documents
                .Where(d => d.KnowledgeBaseId == kb.Id)
                .SelectMany(d => d.Versions
                    .Where(v => v.VersionNumber == d.CurrentVersion && v.ContentHash == upload.Sha256Hex)
                    .Select(v => (Guid?)d.Id))
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);
            if (duplicateId is not null)
                throw new ConflictException($"An identical document already exists in this knowledge base (id: {duplicateId}).");

            document = new Document
            {
                TenantId = scope.TenantId,
                KnowledgeBaseId = kb.Id,
                FileName = fileName,
                ContentType = contentType,
                FileExtension = extension,
                CurrentVersion = 1,
                AccessGroups = groups,
                UploadedByUserId = scope.UserId,
            };
            db.Documents.Add(document);
        }

        var objectKey = $"tenants/{scope.TenantId}/documents/{document.Id}/v{document.CurrentVersion}/original{extension}";
        var version = new DocumentVersion
        {
            TenantId = scope.TenantId,
            DocumentId = document.Id,
            VersionNumber = document.CurrentVersion,
            ObjectKey = objectKey,
            SizeBytes = upload.Length,
            ContentHash = upload.Sha256Hex,
        };
        var job = new IngestionJob { TenantId = scope.TenantId, DocumentId = document.Id, DocumentVersionId = version.Id };

        db.DocumentVersions.Add(version);
        db.IngestionJobs.Add(job);
        db.OutboxMessages.Add(CreateIngestOutbox(document, version, job));
        db.AuditLogs.Add(AuditLog.Create(scope.TenantId, scope.UserId, "document.uploaded", "Document",
            document.Id.ToString(), new { version = version.VersionNumber, size = upload.Length }));
        kb.BumpContentVersion();

        // 1) store the file, 2) commit metadata + outbox atomically, 3) compensate on failure
        await storage.PutAsync(objectKey, upload.Stream, contentType, ct);
        try
        {
            await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        }
        catch
        {
            db.DiscardChanges();
            await TryDeleteObjectAsync(objectKey);
            throw;
        }

        logger.LogInformation("Document {DocumentId} v{Version} uploaded ({Size} bytes), job {JobId}",
            document.Id, version.VersionNumber, upload.Length, job.Id);

        return new UploadResult(document.Id, version.VersionNumber, job.Id, job.Status);
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<BulkUploadItemResult>> BulkUploadAsync(
        Guid knowledgeBaseId, IReadOnlyList<UploadFile> files, IReadOnlyList<string> accessGroups, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        if (files.Count == 0) throw new ValidationException("No files were provided.");
        if (files.Count > _options.MaxBulkFiles)
            throw new ValidationException($"A bulk upload may contain at most {_options.MaxBulkFiles} files.");

        var results = new List<BulkUploadItemResult>(files.Count);
        foreach (var file in files)
        {
            try
            {
                var result = await UploadAsync(new UploadDocumentCommand(knowledgeBaseId, null, file, accessGroups), ct);
                results.Add(new BulkUploadItemResult(file.FileName, true, result, null));
            }
            catch (Exception ex) when (ex is ValidationException or ConflictException or NotFoundException)
            {
                results.Add(new BulkUploadItemResult(file.FileName, false, null, ex.Message));
            }
        }
        return results;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ queries
    public async Task<PagedResult<DocumentDto>> ListAsync(Guid knowledgeBaseId, int page, int pageSize, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var scope = Scope;
        page = Math.Max(1, page);
        pageSize = Math.Clamp(pageSize, 1, 100);

        if (!await db.KnowledgeBases.WhereAccessible(scope).AnyAsync(k => k.Id == knowledgeBaseId, ct))
            throw new NotFoundException("Knowledge base", knowledgeBaseId);

        var query = db.Documents.AsNoTracking().WhereAccessible(scope).Where(d => d.KnowledgeBaseId == knowledgeBaseId);
        var total = await query.CountAsync(ct);
        var items = await ProjectDocuments(query.OrderByDescending(d => d.CreatedAt)
                .Skip((page - 1) * pageSize).Take(pageSize))
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        return new PagedResult<DocumentDto>(items, page, pageSize, total);
    }

    public async Task<DocumentDto> GetAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct) =>
        await ProjectDocuments(db.Documents.AsNoTracking().WhereAccessible(Scope).Where(d => d.Id == id))
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct) ?? throw new NotFoundException("Document", id);

    public async Task<DocumentStatusDto> GetStatusAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var document = await GetAccessibleDocumentAsync(id, ct);
        var job = await db.IngestionJobs.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(j => j.DocumentId == document.Id && j.DocumentVersion.VersionNumber == document.CurrentVersion)
            .OrderByDescending(j => j.CreatedAt)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct) ?? throw new NotFoundException("Ingestion job for document", id);

        return ToStatusDto(document.Id, document.CurrentVersion, job);
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<DocumentVersionDto>> GetVersionsAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var document = await GetAccessibleDocumentAsync(id, ct);
        return await db.DocumentVersions.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(v => v.DocumentId == document.Id)
            .OrderByDescending(v => v.VersionNumber)
            .Select(v => new DocumentVersionDto(v.VersionNumber, v.SizeBytes, v.ContentHash, v.Status, v.ChunkCount, v.CreatedAt))
            .ToListAsync(ct);
    }

    public async Task<DocumentContent> OpenContentAsync(Guid id, int? version, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var document = await GetAccessibleDocumentAsync(id, ct);
        var number = version ?? document.CurrentVersion;
        var objectKey = await db.DocumentVersions.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(v => v.DocumentId == document.Id && v.VersionNumber == number)
            .Select(v => v.ObjectKey)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct) ?? throw new NotFoundException("Document version", number);

        var stream = await storage.OpenReadAsync(objectKey, ct);
        return new DocumentContent(stream, document.ContentType, document.FileName);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ commands
    public async Task DeleteAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var scope = Scope;
        var document = await GetAccessibleDocumentAsync(id, ct, includeVersions: true);
        var now = time.GetUtcNow();

        document.IsDeleted = true;
        document.DeletedAt = now;

        var activeJobs = await db.IngestionJobs
            .Where(j => j.DocumentId == document.Id && !TerminalStatuses.Contains(j.Status))
            .ToListAsync(ct);
        foreach (var job in activeJobs.Where(j => IngestionStateMachine.CanTransition(j.Status, IngestionStatus.Cancelled)))
            job.TransitionTo(IngestionStatus.Cancelled, now, error: "Document deleted");

        db.OutboxMessages.Add(NewOutbox(MessageTypes.DeleteRequested, new DocumentDeleteRequestedMessage(
            Guid.CreateVersion7(), MessageTypes.DeleteRequested, 1,
            document.TenantId, document.KnowledgeBaseId, document.Id, now)));
        db.AuditLogs.Add(AuditLog.Create(scope.TenantId, scope.UserId, "document.deleted", "Document", document.Id.ToString()));
        document.KnowledgeBase.BumpContentVersion();

        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);

        // Original files are removed after the metadata commit; vectors are removed by the Python worker (Phase 6/9).
        foreach (var v in document.Versions) await TryDeleteObjectAsync(v.ObjectKey);
    }

    public async Task<DocumentStatusDto> ReindexAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var scope = Scope;
        var document = await GetAccessibleDocumentAsync(id, ct, includeVersions: true);
        var version = document.Versions.Single(v => v.VersionNumber == document.CurrentVersion);

        if (await db.IngestionJobs.AnyAsync(j => j.DocumentVersionId == version.Id && !TerminalStatuses.Contains(j.Status), ct))
            throw new ConflictException("Ingestion is already in progress for this document.");

        var lastAttempt = await db.IngestionJobs.Where(j => j.DocumentVersionId == version.Id)
            .MaxAsync(j => (int?)j.Attempt, ct) ?? 0;

        var job = new IngestionJob
        {
            TenantId = scope.TenantId,
            DocumentId = document.Id,
            DocumentVersionId = version.Id,
            Attempt = lastAttempt + 1,
        };
        version.Status = IngestionStatus.Uploaded;

        db.IngestionJobs.Add(job);
        db.OutboxMessages.Add(CreateIngestOutbox(document, version, job));
        db.AuditLogs.Add(AuditLog.Create(scope.TenantId, scope.UserId, "document.reindex_requested", "Document", document.Id.ToString()));
        document.KnowledgeBase.BumpContentVersion();

        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        return ToStatusDto(document.Id, version.VersionNumber, job);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers
    private async Task<Document> GetAccessibleDocumentAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct, bool includeVersions = false)
    {
        IQueryable<Document> query = db.Documents.Include(d => d.KnowledgeBase);
        if (includeVersions) query = query.Include(d => d.Versions);
        return await query.WhereAccessible(Scope).FirstOrDefaultAsync(d => d.Id == id, ct)
               ?? throw new NotFoundException("Document", id);
    }

    private static IQueryable<DocumentDto> ProjectDocuments(IQueryable<Document> query) =>
        query
            .Select(d => new { d, v = d.Versions.FirstOrDefault(v => v.VersionNumber == d.CurrentVersion) })
            .Select(x => new DocumentDto(
                x.d.Id, x.d.KnowledgeBaseId, x.d.FileName, x.d.ContentType, x.d.CurrentVersion,
                x.v != null ? x.v.Status : IngestionStatus.Uploaded,
                x.v != null ? x.v.SizeBytes : 0,
                x.v != null ? x.v.ChunkCount : 0,
                x.d.AccessGroups, x.d.CreatedAt, x.d.UpdatedAt));

    private static DocumentStatusDto ToStatusDto(Guid documentId, int versionNumber, IngestionJob job) =>
        new(documentId, versionNumber, job.Id, job.Status, job.ProgressPercent, job.Attempt,
            job.LastError, job.StartedAt, job.CompletedAt);

    private OutboxMessage CreateIngestOutbox(Document d, DocumentVersion v, IngestionJob job) =>
        NewOutbox(MessageTypes.IngestRequested, new IngestionRequestedMessage(
            MessageId: Guid.CreateVersion7(),
            MessageType: MessageTypes.IngestRequested,
            SchemaVersion: 1,
            CorrelationId: job.Id,
            TenantId: d.TenantId,
            KnowledgeBaseId: d.KnowledgeBaseId,
            DocumentId: d.Id,
            DocumentVersion: v.VersionNumber,
            JobId: job.Id,
            ObjectKey: v.ObjectKey,
            FileName: d.FileName,
            ContentType: d.ContentType,
            ContentHash: v.ContentHash,
            AccessGroups: d.AccessGroups,
            RequestedAt: time.GetUtcNow()));

    private static OutboxMessage NewOutbox<T>(string type, T payload) =>
        new() { Type = type, Payload = JsonSerializer.Serialize(payload, JsonOptions) };

    private async Task TryDeleteObjectAsync(string key)
    {
        try
        {
            await storage.DeleteAsync(key, CancellationToken.None);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            logger.LogWarning(ex, "Failed to delete object {ObjectKey}; it may need manual cleanup", key);
        }
    }

    private static string SanitizeFileName(string fileName)
    {
        var name = Path.GetFileName(fileName.Replace('\\', '/'));
        var invalid = Path.GetInvalidFileNameChars();
        var cleaned = new string(name.Where(c => !char.IsControl(c) && !invalid.Contains(c)).ToArray()).Trim();
        if (cleaned.Length == 0) throw new ValidationException("File name is invalid.");
        return cleaned.Length <= 255 ? cleaned : cleaned[^255..];
    }
}

How an upload is processed:

Validate the extension, size, magic bytes, and access groups.
Buffer to a temp file, computing the SHA-256 hash as the bytes stream in.
Detect duplicates by comparing hashes.
Store the file in S3 under a versioned key.
Commit in one transaction: the metadata, an ingestion job, an outbox message, an audit log entry, and the knowledge-base version bump.
Compensate on failure: if the database commit fails, delete the stored file.

The outbox message stays pending until Phase 6 adds the worker that publishes it to RabbitMQ.

backend/NexusRAG.Infrastructure/Persistence/NexusRagDbContext.cs
csharp
using System.Reflection;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Chat;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Common;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Documents;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Identity;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Ingestion;
using NexusRAG.Domain.KnowledgeBases;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Operations;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Tenants;

namespace NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Persistence;

public sealed class NexusRagDbContext(
    DbContextOptions<NexusRagDbContext> options,
    ITenantContext tenantContext,
    TimeProvider timeProvider) : DbContext(options), IApplicationDbContext
{
    public DbSet<Tenant> Tenants => Set<Tenant>();
    public DbSet<User> Users => Set<User>();
    public DbSet<Role> Roles => Set<Role>();
    public DbSet<UserRole> UserRoles => Set<UserRole>();
    public DbSet<RefreshToken> RefreshTokens => Set<RefreshToken>();
    public DbSet<KnowledgeBase> KnowledgeBases => Set<KnowledgeBase>();
    public DbSet<Document> Documents => Set<Document>();
    public DbSet<DocumentVersion> DocumentVersions => Set<DocumentVersion>();
    public DbSet<IngestionJob> IngestionJobs => Set<IngestionJob>();
    public DbSet<Conversation> Conversations => Set<Conversation>();
    public DbSet<Message> Messages => Set<Message>();
    public DbSet<Citation> Citations => Set<Citation>();
    public DbSet<VerificationResult> VerificationResults => Set<VerificationResult>();
    public DbSet<AuditLog> AuditLogs => Set<AuditLog>();
    public DbSet<UsageRecord> UsageRecords => Set<UsageRecord>();
    public DbSet<ProviderUsage> ProviderUsages => Set<ProviderUsage>();
    public DbSet<OutboxMessage> OutboxMessages => Set<OutboxMessage>();

    // Referenced by global query filters; EF re-evaluates them for every context instance.
    public Guid? CurrentTenantId => tenantContext.TenantId;
    public bool BypassTenantFilter => tenantContext.BypassTenantFilter;

    public void DiscardChanges() => ChangeTracker.Clear();

    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        modelBuilder.ApplyConfigurationsFromAssembly(typeof(NexusRagDbContext).Assembly);

        var tenantTypes = modelBuilder.Model.GetEntityTypes()
            .Select(e => e.ClrType)
            .Where(t => typeof(ITenantOwned).IsAssignableFrom(t))
            .ToList();

        foreach (var clrType in tenantTypes)
        {
            var method = typeof(ISoftDeletable).IsAssignableFrom(clrType)
                ? nameof(ApplyTenantAndSoftDeleteFilter)
                : nameof(ApplyTenantFilter);

            typeof(NexusRagDbContext)
                .GetMethod(method, BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance)!
                .MakeGenericMethod(clrType)
                .Invoke(this, [modelBuilder]);
        }
    }

    private void ApplyTenantFilter<T>(ModelBuilder modelBuilder) where T : class, ITenantOwned =>
        modelBuilder.Entity<T>().HasQueryFilter(e => BypassTenantFilter || e.TenantId == CurrentTenantId);

    private void ApplyTenantAndSoftDeleteFilter<T>(ModelBuilder modelBuilder) where T : class, ITenantOwned, ISoftDeletable =>
        modelBuilder.Entity<T>().HasQueryFilter(e => (BypassTenantFilter || e.TenantId == CurrentTenantId) && !e.IsDeleted);

    public override Task<int> SaveChangesAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var now = timeProvider.GetUtcNow();
        foreach (var entry in ChangeTracker.Entries())
        {
            if (entry.Entity is Entity entity)
            {
                if (entry.State == EntityState.Added && entity.CreatedAt == default) entity.CreatedAt = now;
                if (entry.State == EntityState.Modified) entity.UpdatedAt = now;
            }

            if (entry.Entity is ITenantOwned owned && entry.State is EntityState.Added or EntityState.Modified)
                EnforceTenant(owned);
        }
        return base.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    /// <summary>Last line of defense: a request can never write rows for another tenant.</summary>
    private void EnforceTenant(ITenantOwned owned)
    {
        if (CurrentTenantId is { } tenantId && !BypassTenantFilter)
        {
            if (owned.TenantId == Guid.Empty) owned.TenantId = tenantId;
            else if (owned.TenantId != tenantId)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Cross-tenant write blocked.");
        }
        else if (owned.TenantId == Guid.Empty)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException($"{owned.GetType().Name} requires a TenantId.");
        }
    }
}

Tenant isolation is enforced in three layers:

Global query filters on every tenant-owned table, so a query can never return another tenant's rows.
A SaveChanges guard that blocks any write carrying another tenant's ID.
Explicit WhereAccessible filters in the services, on top of the first two.

backend/NexusRAG.Infrastructure/Persistence/DesignTimeDbContextFactory.cs
csharp
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Design;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Tenancy;

namespace NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Persistence;

/// <summary>Used only by `dotnet ef` (migrations). Never used at runtime.</summary>
public sealed class DesignTimeDbContextFactory : IDesignTimeDbContextFactory<NexusRagDbContext>
{
    public NexusRagDbContext CreateDbContext(string[] args)
    {
        var connectionString = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("NEXUSRAG_DESIGN_CONNECTION")
            ?? "Host=localhost;Port=5432;Database=nexusrag;Username=nexusrag;Password=design-time-only";

        var options = new DbContextOptionsBuilder<NexusRagDbContext>().UseNpgsql(connectionString).Options;
        return new NexusRagDbContext(options, new SystemTenantContext(), TimeProvider.System);
    }
}
backend/NexusRAG.Infrastructure/Persistence/StartupInitializer.cs
csharp
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Storage;

namespace NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Persistence;

public static class StartupInitializer
{
    /// <summary>Applies migrations and creates the storage bucket when enabled (Development defaults).</summary>
    public static async Task InitializeInfrastructureAsync(this IServiceProvider services, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        using var scope = services.CreateScope();
        var config = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<IConfiguration>();
        var logger = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<ILoggerFactory>().CreateLogger("NexusRAG.Startup");

        if (config.GetValue<bool>("Database:MigrateOnStartup"))
        {
            var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<NexusRagDbContext>();
            await db.Database.MigrateAsync(ct);
            logger.LogInformation("Database migrations applied");
        }

        if (config.GetValue<bool>("Storage:EnsureBucketOnStartup"))
        {
            try
            {
                await scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<S3ObjectStorage>().EnsureBucketAsync(ct);
                logger.LogInformation("Object storage bucket is ready");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                logger.LogWarning(ex, "Could not ensure the storage bucket; uploads will fail until storage is reachable");
            }
        }
    }
}
backend/NexusRAG.Infrastructure/Identity/IdentityServices.cs
csharp
using System.Buffers.Text;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.Security.Claims;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.JsonWebTokens;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Security;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Identity;

namespace NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Identity;

public sealed class JwtOptions
{
    public const string SectionName = "Jwt";

    [Required] public string Issuer { get; set; } = "nexusrag";
    [Required] public string Audience { get; set; } = "nexusrag-clients";
    [Required, MinLength(32)] public string SigningKey { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    [Range(1, 120)] public int AccessTokenMinutes { get; set; } = 15;
    [Range(1, 90)] public int RefreshTokenDays { get; set; } = 7;
}

internal sealed class JwtTokenService(IOptions<JwtOptions> options, TimeProvider time) : ITokenService
{
    private readonly JwtOptions _options = options.Value;
    private readonly JsonWebTokenHandler _handler = new();

    public TimeSpan RefreshTokenLifetime => TimeSpan.FromDays(_options.RefreshTokenDays);

    public AccessToken CreateAccessToken(User user, IReadOnlyCollection<string> roles)
    {
        var now = time.GetUtcNow();
        var expires = now.AddMinutes(_options.AccessTokenMinutes);

        var claims = new List<Claim>
        {
            new(NexusClaimTypes.Subject, user.Id.ToString()),
            new(JwtRegisteredClaimNames.Jti, Guid.NewGuid().ToString()),
            new(NexusClaimTypes.TenantId, user.TenantId.ToString()),
            new(NexusClaimTypes.Email, user.Email),
            new(NexusClaimTypes.Name, user.DisplayName),
        };
        claims.AddRange(roles.Select(r => new Claim(NexusClaimTypes.Role, r)));
        claims.AddRange(user.AccessGroups.Select(g => new Claim(NexusClaimTypes.Group, g)));

        var descriptor = new SecurityTokenDescriptor
        {
            Issuer = _options.Issuer,
            Audience = _options.Audience,
            Subject = new ClaimsIdentity(claims),
            IssuedAt = now.UtcDateTime,
            NotBefore = now.UtcDateTime,
            Expires = expires.UtcDateTime,
            SigningCredentials = new SigningCredentials(
                new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(_options.SigningKey)), SecurityAlgorithms.HmacSha256),
        };

        return new AccessToken(_handler.CreateToken(descriptor), expires);
    }

    public string GenerateRefreshToken() => Base64Url.EncodeToString(RandomNumberGenerator.GetBytes(64));

    public string HashRefreshToken(string refreshToken) =>
        Convert.ToHexStringLower(SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(refreshToken)));
}

/// <summary>Wraps ASP.NET Core Identity's PBKDF2 password hasher without pulling in full Identity.</summary>
internal sealed class PasswordService : IPasswordService
{
    private readonly PasswordHasher<User> _hasher = new();
    private readonly User _dummyUser = new() { Email = "dummy@invalid", DisplayName = "dummy" };
    private readonly string _dummyHash;

    public PasswordService() => _dummyHash = _hasher.HashPassword(_dummyUser, "dummy-password-for-timing");

    public string Hash(User user, string password) => _hasher.HashPassword(user, password);

    public bool Verify(User user, string passwordHash, string password) =>
        _hasher.VerifyHashedPassword(user, passwordHash, password)
            is PasswordVerificationResult.Success or PasswordVerificationResult.SuccessRehashNeeded;

    public void SimulateVerification(string password) => _hasher.VerifyHashedPassword(_dummyUser, _dummyHash, password);
}
backend/NexusRAG.Infrastructure/Storage/S3ObjectStorage.cs
csharp
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.Net;
using Amazon.Runtime;
using Amazon.S3;
using Amazon.S3.Model;
using Amazon.S3.Util;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Diagnostics.HealthChecks;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;

namespace NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Storage;

public sealed class StorageOptions
{
    public const string SectionName = "Storage";

    [Required, Url] public string ServiceUrl { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    [Required] public string AccessKey { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    [Required] public string SecretKey { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    [Required] public string Bucket { get; set; } = "nexusrag-documents";
    public string Region { get; set; } = "us-east-1";
    public bool ForcePathStyle { get; set; } = true;     // required by MinIO and most S3-compatible stores
}

/// <summary>Works with MinIO locally and AWS S3 / Cloudflare R2 / Backblaze B2 in the cloud (config only).</summary>
internal sealed class S3ObjectStorage : IObjectStorage, IDisposable
{
    private readonly StorageOptions _options;
    private readonly AmazonS3Client _client;

    public S3ObjectStorage(IOptions<StorageOptions> options)
    {
        _options = options.Value;
        var config = new AmazonS3Config
        {
            ServiceURL = _options.ServiceUrl,
            ForcePathStyle = _options.ForcePathStyle,
            AuthenticationRegion = _options.Region,
            // Newer AWS SDKs add checksums by default, which some S3-compatible stores reject.
            // If your SDK version doesn't have these two properties, simply remove them.
            RequestChecksumCalculation = RequestChecksumCalculation.WHEN_REQUIRED,
            ResponseChecksumValidation = ResponseChecksumValidation.WHEN_REQUIRED,
        };
        _client = new AmazonS3Client(new BasicAWSCredentials(_options.AccessKey, _options.SecretKey), config);
    }

    public async Task PutAsync(string key, Stream content, string contentType, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var request = new PutObjectRequest
        {
            BucketName = _options.Bucket,
            Key = key,
            InputStream = content,
            ContentType = contentType,
            AutoCloseStream = false,
        };
        await _client.PutObjectAsync(request, cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task<Stream> OpenReadAsync(string key, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        try
        {
            var response = await _client.GetObjectAsync(_options.Bucket, key, cancellationToken);
            return response.ResponseStream;
        }
        catch (AmazonS3Exception ex) when (ex.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.NotFound)
        {
            throw new NotFoundException("Stored object", key);
        }
    }

    public Task DeleteAsync(string key, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) =>
        _client.DeleteObjectAsync(_options.Bucket, key, cancellationToken);

    public async Task EnsureBucketAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        if (!await AmazonS3Util.DoesS3BucketExistV2Async(_client, _options.Bucket))
            await _client.PutBucketAsync(_options.Bucket, cancellationToken);
    }

    public Task<bool> BucketExistsAsync() => AmazonS3Util.DoesS3BucketExistV2Async(_client, _options.Bucket);

    public void Dispose() => _client.Dispose();
}

internal sealed class StorageHealthCheck(S3ObjectStorage storage) : IHealthCheck
{
    public async Task<HealthCheckResult> CheckHealthAsync(HealthCheckContext context, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        try
        {
            return await storage.BucketExistsAsync()
                ? HealthCheckResult.Healthy("Bucket reachable")
                : HealthCheckResult.Degraded("Bucket does not exist yet");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            return HealthCheckResult.Unhealthy("Object storage unreachable", ex);
        }
    }
}

using Microsoft.Extensions.Diagnostics.HealthChecks;

namespace NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Persistence;

internal sealed class DatabaseHealthCheck(NexusRagDbContext db) : IHealthCheck
{
    public async Task<HealthCheckResult> CheckHealthAsync(HealthCheckContext context, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) =>
        await db.Database.CanConnectAsync(cancellationToken)

            : HealthCheckResult.Unhealthy("PostgreSQL unreachable");

backend/NexusRAG.Infrastructure/DependencyInjection.cs (replace)
csharp
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection.Extensions;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Auth;
using NexusRAG.Application.Documents;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.AiService;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Identity;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Storage;

namespace NexusRAG.Infrastructure;

public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddInfrastructure(this IServiceCollection services, IConfiguration configuration)
    {
        services.TryAddSingleton(TimeProvider.System);

        // ---- Options (validated at startup) ----
        AddValidatedOptions<AiServiceOptions>(services, configuration, AiServiceOptions.SectionName);
        AddValidatedOptions<JwtOptions>(services, configuration, JwtOptions.SectionName);
        AddValidatedOptions<StorageOptions>(services, configuration, StorageOptions.SectionName);
        AddValidatedOptions<DocumentUploadOptions>(services, configuration, DocumentUploadOptions.SectionName);
        services.AddOptions<AuthOptions>().Bind(configuration.GetSection(AuthOptions.SectionName));

        // ---- PostgreSQL (connection string resolved lazily so tests can override it) ----
        services.AddDbContext<NexusRagDbContext>((sp, options) =>
        {
            var connectionString = sp.GetRequiredService<IConfiguration>().GetConnectionString("Postgres");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(connectionString))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("ConnectionStrings:Postgres is not configured.");
            options.UseNpgsql(connectionString, npgsql => npgsql.EnableRetryOnFailure(3));
        });
        services.AddScoped<IApplicationDbContext>(sp => sp.GetRequiredService<NexusRagDbContext>());

        // ---- Identity ----
        services.AddSingleton<ITokenService, JwtTokenService>();
        services.AddSingleton<IPasswordService, PasswordService>();

        // ---- Object storage ----
        services.AddSingleton<S3ObjectStorage>();
        services.AddSingleton<IObjectStorage>(sp => sp.GetRequiredService<S3ObjectStorage>());

        // ---- Python AI service client ----
        services.AddHttpClient<IAiServiceClient, AiServiceClient>((sp, client) =>
            {
                var options = sp.GetRequiredService<IOptions<AiServiceOptions>>().Value;
                client.BaseAddress = new Uri(options.BaseUrl.TrimEnd('/') + "/");
                client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Add("X-Service-Token", options.ServiceToken);
            })
            .AddStandardResilienceHandler();

        // ---- Health checks ----
        services.AddHealthChecks()
            .AddCheck<DatabaseHealthCheck>("postgres", tags: ["ready"])
            .AddCheck<StorageHealthCheck>("object-storage", tags: ["ready"])
            .AddCheck<AiServiceHealthCheck>("ai-service", tags: ["ready"]);

        return services;
    }

    private static void AddValidatedOptions<T>(IServiceCollection services, IConfiguration configuration, string section)
        where T : class =>
        services.AddOptions<T>().Bind(configuration.GetSection(section)).ValidateDataAnnotations().ValidateOnStart();
}
Part D — API (Phases 3–5)
backend/NexusRAG.API/Security/HttpCurrentUser.cs
csharp
using System.Security.Claims;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;
using NexusRAG.Application.Security;

namespace NexusRAG.API.Security;

/// <summary>Reads the authenticated user from the JWT. Also acts as the tenant context for EF filters.</summary>
internal sealed class HttpCurrentUser(IHttpContextAccessor accessor) : ICurrentUser, ITenantContext
{
    private ClaimsPrincipal? Principal => accessor.HttpContext?.User;

    public bool IsAuthenticated => Principal?.Identity?.IsAuthenticated == true;
    public Guid UserId => RequireGuid(NexusClaimTypes.Subject);
    public Guid TenantId => RequireGuid(NexusClaimTypes.TenantId);

    public IReadOnlyCollection<string> Roles =>
        Principal?.FindAll(NexusClaimTypes.Role).Select(c => c.Value).ToArray() ?? [];

    public IReadOnlyCollection<string> AccessGroups =>
        Principal?.FindAll(NexusClaimTypes.Group).Select(c => c.Value).ToArray() ?? [];

    public bool IsInRole(string role) => Roles.Contains(role);

    Guid? ITenantContext.TenantId =>
        IsAuthenticated && Guid.TryParse(Principal!.FindFirstValue(NexusClaimTypes.TenantId), out var tenantId)
            ? tenantId
            : null;

    bool ITenantContext.BypassTenantFilter => false;

    private Guid RequireGuid(string claimType) =>
        Guid.TryParse(Principal?.FindFirstValue(claimType), out var value)
            ? value
            : throw new UnauthorizedException("Authentication required.");
}
backend/NexusRAG.API/Security/Policies.cs
csharp
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Identity;

namespace NexusRAG.API.Security;

public static class Policies
{
    public const string TenantAdmin = "TenantAdmin";
    public const string ContentEditor = "ContentEditor";
    public const string Reader = "Reader";

    public static void AddNexusPolicies(this AuthorizationOptions options)
    {
        options.AddPolicy(TenantAdmin, p => p.RequireRole(RoleNames.TenantAdmin, RoleNames.PlatformAdmin));
        options.AddPolicy(ContentEditor, p => p.RequireRole(RoleNames.TenantAdmin, RoleNames.Editor, RoleNames.PlatformAdmin));
        options.AddPolicy(Reader, p => p.RequireRole(RoleNames.All.ToArray()));

        // Secure by default: every endpoint requires authentication unless marked [AllowAnonymous].
        options.FallbackPolicy = new AuthorizationPolicyBuilder().RequireAuthenticatedUser().Build();
    }
}
backend/NexusRAG.API/Errors/GlobalExceptionHandler.cs
csharp
using FluentValidation;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Diagnostics;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Common;

namespace NexusRAG.API.Errors;

/// <summary>Maps exceptions to RFC 7807 ProblemDetails. Never leaks internals outside Development.</summary>
internal sealed class GlobalExceptionHandler(
    IProblemDetailsService problemDetailsService,
    IHostEnvironment environment,
    ILogger<GlobalExceptionHandler> logger) : IExceptionHandler
{
    public async ValueTask<bool> TryHandleAsync(HttpContext httpContext, Exception exception, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var (status, title) = exception switch
        {
            ValidationException => (StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest, "Validation failed"),
            BadHttpRequestException bad => (bad.StatusCode, "Bad request"),
            UnauthorizedException => (StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized, "Unauthorized"),
            ForbiddenException => (StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden, "Forbidden"),
            NotFoundException => (StatusCodes.Status404NotFound, "Not found"),
            ConflictException => (StatusCodes.Status409Conflict, "Conflict"),
            DomainException => (StatusCodes.Status422UnprocessableEntity, "Business rule violated"),
            _ => (StatusCodes.Status500InternalServerError, "Unexpected error"),
        };

        if (status >= 500) logger.LogError(exception, "Unhandled exception");
        else logger.LogInformation("Request failed with {StatusCode}: {Message}", status, exception.Message);

        var problem = new ProblemDetails
        {
            Status = status,
            Title = title,
            Detail = status >= 500 && !environment.IsDevelopment() ? "An unexpected error occurred." : exception.Message,
        };
        problem.Extensions["traceId"] = httpContext.TraceIdentifier;

        if (exception is ValidationException validation && validation.Errors.Any())
        {
            problem.Extensions["errors"] = validation.Errors
                .GroupBy(e => e.PropertyName)
                .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.Select(e => e.ErrorMessage).ToArray());
        }

        httpContext.Response.StatusCode = status;
        return await problemDetailsService.TryWriteAsync(new ProblemDetailsContext
        {
            HttpContext = httpContext,
            ProblemDetails = problem,
            Exception = exception,
        });
    }
}

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.RateLimiting;
using NexusRAG.API.Security;
using NexusRAG.Application.Auth;
using NexusRAG.Application.Users;

namespace NexusRAG.API.Controllers;

[ApiController]
[Route("api/auth")]
[EnableRateLimiting("auth")]
public sealed class AuthController(IAuthService auth, IUserService users) : ControllerBase
{
    [HttpPost("register-tenant"), AllowAnonymous]
    public async Task<ActionResult<AuthResponse>> RegisterTenant(RegisterTenantRequest request, CancellationToken ct) =>
        Ok(await auth.RegisterTenantAsync(request, ct));

    [HttpPost("login"), AllowAnonymous]
    public async Task<ActionResult<AuthResponse>> Login(LoginRequest request, CancellationToken ct) =>
        Ok(await auth.LoginAsync(request, ct));

    [HttpPost("refresh"), AllowAnonymous]
    public async Task<ActionResult<AuthResponse>> Refresh(RefreshRequest request, CancellationToken ct) =>
        Ok(await auth.RefreshAsync(request, ct));

    [HttpPost("logout"), AllowAnonymous]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Logout(RefreshRequest request, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        await auth.LogoutAsync(request, ct);
        return NoContent();
    }

    [HttpGet("me"), Authorize(Policy = Policies.Reader)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<UserSummary>> Me(CancellationToken ct) => Ok(await users.GetMeAsync(ct));
}
