backend/NexusRAG.API/Controllers/DocumentsController.cs
csharp
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.RateLimiting;
using NexusRAG.API.Contracts;
using NexusRAG.API.Security;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;
using NexusRAG.Application.Documents;

namespace NexusRAG.API.Controllers;

[ApiController]
[Route("api/documents")]
[Authorize(Policy = Policies.Reader)]
[EnableRateLimiting("api")]
public sealed class DocumentsController(IDocumentService documents) : ControllerBase
{
    private const long MaxSingleRequestBytes = 30L * 1024 * 1024;     // 25 MB file + multipart overhead
    private const long MaxBulkRequestBytes = 300L * 1024 * 1024;

    [HttpPost("upload"), Authorize(Policy = Policies.ContentEditor)]
    [RequestSizeLimit(MaxSingleRequestBytes)]
    [RequestFormLimits(MultipartBodyLengthLimit = MaxSingleRequestBytes)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<UploadResult>> Upload([FromForm] UploadDocumentForm form, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var result = await documents.UploadAsync(
            new UploadDocumentCommand(form.KnowledgeBaseId, form.DocumentId, ToUploadFile(form.File), SplitGroups(form.AccessGroups)),
            ct);
        return AcceptedAtAction(nameof(GetStatus), new { id = result.DocumentId }, result);
    }

    [HttpPost("bulk-upload"), Authorize(Policy = Policies.ContentEditor)]
    [RequestSizeLimit(MaxBulkRequestBytes)]
    [RequestFormLimits(MultipartBodyLengthLimit = MaxBulkRequestBytes)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<IReadOnlyList<BulkUploadItemResult>>> BulkUpload([FromForm] BulkUploadForm form,
        CancellationToken ct) =>
        Ok(await documents.BulkUploadAsync(form.KnowledgeBaseId, form.Files.Select(ToUploadFile).ToList(),
            SplitGroups(form.AccessGroups), ct));

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResult<DocumentDto>>> List([FromQuery] Guid knowledgeBaseId,
        [FromQuery] int page = 1, [FromQuery] int pageSize = 25, CancellationToken ct = default) =>
        Ok(await documents.ListAsync(knowledgeBaseId, page, pageSize, ct));

    [HttpGet("{id:guid}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<DocumentDto>> Get(Guid id, CancellationToken ct) => Ok(await documents.GetAsync(id, ct));

    [HttpGet("{id:guid}/status")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<DocumentStatusDto>> GetStatus(Guid id, CancellationToken ct) =>
        Ok(await documents.GetStatusAsync(id, ct));

    [HttpGet("{id:guid}/versions")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<IReadOnlyList<DocumentVersionDto>>> GetVersions(Guid id, CancellationToken ct) =>
        Ok(await documents.GetVersionsAsync(id, ct));

    [HttpGet("{id:guid}/content")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetContent(Guid id, [FromQuery] int? version, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var content = await documents.OpenContentAsync(id, version, ct);
        return File(content.Stream, content.ContentType, content.FileName);
    }

    [HttpDelete("{id:guid}"), Authorize(Policy = Policies.ContentEditor)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(Guid id, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        await documents.DeleteAsync(id, ct);
        return NoContent();
    }

    [HttpPost("{id:guid}/reindex"), Authorize(Policy = Policies.ContentEditor)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<DocumentStatusDto>> Reindex(Guid id, CancellationToken ct) =>
        Accepted(await documents.ReindexAsync(id, ct));

    private static UploadFile ToUploadFile(IFormFile file) => new(file.FileName, file.Length, file.OpenReadStream);

    private static string[] SplitGroups(string? groups) =>
        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(groups) ? [] : groups.Split(',', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries | StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries);
}
backend/NexusRAG.API/Program.cs (replace)
csharp
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
using System.Threading.RateLimiting;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.JwtBearer;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Diagnostics.HealthChecks;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
using NexusRAG.API.Errors;
using NexusRAG.API.Security;
using NexusRAG.Application;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Security;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Identity;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using Serilog;
using Serilog.Formatting.Json;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

builder.Host.UseSerilog((context, services, logger) => logger
    .ReadFrom.Configuration(context.Configuration)
    .ReadFrom.Services(services)
    .Enrich.FromLogContext()
    .Enrich.WithProperty("service", "nexusrag-api")
    .WriteTo.Console(new JsonFormatter()));

// ---------- MVC, OpenAPI, errors ----------
builder.Services.AddControllers().AddJsonOptions(o =>
    o.JsonSerializerOptions.Converters.Add(new JsonStringEnumConverter(JsonNamingPolicy.SnakeCaseUpper)));
builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer();
builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen();
builder.Services.AddProblemDetails();
builder.Services.AddExceptionHandler<GlobalExceptionHandler>();

// ---------- Current user / tenant ----------
builder.Services.AddHttpContextAccessor();
builder.Services.AddScoped<HttpCurrentUser>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<ICurrentUser>(sp => sp.GetRequiredService<HttpCurrentUser>());
builder.Services.AddScoped<ITenantContext>(sp => sp.GetRequiredService<HttpCurrentUser>());

// ---------- Layers ----------
builder.Services.AddApplication();
builder.Services.AddInfrastructure(builder.Configuration);

// ---------- Authentication (JWT) ----------
builder.Services.AddAuthentication(JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme).AddJwtBearer();
builder.Services.AddOptions<JwtBearerOptions>(JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme)
    .Configure<IOptions<JwtOptions>>((options, jwtOptions) =>
    {
        var jwt = jwtOptions.Value;
        options.MapInboundClaims = false;   // keep "sub", "role", "tid" as-is
        options.TokenValidationParameters = new TokenValidationParameters
        {
            ValidateIssuer = true,
            ValidIssuer = jwt.Issuer,
            ValidateAudience = true,
            ValidAudience = jwt.Audience,
            ValidateIssuerSigningKey = true,
            IssuerSigningKey = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(jwt.SigningKey)),
            ValidateLifetime = true,
            ClockSkew = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30),
            NameClaimType = NexusClaimTypes.Subject,
            RoleClaimType = NexusClaimTypes.Role,
        };
    });
builder.Services.AddAuthorization(options => options.AddNexusPolicies());

// ---------- Rate limiting ----------
builder.Services.AddRateLimiter(options =>
{
    options.RejectionStatusCode = StatusCodes.Status429TooManyRequests;
    options.AddPolicy("auth", ctx => RateLimitPartition.GetFixedWindowLimiter(
        ctx.Connection.RemoteIpAddress?.ToString() ?? "unknown",
        _ => new FixedWindowRateLimiterOptions { PermitLimit = 10, Window = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1) }));
    options.AddPolicy("api", ctx => RateLimitPartition.GetTokenBucketLimiter(
        ctx.User.FindFirst(NexusClaimTypes.Subject)?.Value ?? ctx.Connection.RemoteIpAddress?.ToString() ?? "anonymous",
        _ => new TokenBucketRateLimiterOptions
        {
            TokenLimit = 60,
            TokensPerPeriod = 60,
            ReplenishmentPeriod = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1),
            QueueLimit = 0,
        }));
});

var app = builder.Build();

await app.Services.InitializeInfrastructureAsync();

app.UseSerilogRequestLogging();
app.UseExceptionHandler();
app.UseStatusCodePages();

if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.UseSwagger();       // before auth so Swagger UI stays reachable
    app.UseSwaggerUI();
}

app.UseAuthentication();
app.UseRateLimiter();
app.UseAuthorization();

app.MapControllers();
app.MapHealthChecks("/health/live", new HealthCheckOptions { Predicate = _ => false }).AllowAnonymous();
app.MapHealthChecks("/health/ready", new HealthCheckOptions { Predicate = c => c.Tags.Contains("ready") }).AllowAnonymous();

app.Run();

public partial class Program { }

backend/NexusRAG.Tests/Security/TenantIsolationTests.cs
csharp
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Domain.KnowledgeBases;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Tenancy;

namespace NexusRAG.Tests.Security;

public class TenantIsolationTests
{
    private sealed class FixedTenant(Guid? tenantId) : ITenantContext
    {
        public Guid? TenantId => tenantId;
        public bool BypassTenantFilter => false;
    }

    private static NexusRagDbContext CreateContext(string database, ITenantContext tenant) =>
        new(new DbContextOptionsBuilder<NexusRagDbContext>().UseInMemoryDatabase(database).Options, tenant, TimeProvider.System);

    private static async Task<(string Db, Guid TenantA, Guid TenantB)> SeedAsync()
    {
        var db = Guid.NewGuid().ToString();
        var tenantA = Guid.NewGuid();
        var tenantB = Guid.NewGuid();
        await using var seed = CreateContext(db, new SystemTenantContext());
        seed.KnowledgeBases.AddRange(
            new KnowledgeBase { TenantId = tenantA, Name = "A-HR" },
            new KnowledgeBase { TenantId = tenantB, Name = "B-HR" },
            new KnowledgeBase { TenantId = tenantA, Name = "A-Deleted", IsDeleted = true });
        await seed.SaveChangesAsync();
        return (db, tenantA, tenantB);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task QueryFilter_ReturnsOnlyCurrentTenantRows_AndHidesSoftDeleted()
    {
        var (db, tenantA, _) = await SeedAsync();
        await using var context = CreateContext(db, new FixedTenant(tenantA));

        var names = await context.KnowledgeBases.Select(k => k.Name).ToListAsync();

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "A-HR" }, names);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task AnonymousContext_SeesNoTenantRows()
    {
        var (db, _, _) = await SeedAsync();
        await using var context = CreateContext(db, new FixedTenant(null));
        Assert.Empty(await context.KnowledgeBases.ToListAsync());
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task CrossTenantWrite_IsBlocked()
    {
        var (db, tenantA, tenantB) = await SeedAsync();
        await using var context = CreateContext(db, new FixedTenant(tenantA));

        context.KnowledgeBases.Add(new KnowledgeBase { TenantId = tenantB, Name = "Injected" });

        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<InvalidOperationException>(() => context.SaveChangesAsync());
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task NewEntity_WithoutTenant_GetsCurrentTenantAutomatically()
    {
        var (db, tenantA, _) = await SeedAsync();
        await using var context = CreateContext(db, new FixedTenant(tenantA));

        var kb = new KnowledgeBase { Name = "Auto" };
        context.KnowledgeBases.Add(kb);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        Assert.Equal(tenantA, kb.TenantId);
    }
}
backend/NexusRAG.Tests/Security/AccessScopeTests.cs
csharp
using NexusRAG.Application.Security;
using NexusRAG.Domain.KnowledgeBases;

namespace NexusRAG.Tests.Security;

public class AccessScopeTests
{
    private static readonly Guid Tenant = Guid.NewGuid();

    private static IQueryable<KnowledgeBase> KnowledgeBases() => new List<KnowledgeBase>
    {
        new() { TenantId = Tenant, Name = "Public", AllowedGroups = [] },
        new() { TenantId = Tenant, Name = "HR", AllowedGroups = ["hr"] },
        new() { TenantId = Tenant, Name = "Finance", AllowedGroups = ["finance"] },
        new() { TenantId = Guid.NewGuid(), Name = "OtherTenant", AllowedGroups = [] },
    }.AsQueryable();

    [Fact]
    public void Viewer_SeesPublicAndOwnGroupKnowledgeBases_Only()
    {
        var scope = new AccessScope(Tenant, Guid.NewGuid(), IsTenantAdmin: false, Groups: ["hr"]);
        var names = KnowledgeBases().WhereAccessible(scope).Select(k => k.Name).OrderBy(n => n).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "HR", "Public" }, names);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TenantAdmin_SeesAllKnowledgeBasesInOwnTenant_Only()
    {
        var scope = new AccessScope(Tenant, Guid.NewGuid(), IsTenantAdmin: true, Groups: []);
        Assert.Equal(3, KnowledgeBases().WhereAccessible(scope).Count());
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(" HR ", "hr")]
    [InlineData("Finance-Team", "finance-team")]
    public void AccessGroups_AreNormalized(string input, string expected) =>
        Assert.Equal(new[] { expected }, AccessGroupRules.Normalize([input]));

    [Fact]
    public void InvalidAccessGroup_IsRejected() =>
        Assert.Throws<FluentValidation.ValidationException>(() => AccessGroupRules.NormalizeAndValidate(["bad group!"]));
}

using System.Text;
using NexusRAG.Application.Documents;

namespace NexusRAG.Tests.Documents;

public class FileSignatureValidatorTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void Pdf_WithPdfMagic_IsValid() =>
        Assert.True(FileSignatureValidator.IsValid(".pdf", Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes("%PDF-1.7 ...")));

    [Fact]
    public void Pdf_WithExecutableContent_IsRejected() =>
        Assert.False(FileSignatureValidator.IsValid(".pdf", "MZ\u0090\0"u8.ToArray()));

    [Fact]
    public void Docx_WithZipMagic_IsValid() =>
        Assert.True(FileSignatureValidator.IsValid(".docx", [0x50, 0x4B, 0x03, 0x04, 0x14, 0x00]));

    [Fact]
    public void Text_WithNullBytes_IsRejected() =>
        Assert.False(FileSignatureValidator.IsValid(".txt", [0x48, 0x00, 0x49]));

    [Fact]
    public void Csv_PlainText_IsValid() =>
        Assert.True(FileSignatureValidator.IsValid(".csv", Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes("country,annual_leave\nIndia,18")));

    [Fact]
    public void UnknownExtension_IsRejected() =>
        Assert.False(FileSignatureValidator.IsValid(".exe", "MZ"u8.ToArray()));
}
backend/NexusRAG.Tests/Identity/JwtTokenServiceTests.cs
csharp
using System.Text;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.JsonWebTokens;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
using NexusRAG.Application.Security;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Identity;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Identity;

namespace NexusRAG.Tests.Identity;

public class JwtTokenServiceTests
{
    private static readonly string Key = new('k', 48);
    private readonly JwtTokenService _service = new(Options.Create(new JwtOptions { SigningKey = Key }), TimeProvider.System);

    [Fact]
    public async Task AccessToken_IsValid_AndCarriesTenantRolesAndGroups()
    {
        var tenantId = Guid.NewGuid();
        var user = new User { TenantId = tenantId, Email = "a@acme.test", DisplayName = "A", AccessGroups = ["hr-india"] };

        var token = _service.CreateAccessToken(user, [RoleNames.Editor]);

        var result = await new JsonWebTokenHandler().ValidateTokenAsync(token.Token, new TokenValidationParameters
        {
            ValidIssuer = "nexusrag",
            ValidAudience = "nexusrag-clients",
            IssuerSigningKey = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(Key)),
        });

        Assert.True(result.IsValid);
        Assert.True(result.ClaimsIdentity.HasClaim(NexusClaimTypes.TenantId, tenantId.ToString()));
        Assert.True(result.ClaimsIdentity.HasClaim(NexusClaimTypes.Role, RoleNames.Editor));
        Assert.True(result.ClaimsIdentity.HasClaim(NexusClaimTypes.Group, "hr-india"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RefreshTokens_AreRandom_AndHashDeterministically()
    {
        var a = _service.GenerateRefreshToken();
        var b = _service.GenerateRefreshToken();

        Assert.NotEqual(a, b);
        Assert.Equal(_service.HashRefreshToken(a), _service.HashRefreshToken(a));
        Assert.NotEqual(a, _service.HashRefreshToken(a));
    }
}
backend/NexusRAG.Tests/Domain/IngestionJobTests.cs
csharp
using NexusRAG.Domain.Common;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Ingestion;

namespace NexusRAG.Tests.Domain;

public class IngestionJobTests
{
    private static readonly DateTimeOffset Now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;

    [Fact]
    public void HappyPath_ReachesIndexed_With100Percent()
    {
        var job = new IngestionJob();
        foreach (var s in new[] { IngestionStatus.Queued, IngestionStatus.Parsing, IngestionStatus.Chunking,
                     IngestionStatus.Embedding, IngestionStatus.Indexing, IngestionStatus.Indexed })
            job.TransitionTo(s, Now);

        Assert.Equal(IngestionStatus.Indexed, job.Status);
        Assert.Equal(100, job.ProgressPercent);
        Assert.NotNull(job.StartedAt);
        Assert.NotNull(job.CompletedAt);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void IllegalTransition_Throws()
    {
        var job = new IngestionJob();
        Assert.Throws<DomainException>(() => job.TransitionTo(IngestionStatus.Indexed, Now));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void LongErrors_AreTruncated()
    {
        var job = new IngestionJob();
        job.TransitionTo(IngestionStatus.Failed, Now, error: new string('e', 5000));
        Assert.Equal(2000, job.LastError!.Length);
    }
}

ai-services/app/core/config.py (replace)
python
"""Typed configuration loaded from environment variables / .env files."""

from functools import lru_cache
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import Field, SecretStr, field_validator
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict


def _is_set(secret: SecretStr | None) -> bool:
    return bool(secret and secret.get_secret_value().strip())


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=("../.env", ".env"), env_file_encoding="utf-8", extra="ignore")

    app_name: str = "nexusrag-ai-service"
    environment: str = "development"
    log_level: str = "INFO"

    # Service-to-service authentication (.NET -> Python)
    ai_service_token: SecretStr

    # ---- Infrastructure ----
    redis_host: str = "localhost"
    redis_port: int = 6379
    redis_password: SecretStr | None = None

    qdrant_url: str = "http://localhost:6333"
    qdrant_api_key: SecretStr | None = None

    rabbitmq_host: str = "localhost"
    rabbitmq_port: int = 5672
    rabbitmq_default_user: str = "nexusrag"
    rabbitmq_default_pass: SecretStr | None = None

    s3_endpoint: str = "http://localhost:9000"
    s3_access_key: str = ""
    s3_secret_key: SecretStr | None = None
    s3_bucket: str = "nexusrag-documents"
    s3_region: str = "us-east-1"

    dependency_check_timeout_seconds: float = Field(default=3.0, gt=0)

    # ---- Chat providers (free tiers only) ----
    provider_order: str = "groq,openrouter"

    groq_api_key: SecretStr | None = None
    groq_base_url: str = "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1"
    groq_fast_model: str = ""
    groq_strong_model: str = ""

    openrouter_api_key: SecretStr | None = None
    openrouter_base_url: str = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1"
    openrouter_fast_model: str = ""
    openrouter_strong_model: str = ""

    # ---- Embeddings (model + dimensions are part of the index identity) ----
    embedding_provider: str = "gemini"
    gemini_api_key: SecretStr | None = None
    embedding_model: str = "gemini-embedding-001"
    embedding_dimensions: int = Field(default=768, gt=0)

    # ---- Reranking ----
    rerank_provider: Literal["none", "jina"] = "none"
    jina_api_key: SecretStr | None = None

    @field_validator("ai_service_token")
    @classmethod
    def _validate_token(cls, value: SecretStr) -> SecretStr:
        if len(value.get_secret_value()) < 32:
            raise ValueError("AI_SERVICE_TOKEN must be at least 32 characters")
        return value

    @property
    def provider_order_list(self) -> list[str]:
        return [p.strip().lower() for p in self.provider_order.split(",") if p.strip()]

    def configured_chat_providers(self) -> list[str]:
        keys = {"groq": self.groq_api_key, "openrouter": self.openrouter_api_key}
        return [name for name in self.provider_order_list if _is_set(keys.get(name))]

    def provider_summary(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        """Safe-to-expose configuration summary. Never includes secrets."""
        return {
            "provider_order": self.provider_order_list,
            "chat_providers": {
                "groq": {
                    "configured": _is_set(self.groq_api_key),
                    "fast_model": self.groq_fast_model or None,
                    "strong_model": self.groq_strong_model or None,
                },
                "openrouter": {
                    "configured": _is_set(self.openrouter_api_key),
                    "fast_model": self.openrouter_fast_model or None,
                    "strong_model": self.openrouter_strong_model or None,
                },
            },
            "embedding": {
                "provider": self.embedding_provider,
                "model": self.embedding_model,
                "dimensions": self.embedding_dimensions,
                "configured": _is_set(self.gemini_api_key),
            },
            "rerank": {
                "provider": self.rerank_provider,
                "configured": self.rerank_provider == "none" or _is_set(self.jina_api_key),
            },
        }


@lru_cache
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    return Settings()
ai-services/app/core/infra.py (new)
python
"""Shared infrastructure clients (Redis, Qdrant, RabbitMQ, S3) and dependency health checks."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
from typing import Protocol
from urllib.parse import quote

import aio_pika
import boto3
from botocore.config import Config as BotoConfig
from qdrant_client import AsyncQdrantClient
from redis.asyncio import Redis

from app.core.config import Settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class DependencyChecker(Protocol):
    async def check_all(self) -> dict[str, bool]: ...
    async def close(self) -> None: ...


class Infrastructure:
    """Created once per process in the FastAPI lifespan. Clients connect lazily."""

    def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
        self._settings = settings
        self._timeout = settings.dependency_check_timeout_seconds

        self.redis = Redis(
            host=settings.redis_host,
            port=settings.redis_port,
            password=settings.redis_password.get_secret_value() if settings.redis_password else None,
            socket_connect_timeout=self._timeout,
        )
        self.qdrant = AsyncQdrantClient(
            url=settings.qdrant_url,
            api_key=settings.qdrant_api_key.get_secret_value() if settings.qdrant_api_key else None,
            timeout=int(max(self._timeout, 1)),
        )
        self.s3 = boto3.client(
            "s3",
            endpoint_url=settings.s3_endpoint,
            aws_access_key_id=settings.s3_access_key,
            aws_secret_access_key=settings.s3_secret_key.get_secret_value() if settings.s3_secret_key else None,
            region_name=settings.s3_region,
            config=BotoConfig(
                signature_version="s3v4",
                s3={"addressing_style": "path"},
                connect_timeout=self._timeout,
                read_timeout=30,
                retries={"max_attempts": 3, "mode": "standard"},
            ),
        )

    @property
    def amqp_url(self) -> str:
        s = self._settings
        password = s.rabbitmq_default_pass.get_secret_value() if s.rabbitmq_default_pass else ""
        return f"amqp://{quote(s.rabbitmq_default_user)}:{quote(password)}@{s.rabbitmq_host}:{s.rabbitmq_port}/"

    async def _check_redis(self) -> bool:
        return bool(await self.redis.ping())

    async def _check_qdrant(self) -> bool:
        await self.qdrant.get_collections()
        return True

    async def _check_rabbitmq(self) -> bool:
        connection = await aio_pika.connect(self.amqp_url, timeout=self._timeout)
        await connection.close()
        return True

    async def _check_s3(self) -> bool:
        await asyncio.to_thread(self.s3.head_bucket, Bucket=self._settings.s3_bucket)
        return True

    async def _safe(self, name: str, check: Callable[[], Awaitable[bool]]) -> tuple[str, bool]:
        try:
            return name, await asyncio.wait_for(check(), timeout=self._timeout + 1)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - health checks must never raise
            logger.warning("Dependency check failed: %s (%s)", name, type(exc).__name__)
            return name, False

    async def check_all(self) -> dict[str, bool]:
        checks = {
            "redis": self._check_redis,
            "qdrant": self._check_qdrant,
            "rabbitmq": self._check_rabbitmq,
            "object_storage": self._check_s3,
        }
        results = await asyncio.gather(*(self._safe(name, fn) for name, fn in checks.items()))
        return dict(results)

    async def close(self) -> None:
        await self.redis.aclose()
        await self.qdrant.close()
ai-services/app/api/health.py (replace)
python
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request, Response, status

router = APIRouter(tags=["health"])


@router.get("/health/live")
async def live() -> dict[str, str]:
    return {"status": "ok"}


@router.get("/health/ready")
async def ready(request: Request, response: Response) -> dict[str, object]:
    settings = request.app.state.settings
    dependencies = await request.app.state.dependencies.check_all()
    configured = settings.configured_chat_providers()

    if not all(dependencies.values()):
        response.status_code = status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE
        overall = "unavailable"
    elif not configured:
        overall = "degraded"      # infra fine, but no AI provider key configured
    else:
        overall = "ok"

    return {"status": overall, "dependencies": dependencies, "chat_providers_configured": configured}
ai-services/app/main.py (replace)
python
"""FastAPI application factory. Run with: uvicorn app.main:create_app --factory"""

import logging
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI

from app.api import health, internal
from app.core.config import Settings, get_settings
from app.core.infra import DependencyChecker, Infrastructure
from app.core.logging import configure_logging

logger = logging.getLogger("nexusrag.ai")


def create_app(settings: Settings | None = None, dependencies: DependencyChecker | None = None) -> FastAPI:
    settings = settings or get_settings()
    configure_logging(settings.log_level)
    deps: DependencyChecker = dependencies or Infrastructure(settings)

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(_: FastAPI):
        logger.info("AI service starting (env=%s, providers=%s)",
                    settings.environment, settings.configured_chat_providers())
        yield
        await deps.close()
        logger.info("AI service stopped")

    is_prod = settings.environment.lower() == "production"
    app = FastAPI(
        title="NexusRAG AI Service",
        version="0.2.0",
        lifespan=lifespan,
        docs_url=None if is_prod else "/docs",
        redoc_url=None,
    )
    app.state.settings = settings
    app.state.dependencies = deps
    app.include_router(health.router)
    app.include_router(internal.router)
    return app

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.core.config import Settings
from app.main import create_app

TEST_TOKEN = "t" * 40
FAKE_GROQ_KEY = "gsk_test_secret_value_never_exposed"


class FakeDependencies:
    def __init__(self, healthy: bool = True) -> None:
        self.healthy = healthy
        self.closed = False

    async def check_all(self) -> dict[str, bool]:
        return {"redis": self.healthy, "qdrant": True, "rabbitmq": True, "object_storage": True}

    async def close(self) -> None:
        self.closed = True


@pytest.fixture
def settings() -> Settings:
    return Settings(
        _env_file=None,
        ai_service_token=TEST_TOKEN,
        groq_api_key=FAKE_GROQ_KEY,
        provider_order="groq,openrouter",
    )


@pytest.fixture
def client(settings: Settings):
    with TestClient(create_app(settings, FakeDependencies())) as test_client:
        yield test_client

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.main import create_app
from tests.conftest import FAKE_GROQ_KEY, TEST_TOKEN, FakeDependencies


def test_live_returns_ok(client):
    response = client.get("/health/live")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json() == {"status": "ok"}


def test_ready_reports_dependencies_and_providers(client):
    body = client.get("/health/ready").json()
    assert body["status"] == "ok"
    assert body["chat_providers_configured"] == ["groq"]
    assert all(body["dependencies"].values())


def test_ready_returns_503_when_a_dependency_is_down(settings):
    with TestClient(create_app(settings, FakeDependencies(healthy=False))) as client:
        response = client.get("/health/ready")
    assert response.status_code == 503
    assert response.json()["dependencies"]["redis"] is False


def test_dependencies_closed_on_shutdown(settings):
    deps = FakeDependencies()
    with TestClient(create_app(settings, deps)):
        pass
    assert deps.closed is True


def test_internal_endpoint_rejects_missing_token(client):
    assert client.get("/internal/v1/providers").status_code == 401


def test_internal_endpoint_rejects_wrong_token(client):
    response = client.get("/internal/v1/providers", headers={"X-Service-Token": "w" * 40})
    assert response.status_code == 401


def test_internal_endpoint_accepts_valid_token_and_hides_secrets(client):
    response = client.get("/internal/v1/providers", headers={"X-Service-Token": TEST_TOKEN})
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()["chat_providers"]["groq"]["configured"] is True
    assert FAKE_GROQ_KEY not in response.text

Part H — Build, migrate, run
bash
# 1. Build and test .NET
cd backend
dotnet build
dotnet test

# 2. Create the initial migration (generates files in NexusRAG.Infrastructure/Persistence/Migrations)
dotnet ef migrations add InitialSchema --project NexusRAG.Infrastructure --startup-project NexusRAG.API --output-dir Persistence/Migrations

# 3. Local secrets for the API (values from your .env)
dotnet user-secrets set "ConnectionStrings:Postgres" "Host=localhost;Port=5432;Database=nexusrag;Username=nexusrag;Password=<POSTGRES_PASSWORD>" --project NexusRAG.API
dotnet user-secrets set "Jwt:SigningKey" "<JWT_SIGNING_KEY>" --project NexusRAG.API
dotnet user-secrets set "Storage:AccessKey" "<S3_ACCESS_KEY>" --project NexusRAG.API
dotnet user-secrets set "Storage:SecretKey" "<S3_SECRET_KEY>" --project NexusRAG.API
cd ..

# 4. Python
cd ai-services
pip install -r requirements.txt -r requirements-dev.txt
pytest -q
cd ..

# 5. Run
docker compose up -d
cd ai-services && uvicorn app.main:create_app --factory --reload --port 8000     # terminal 1
cd backend && dotnet run --project NexusRAG.API --launch-profile http            # terminal 2 (migrates DB, creates bucket)
Part I — End-to-end check (bash with jq; on Windows use Git Bash)
bash
API=http://localhost:5080

# Register a tenant (Development only)
TOKEN=$(curl -s -X POST $API/api/auth/register-tenant -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"tenantName":"Acme Corp","tenantSlug":"acme","adminEmail":"admin@acme.test","adminDisplayName":"Acme Admin","adminPassword":"ChangeMe!2026x"}' \
  | jq -r .accessToken)

# Create a knowledge base
KB=$(curl -s -X POST $API/api/knowledge-bases -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"name":"HR Policies","description":"Leave and benefits","allowedGroups":[]}' | jq -r .id)

# Upload a document (use any PDF or text file you have)
DOC=$(curl -s -X POST $API/api/documents/upload -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -F "file=@./leave-policy.pdf" -F "knowledgeBaseId=$KB" -F "accessGroups=hr-india" | jq -r .documentId)

curl -s $API/api/documents/$DOC/status -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" | jq   # status "UPLOADED"
curl -s "$API/api/documents?knowledgeBaseId=$KB" -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" | jq
curl -s $API/health/ready                                                         # Healthy (Postgres, storage, AI service)

# Uploading the same file again must return 409 Conflict (duplicate detection)
curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" -X POST $API/api/documents/upload -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -F "file=@./leave-policy.pdf" -F "knowledgeBaseId=$KB"

Documents stay in UPLOADED for now. Phase 6, in the next reply, adds the outbox publisher and the Python worker that move them through QUEUED → … → INDEXED.

Status, verification, and limitations

Verification: I couldn't compile or run any of this; my sandbox has no network access to restore packages. Most of the APIs used are long-standing and stable. A few, flagged below, depend on very recent package versions. If dotnet build or pytest reports an error, paste it here and I'll fix it before Phase 6.

Most likely friction points:

AWS SDK checksum settings. RequestChecksumCalculation and ResponseChecksumValidation require a recent AWSSDK version. If they don't compile, delete those two lines, as the code comment says.
Swagger has no "Authorize" button yet. I avoided version-specific OpenAPI code. Use curl or a .http file with a bearer token for now.

Known limitations in this batch:

Concurrent uploads to the same knowledge base can make ContentVersion increase by 1 instead of 2. This is harmless, because the counter is only used to detect change for caching.
Delete during indexing: if a document is deleted while it is being indexed, its vectors could survive briefly. Phase 6 handles this: an INDEXED event for a deleted document triggers a new delete.
Platform-admin seeding and the tenant administration screens come with Phase 14.

Reply "continue" for Phases 6–9. That batch makes documents actually flow end to end: RabbitMQ topology, the outbox publisher, the Python ingestion worker, parsers for all 7 file types, chunking, the Groq/OpenRouter/Gemini adapters with rate limiting and fallback, and Qdrant hybrid indexing.

continue

Phases 6–9: the ingestion pipeline

After this batch, an uploaded document moves through UPLOADED → QUEUED → PARSING → CHUNKING → EMBEDDING → INDEXING → INDEXED. Its chunks are searchable in Qdrant using dense vectors (Gemini) plus sparse keyword vectors.

How the pieces fit
text
API (upload) ──► OutboxMessages (Postgres, same transaction)
                      │  .NET Workers: OutboxPublisher (FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED)
                      ▼
            RabbitMQ  nexus.ingestion ──► ingestion.requests ──► Python ai-worker
                                               ▲   │                   │ download (S3) → verify SHA-256
               retry.10s / 60s / 300s ─────────┘   └──► .dlq           │ parse → chunk → embed (Gemini) → Qdrant
                                                                       │
            ingestion.status ◄──── status events (QUEUED…INDEXED + usage)
                 │  .NET Workers: IngestionStatusConsumer (single writer of job status)
                 ▼
            PostgreSQL (IngestionJobs, DocumentVersions, UsageRecords, ProviderUsages)

Key decisions in this batch:

Single writer for job status. Only the .NET status consumer updates ingestion status. Python emits QUEUED as its first event, which avoids race conditions between the publisher and the consumer.
Retries use three delay queues (10s, 60s, 300s), not per-message TTLs. Per-message TTLs in a single queue cause head-of-line blocking. After 4 attempts the job is marked FAILED and a copy goes to the dead-letter queue.
Idempotency. Each job holds a Redis lock and a "done" marker. Chunk IDs are deterministic, and after each upsert any point that doesn't carry the current ingestion_job_id is deleted. This one rule handles re-indexing, new versions, and changes in how a document is chunked.
Sparse vectors need no model. Keyword vectors are built by a deterministic hashing tokenizer, and Qdrant's IDF modifier supplies BM25-style weighting.
Parent chunks are stored with a sparse vector only. Only child chunks are embedded, so parents cost no embedding quota. Phase 10 uses parents for context expansion.
No OCR. A local OCR engine would mean running a local model, which your rules forbid. Scanned PDFs therefore fail with a clear message.
Quota protection. Redis counters per provider (requests/minute, tokens/minute, requests/day) stop calls before the provider has to reject them. Embedding batches are sized to fit Gemini's free-tier limit of 30k tokens per minute.

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Documents;

/// <summary>RabbitMQ message contracts shared with the Python AI service (camelCase JSON).</summary>
public static class MessageTypes
{
    public const string IngestRequested = "document.ingest.requested";
    public const string DeleteRequested = "document.delete.requested";
    public const string IngestionStatus = "document.ingestion.status";
}

public sealed record IngestionRequestedMessage(
    Guid MessageId,
    string MessageType,
    int SchemaVersion,
    Guid CorrelationId,
    Guid TenantId,
    Guid KnowledgeBaseId,
    Guid DocumentId,
    int DocumentVersion,
    Guid JobId,
    string ObjectKey,
    string FileName,
    string ContentType,
    string ContentHash,
    string[] AccessGroups,
    DateTimeOffset RequestedAt);

/// <summary>Deletes vectors of a document. OnlyVersionsBelow = delete only older versions.</summary>
public sealed record DocumentDeleteRequestedMessage(
    Guid MessageId,
    string MessageType,
    int SchemaVersion,
    Guid TenantId,
    Guid KnowledgeBaseId,
    Guid DocumentId,
    int? OnlyVersionsBelow,
    DateTimeOffset RequestedAt);

public sealed record ProviderUsageEntry(

    string Model,
    string Operation,
    int Requests,
    int Failures,
    int RateLimited,
    int InputTokens,
    int OutputTokens,

backend/NexusRAG.Application/Ingestion/IngestionStatusHandler.cs
csharp
using System.Text.Json;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Documents;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Ingestion;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Operations;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Ingestion;

public enum StatusHandlingResult { Applied, IgnoredUnknownJob, IgnoredInvalidStatus, IgnoredInvalidTransition }

public interface IIngestionStatusHandler
{
    Task<StatusHandlingResult> HandleAsync(IngestionStatusEvent statusEvent, CancellationToken ct);
}

/// <summary>
/// The single writer of ingestion status. Validates every event against the domain state machine,
/// so duplicate or out-of-order messages can never move a job backwards.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class IngestionStatusHandler(
    IApplicationDbContext db,
    TimeProvider time,
    ILogger<IngestionStatusHandler> logger) : IIngestionStatusHandler
{
    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new(JsonSerializerDefaults.Web);

    public async Task<StatusHandlingResult> HandleAsync(IngestionStatusEvent evt, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        if (!Enum.TryParse<IngestionStatus>(evt.Status, ignoreCase: true, out var status) || !Enum.IsDefined(status))
        {
            logger.LogWarning("Ignoring status event {MessageId} with unknown status {Status}", evt.MessageId, evt.Status);
            return StatusHandlingResult.IgnoredInvalidStatus;
        }

        var job = await db.IngestionJobs.Include(j => j.DocumentVersion)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(j => j.Id == evt.JobId && j.TenantId == evt.TenantId, ct);
        if (job is null)
        {
            logger.LogWarning("Ignoring status event for unknown job {JobId}", evt.JobId);
            return StatusHandlingResult.IgnoredUnknownJob;
        }

        var now = time.GetUtcNow();
        await RecordUsageAsync(evt, now, ct);

        // Vectors may have been written for a document that was deleted or superseded meanwhile.
        if (status == IngestionStatus.Indexed)
            await ScheduleVectorCleanupIfNeededAsync(evt, now, ct);

        if (job.Status != status && !IngestionStateMachine.CanTransition(job.Status, status))
        {
            logger.LogInformation("Ignoring out-of-order transition {From} -> {To} for job {JobId}", job.Status, status, job.Id);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
            return StatusHandlingResult.IgnoredInvalidTransition;
        }

        job.TransitionTo(status, now, evt.Progress, evt.Error);

        // Only the newest job of a version may change the version's visible status.
        var latestJobId = await db.IngestionJobs
            .Where(j => j.DocumentVersionId == job.DocumentVersionId)
            .OrderByDescending(j => j.CreatedAt).ThenByDescending(j => j.Id)
            .Select(j => j.Id)
            .FirstAsync(ct);

        if (latestJobId == job.Id)
        {
            job.DocumentVersion.Status = status;
            if (status == IngestionStatus.Indexed)
            {
                job.DocumentVersion.ChunkCount = evt.ChunkCount ?? job.DocumentVersion.ChunkCount;
                job.DocumentVersion.EmbeddingModel = evt.EmbeddingModel ?? job.DocumentVersion.EmbeddingModel;
            }
        }

        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        return StatusHandlingResult.Applied;
    }

    private async Task ScheduleVectorCleanupIfNeededAsync(IngestionStatusEvent evt, DateTimeOffset now, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var document = await db.Documents.IgnoreQueryFilters()
            .Where(d => d.Id == evt.DocumentId && d.TenantId == evt.TenantId)
            .Select(d => new { d.KnowledgeBaseId, d.IsDeleted, d.CurrentVersion })
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);
        if (document is null) return;

        int? onlyVersionsBelow;
        if (document.IsDeleted) onlyVersionsBelow = null;                                       // remove everything
        else if (evt.DocumentVersion < document.CurrentVersion) onlyVersionsBelow = document.CurrentVersion; // remove stale versions
        else return;

        db.OutboxMessages.Add(new OutboxMessage
        {
            Type = MessageTypes.DeleteRequested,
            Payload = JsonSerializer.Serialize(new DocumentDeleteRequestedMessage(
                Guid.CreateVersion7(), MessageTypes.DeleteRequested, 1, evt.TenantId,
                document.KnowledgeBaseId, evt.DocumentId, onlyVersionsBelow, now), JsonOptions),
        });
        logger.LogInformation("Scheduled vector cleanup for document {DocumentId} (onlyVersionsBelow={Below})",
            evt.DocumentId, onlyVersionsBelow);
    }

    private async Task RecordUsageAsync(IngestionStatusEvent evt, DateTimeOffset now, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        if (evt.Usage is not { Count: > 0 }) return;
        var day = DateOnly.FromDateTime(now.UtcDateTime);

        foreach (var u in evt.Usage)
        {
            var provider = Trim(u.Provider, 50);
            var model = Trim(u.Model, 200);

            db.UsageRecords.Add(new UsageRecord
            {
                TenantId = evt.TenantId,
                Operation = Trim(u.Operation, 50),
                Provider = provider,
                Model = model,
                InputTokens = u.InputTokens,
                OutputTokens = u.OutputTokens,
                EmbeddingTokens = u.EmbeddingTokens,
            });

            var aggregate = db.ProviderUsages.Local.FirstOrDefault(p => p.Provider == provider && p.Model == model && p.Day == day)
                ?? await db.ProviderUsages.FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.Provider == provider && p.Model == model && p.Day == day, ct);
            if (aggregate is null)
            {
                aggregate = new ProviderUsage { Provider = provider, Model = model, Day = day };
                db.ProviderUsages.Add(aggregate);
            }

            aggregate.Requests += u.Requests;
            aggregate.Failures += u.Failures;
            aggregate.RateLimitedCount += u.RateLimited;
            aggregate.InputTokens += u.InputTokens + u.EmbeddingTokens;
            aggregate.OutputTokens += u.OutputTokens;
        }
    }

    private static string Trim(string value, int max) => value.Length <= max ? value : value[..max];
}

backend/NexusRAG.Infrastructure/Messaging/RabbitMq.cs
csharp
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using RabbitMQ.Client;
using RabbitMQ.Client.Exceptions;

namespace NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Messaging;

public sealed class RabbitMqOptions
{
    public const string SectionName = "RabbitMq";

    [Required] public string Host { get; set; } = "localhost";
    [Range(1, 65535)] public int Port { get; set; } = 5672;
    [Required] public string UserName { get; set; } = "nexusrag";
    [Required] public string Password { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string VirtualHost { get; set; } = "/";
    public bool UseTls { get; set; }                       // true for CloudAMQP (port 5671)
    public string ClientName { get; set; } = "nexusrag-workers";
}

/// <summary>
/// Single source of truth for broker topology. The Python worker declares the SAME names and arguments
/// (ai-services/app/messaging/topology.py). Declarations are idempotent.
/// </summary>
public static class RabbitMqTopology
{
    public const string IngestionExchange = "nexus.ingestion";
    public const string RequestsQueue = "ingestion.requests";
    public const string RequestsDlq = "ingestion.requests.dlq";
    public const string StatusQueue = "ingestion.status";
    public const string StatusDlq = "ingestion.status.dlq";
    public const string IngestRoutingKey = "document.ingest.requested";
    public const string DeleteRoutingKey = "document.delete.requested";
    public const string StatusRoutingKey = "document.ingestion.status";

    public static readonly (string Queue, int TtlMs)[] RetryQueues =
    [
        ("ingestion.requests.retry.10s", 10_000),
        ("ingestion.requests.retry.60s", 60_000),
        ("ingestion.requests.retry.300s", 300_000),
    ];

    public static async Task DeclareAsync(IChannel channel, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        await channel.ExchangeDeclareAsync(IngestionExchange, ExchangeType.Topic, durable: true, autoDelete: false,
            arguments: null, cancellationToken: ct);

        await DeclareQueueAsync(channel, RequestsDlq, null, ct);
        await DeclareQueueAsync(channel, StatusDlq, null, ct);

        await DeclareQueueAsync(channel, RequestsQueue, DeadLetterTo(RequestsDlq), ct);
        await channel.QueueBindAsync(RequestsQueue, IngestionExchange, IngestRoutingKey, cancellationToken: ct);
        await channel.QueueBindAsync(RequestsQueue, IngestionExchange, DeleteRoutingKey, cancellationToken: ct);

        await DeclareQueueAsync(channel, StatusQueue, DeadLetterTo(StatusDlq), ct);
        await channel.QueueBindAsync(StatusQueue, IngestionExchange, StatusRoutingKey, cancellationToken: ct);

        foreach (var (queue, ttl) in RetryQueues)
        {
            await DeclareQueueAsync(channel, queue, new Dictionary<string, object?>
            {
                ["x-message-ttl"] = ttl,
                ["x-dead-letter-exchange"] = IngestionExchange,
                ["x-dead-letter-routing-key"] = IngestRoutingKey,
            }, ct);
        }
    }

    private static Dictionary<string, object?> DeadLetterTo(string queue) => new()
    {
        ["x-dead-letter-exchange"] = "",
        ["x-dead-letter-routing-key"] = queue,
    };

    private static Task DeclareQueueAsync(IChannel channel, string name, IDictionary<string, object?>? args, CancellationToken ct) =>
        channel.QueueDeclareAsync(name, durable: true, exclusive: false, autoDelete: false, arguments: args, cancellationToken: ct);
}

/// <summary>One long-lived connection per process (RabbitMQ best practice), with automatic recovery.</summary>
public sealed class RabbitMqConnection(IOptions<RabbitMqOptions> options, ILogger<RabbitMqConnection> logger) : IAsyncDisposable
{
    private readonly SemaphoreSlim _lock = new(1, 1);
    private IConnection? _connection;
    private bool _topologyDeclared;

    public async Task<IConnection> GetConnectionAsync(CancellationToken ct)
    {
        if (_connection is { IsOpen: true }) return _connection;

        await _lock.WaitAsync(ct);
        try
        {
            if (_connection is { IsOpen: true }) return _connection;

            var o = options.Value;
            var factory = new ConnectionFactory
            {
                HostName = o.Host,
                Port = o.Port,
                UserName = o.UserName,
                Password = o.Password,
                VirtualHost = o.VirtualHost,
                ClientProvidedName = o.ClientName,
                AutomaticRecoveryEnabled = true,
                TopologyRecoveryEnabled = true,
                NetworkRecoveryInterval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5),
            };
            if (o.UseTls) factory.Ssl = new SslOption { Enabled = true, ServerName = o.Host };

            var delay = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2);
            while (true)
            {
                try
                {
                    _connection = await factory.CreateConnectionAsync(ct);
                    break;
                }
                catch (BrokerUnreachableException ex)
                {
                    logger.LogWarning(ex, "RabbitMQ unreachable at {Host}:{Port}; retrying in {Delay}", o.Host, o.Port, delay);
                    await Task.Delay(delay, ct);
                    delay = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(Math.Min(delay.TotalSeconds * 2, 30));
                }
            }

            if (!_topologyDeclared)
            {
                await using var channel = await _connection.CreateChannelAsync(cancellationToken: ct);
                await RabbitMqTopology.DeclareAsync(channel, ct);
                _topologyDeclared = true;
                logger.LogInformation("RabbitMQ topology declared");
            }

            return _connection;
        }
        finally
        {
            _lock.Release();
        }
    }

    public async Task<IChannel> CreatePublisherChannelAsync(CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var connection = await GetConnectionAsync(ct);
        return await connection.CreateChannelAsync(
            new CreateChannelOptions(publisherConfirmationsEnabled: true, publisherConfirmationTrackingEnabled: true), ct);
    }

    public async Task<IChannel> CreateConsumerChannelAsync(ushort prefetch, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var connection = await GetConnectionAsync(ct);
        var channel = await connection.CreateChannelAsync(cancellationToken: ct);
        await channel.BasicQosAsync(prefetchSize: 0, prefetchCount: prefetch, global: false, cancellationToken: ct);
        return channel;
    }

    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()
    {
        if (_connection is not null)
        {
            await _connection.CloseAsync();
            await _connection.DisposeAsync();
        }
        _lock.Dispose();
    }
}

/// <summary>Publishes with publisher confirms + mandatory routing: a call only returns once the broker has the message.</summary>
internal sealed class RabbitMqPublisher(RabbitMqConnection connection) : IMessagePublisher, IAsyncDisposable
{
    private readonly SemaphoreSlim _lock = new(1, 1);
    private IChannel? _channel;

    public async Task PublishAsync(string routingKey, string messageType, string messageId, ReadOnlyMemory<byte> body,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await _lock.WaitAsync(cancellationToken);
        try
        {
            if (_channel is not { IsOpen: true })
            {
                if (_channel is not null) await _channel.DisposeAsync();
                _channel = await connection.CreatePublisherChannelAsync(cancellationToken);
            }

            var properties = new BasicProperties
            {
                Persistent = true,
                ContentType = "application/json",
                MessageId = messageId,
                Type = messageType,
                Timestamp = new AmqpTimestamp(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds()),
            };

            await _channel.BasicPublishAsync(RabbitMqTopology.IngestionExchange, routingKey, mandatory: true,
                basicProperties: properties, body: body, cancellationToken: cancellationToken);
        }
        finally
        {
            _lock.Release();
        }
    }

    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()
    {
        if (_channel is not null) await _channel.DisposeAsync();
        _lock.Dispose();
    }
}
backend/NexusRAG.Infrastructure/Messaging/OutboxDispatcher.cs
csharp
using System.Text;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Operations;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Persistence;

namespace NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Messaging;

/// <summary>
/// Transactional-outbox relay. Rows are locked with FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED, so several worker instances
/// can run without double-publishing in the normal case. Duplicates are still possible after a crash
/// between publish and commit; consumers are idempotent, so that is safe.
/// </summary>
public sealed class OutboxDispatcher(
    NexusRagDbContext db,
    IMessagePublisher publisher,
    TimeProvider time,
    ILogger<OutboxDispatcher> logger)
{
    public const int BatchSize = 50;

    /// <returns>Number of messages processed (published or rescheduled).</returns>
    public async Task<int> DispatchBatchAsync(CancellationToken ct)
    {
        if (!db.Database.IsRelational())
        {
            // Test path (EF InMemory): no row locking available.
            var now = time.GetUtcNow();
            var pending = await db.OutboxMessages
                .Where(m => m.ProcessedAt == null && (m.NextAttemptAt == null || m.NextAttemptAt <= now))
                .OrderBy(m => m.CreatedAt).Take(BatchSize)
                .ToListAsync(ct);
            return await PublishAndSaveAsync(pending, ct);
        }

        var strategy = db.Database.CreateExecutionStrategy();
        return await strategy.ExecuteAsync(async () =>
        {
            db.ChangeTracker.Clear();
            await using var transaction = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync(ct);
            var now = time.GetUtcNow();

            var pending = await db.OutboxMessages.FromSqlInterpolated($"""
                SELECT * FROM "OutboxMessages"
                WHERE "ProcessedAt" IS NULL AND ("NextAttemptAt" IS NULL OR "NextAttemptAt" <= {now})
                ORDER BY "CreatedAt"
                LIMIT {BatchSize}
                FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
                """).ToListAsync(ct);

            var processed = await PublishAndSaveAsync(pending, ct);
            await transaction.CommitAsync(ct);
            return processed;
        });
    }

    private async Task<int> PublishAndSaveAsync(List<OutboxMessage> pending, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        foreach (var message in pending)
        {
            try
            {
                await publisher.PublishAsync(message.Type, message.Type, message.Id.ToString(),
                    Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(message.Payload), ct);
                message.ProcessedAt = time.GetUtcNow();
            }
            catch (Exception ex) when (ex is not OperationCanceledException)
            {
                message.Attempts++;
                message.LastError = ex.Message.Length > 2000 ? ex.Message[..2000] : ex.Message;
                message.NextAttemptAt = time.GetUtcNow().AddSeconds(Math.Min(300, Math.Pow(2, message.Attempts)));
                logger.LogWarning(ex, "Failed to publish outbox message {MessageId} (attempt {Attempt})", message.Id, message.Attempts);
            }
        }

        if (pending.Count > 0) await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        return pending.Count;
    }
}
backend/NexusRAG.Infrastructure/DependencyInjection.cs (replace)
csharp
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection.Extensions;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Auth;
using NexusRAG.Application.Documents;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.AiService;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Identity;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Messaging;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Storage;

namespace NexusRAG.Infrastructure;

public static class DependencyInjection
{
    /// <summary>Everything the HTTP API needs.</summary>
    public static IServiceCollection AddInfrastructure(this IServiceCollection services, IConfiguration configuration)
    {
        services.AddPersistence();

        AddValidatedOptions<AiServiceOptions>(services, configuration, AiServiceOptions.SectionName);
        AddValidatedOptions<JwtOptions>(services, configuration, JwtOptions.SectionName);
        AddValidatedOptions<StorageOptions>(services, configuration, StorageOptions.SectionName);
        AddValidatedOptions<DocumentUploadOptions>(services, configuration, DocumentUploadOptions.SectionName);
        services.AddOptions<AuthOptions>().Bind(configuration.GetSection(AuthOptions.SectionName));

        services.AddSingleton<ITokenService, JwtTokenService>();
        services.AddSingleton<IPasswordService, PasswordService>();

        services.AddSingleton<S3ObjectStorage>();
        services.AddSingleton<IObjectStorage>(sp => sp.GetRequiredService<S3ObjectStorage>());

        services.AddHttpClient<IAiServiceClient, AiServiceClient>((sp, client) =>
            {
                var options = sp.GetRequiredService<IOptions<AiServiceOptions>>().Value;
                client.BaseAddress = new Uri(options.BaseUrl.TrimEnd('/') + "/");
                client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Add("X-Service-Token", options.ServiceToken);
            })
            .AddStandardResilienceHandler();

        services.AddHealthChecks()
            .AddCheck<DatabaseHealthCheck>("postgres", tags: ["ready"])
            .AddCheck<StorageHealthCheck>("object-storage", tags: ["ready"])
            .AddCheck<AiServiceHealthCheck>("ai-service", tags: ["ready"]);

        return services;
    }

    /// <summary>PostgreSQL only (used by both API and Workers).</summary>
    public static IServiceCollection AddPersistence(this IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.TryAddSingleton(TimeProvider.System);
        services.AddDbContext<NexusRagDbContext>((sp, options) =>
        {
            var connectionString = sp.GetRequiredService<IConfiguration>().GetConnectionString("Postgres");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(connectionString))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("ConnectionStrings:Postgres is not configured.");
            options.UseNpgsql(connectionString, npgsql => npgsql.EnableRetryOnFailure(3));
        });
        services.AddScoped<IApplicationDbContext>(sp => sp.GetRequiredService<NexusRagDbContext>());
        return services;
    }

    /// <summary>RabbitMQ connection, publisher and outbox relay (Workers host).</summary>
    public static IServiceCollection AddMessaging(this IServiceCollection services, IConfiguration configuration)
    {
        AddValidatedOptions<RabbitMqOptions>(services, configuration, RabbitMqOptions.SectionName);
        services.AddSingleton<RabbitMqConnection>();
        services.AddSingleton<IMessagePublisher, RabbitMqPublisher>();
        services.AddScoped<OutboxDispatcher>();
        return services;
    }

    private static void AddValidatedOptions<T>(IServiceCollection services, IConfiguration configuration, string section)
        where T : class =>
        services.AddOptions<T>().Bind(configuration.GetSection(section)).ValidateDataAnnotations().ValidateOnStart();
}

backend/NexusRAG.Workers/OutboxPublisherWorker.cs
csharp
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Messaging;

namespace NexusRAG.Workers;

public sealed class OutboxOptions
{
    public const string SectionName = "Outbox";
    public int PollIntervalSeconds { get; set; } = 2;
}

internal sealed class OutboxPublisherWorker(
    IServiceScopeFactory scopes,
    IOptions<OutboxOptions> options,
    ILogger<OutboxPublisherWorker> logger) : BackgroundService
{
    protected override async Task ExecuteAsync(CancellationToken stoppingToken)
    {
        var idleDelay = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(Math.Max(1, options.Value.PollIntervalSeconds));
        logger.LogInformation("Outbox publisher started (poll every {Delay})", idleDelay);

        while (!stoppingToken.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            var processed = 0;
            try
            {
                using var scope = scopes.CreateScope();
                processed = await scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<OutboxDispatcher>().DispatchBatchAsync(stoppingToken);
            }
            catch (OperationCanceledException) when (stoppingToken.IsCancellationRequested)
            {
                break;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                logger.LogError(ex, "Outbox dispatch cycle failed");
            }

            // Keep draining while full batches come back; otherwise wait.
            if (processed < OutboxDispatcher.BatchSize)
                await Task.Delay(idleDelay, stoppingToken);
        }
    }
}
backend/NexusRAG.Workers/IngestionStatusConsumerWorker.cs
csharp
using System.Text.Json;
using NexusRAG.Application.Documents;
using NexusRAG.Application.Ingestion;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Messaging;
using RabbitMQ.Client;
using RabbitMQ.Client.Events;

namespace NexusRAG.Workers;

/// <summary>Consumes status events from Python and applies them through the domain state machine.</summary>
internal sealed class IngestionStatusConsumerWorker(
    RabbitMqConnection rabbit,
    IServiceScopeFactory scopes,
    ILogger<IngestionStatusConsumerWorker> logger) : BackgroundService
{
    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new(JsonSerializerDefaults.Web);
    private IChannel? _channel;

    protected override async Task ExecuteAsync(CancellationToken stoppingToken)
    {
        _channel = await rabbit.CreateConsumerChannelAsync(prefetch: 20, stoppingToken);

        var consumer = new AsyncEventingBasicConsumer(_channel);
        consumer.ReceivedAsync += (_, args) => HandleAsync(args, stoppingToken);

        await _channel.BasicConsumeAsync(queue: RabbitMqTopology.StatusQueue, autoAck: false, consumer: consumer,
            cancellationToken: stoppingToken);
        logger.LogInformation("Consuming {Queue}", RabbitMqTopology.StatusQueue);

        await Task.Delay(Timeout.Infinite, stoppingToken);
    }

    private async Task HandleAsync(BasicDeliverEventArgs args, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var channel = _channel!;
        try
        {
            var statusEvent = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<IngestionStatusEvent>(args.Body.Span, JsonOptions)
                              ?? throw new JsonException("Empty message");

            using var scope = scopes.CreateScope();
            var result = await scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<IIngestionStatusHandler>().HandleAsync(statusEvent, ct);
            logger.LogDebug("Status {Status} for job {JobId}: {Result}", statusEvent.Status, statusEvent.JobId, result);

            await channel.BasicAckAsync(args.DeliveryTag, multiple: false, ct);
        }
        catch (JsonException ex)
        {
            logger.LogError(ex, "Malformed status message; dead-lettering");
            await channel.BasicNackAsync(args.DeliveryTag, multiple: false, requeue: false, ct);
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (ct.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            // Not acked: the broker redelivers it after restart.
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            logger.LogError(ex, "Failed to apply status event; {Action}", args.Redelivered ? "dead-lettering" : "requeueing once");
            await channel.BasicNackAsync(args.DeliveryTag, multiple: false, requeue: !args.Redelivered, ct);
        }
    }

    public override async Task StopAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await base.StopAsync(cancellationToken);
        if (_channel is not null) await _channel.DisposeAsync();
    }
}

backend/NexusRAG.Tests/Ingestion/IngestionStatusHandlerTests.cs
csharp
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Documents;
using NexusRAG.Application.Ingestion;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Documents;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Ingestion;
using NexusRAG.Domain.KnowledgeBases;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Tenancy;

namespace NexusRAG.Tests.Ingestion;

public class IngestionStatusHandlerTests
{
    private static NexusRagDbContext NewContext(string name) =>
        new(new DbContextOptionsBuilder<NexusRagDbContext>().UseInMemoryDatabase(name).Options,
            new SystemTenantContext(), TimeProvider.System);

    private sealed record Seed(string Db, Guid TenantId, Guid DocumentId, Guid JobId, Guid VersionId);

    private static async Task<Seed> SeedAsync(bool documentDeleted = false, int currentVersion = 1)
    {
        var dbName = Guid.NewGuid().ToString();
        var tenant = Guid.NewGuid();
        await using var db = NewContext(dbName);

        var kb = new KnowledgeBase { TenantId = tenant, Name = "HR" };
        var doc = new Document
        {
            TenantId = tenant, KnowledgeBaseId = kb.Id, FileName = "p.txt", ContentType = "text/plain",
            FileExtension = ".txt", CurrentVersion = currentVersion, IsDeleted = documentDeleted,
        };
        var version = new DocumentVersion { TenantId = tenant, DocumentId = doc.Id, VersionNumber = 1, ObjectKey = "k", ContentHash = "h" };
        var job = new IngestionJob { TenantId = tenant, DocumentId = doc.Id, DocumentVersionId = version.Id };

        db.AddRange(kb, doc, version, job);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        return new Seed(dbName, tenant, doc.Id, job.Id, version.Id);
    }

    private static IngestionStatusEvent Event(Seed s, string status, int? chunks = null,
        IReadOnlyList<ProviderUsageEntry>? usage = null) =>
        new(Guid.NewGuid(), MessageTypes.IngestionStatus, 1, s.TenantId, s.DocumentId, 1, s.JobId, status,
            null, chunks, chunks is null ? null : "gemini-embedding-001", null, usage, DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

    private static IngestionStatusHandler Handler(NexusRagDbContext db) =>
        new(db, TimeProvider.System, NullLogger<IngestionStatusHandler>.Instance);

    [Fact]
    public async Task FullSequence_ReachesIndexed_AndUpdatesVersion()
    {
        var seed = await SeedAsync();
        await using var db = NewContext(seed.Db);
        var handler = Handler(db);

        foreach (var status in new[] { "QUEUED", "PARSING", "CHUNKING", "EMBEDDING", "INDEXING" })
            Assert.Equal(StatusHandlingResult.Applied, await handler.HandleAsync(Event(seed, status), default));
        Assert.Equal(StatusHandlingResult.Applied, await handler.HandleAsync(Event(seed, "INDEXED", chunks: 12), default));

        var version = await db.DocumentVersions.SingleAsync(v => v.Id == seed.VersionId);
        Assert.Equal(IngestionStatus.Indexed, version.Status);
        Assert.Equal(12, version.ChunkCount);
        Assert.Equal("gemini-embedding-001", version.EmbeddingModel);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task OutOfOrderEvent_IsIgnored()
    {
        var seed = await SeedAsync();
        await using var db = NewContext(seed.Db);
        var handler = Handler(db);

        await handler.HandleAsync(Event(seed, "QUEUED"), default);
        var result = await handler.HandleAsync(Event(seed, "INDEXING"), default);

        Assert.Equal(StatusHandlingResult.IgnoredInvalidTransition, result);
        Assert.Equal(IngestionStatus.Queued, (await db.IngestionJobs.SingleAsync()).Status);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task UnknownStatus_IsIgnored()
    {
        var seed = await SeedAsync();
        await using var db = NewContext(seed.Db);
        Assert.Equal(StatusHandlingResult.IgnoredInvalidStatus, await Handler(db).HandleAsync(Event(seed, "EXPLODED"), default));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Usage_IsRecorded_AndAggregatedPerDay()
    {
        var seed = await SeedAsync();
        await using var db = NewContext(seed.Db);
        var usage = new[] { new ProviderUsageEntry("gemini", "gemini-embedding-001", "embed", 3, 0, 1, 0, 0, 1200) };

        await Handler(db).HandleAsync(Event(seed, "QUEUED", usage: usage), default);

        Assert.Equal(1, await db.UsageRecords.CountAsync());
        var aggregate = await db.ProviderUsages.SingleAsync();
        Assert.Equal(3, aggregate.Requests);
        Assert.Equal(1, aggregate.RateLimitedCount);
        Assert.Equal(1200, aggregate.InputTokens);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task IndexedForDeletedDocument_SchedulesVectorDeletion()
    {
        var seed = await SeedAsync(documentDeleted: true);
        await using var db = NewContext(seed.Db);

        await Handler(db).HandleAsync(Event(seed, "INDEXED", chunks: 5), default);

        var outbox = await db.OutboxMessages.SingleAsync();
        Assert.Equal(MessageTypes.DeleteRequested, outbox.Type);
        Assert.Contains("\"onlyVersionsBelow\":null", outbox.Payload);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task IndexedForSupersededVersion_SchedulesOldVersionCleanup()
    {
        var seed = await SeedAsync(currentVersion: 2);
        await using var db = NewContext(seed.Db);

        await Handler(db).HandleAsync(Event(seed, "INDEXED", chunks: 5), default);

        var outbox = await db.OutboxMessages.SingleAsync();
        Assert.Contains("\"onlyVersionsBelow\":2", outbox.Payload);
    }
}
backend/NexusRAG.Tests/Ingestion/OutboxDispatcherTests.cs
csharp
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Operations;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Messaging;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Tenancy;

namespace NexusRAG.Tests.Ingestion;

public class OutboxDispatcherTests
{
    private sealed class FakePublisher(bool fail) : IMessagePublisher
    {
        public List<string> Published { get; } = [];

        public Task PublishAsync(string routingKey, string messageType, string messageId, ReadOnlyMemory<byte> body,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            if (fail) throw new InvalidOperationException("broker down");
            Published.Add(routingKey);
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        }
    }

    private static NexusRagDbContext NewContext(string name) =>
        new(new DbContextOptionsBuilder<NexusRagDbContext>().UseInMemoryDatabase(name).Options,
            new SystemTenantContext(), TimeProvider.System);

    private static async Task<string> SeedAsync()
    {
        var name = Guid.NewGuid().ToString();
        await using var db = NewContext(name);
        db.OutboxMessages.AddRange(
            new OutboxMessage { Type = "document.ingest.requested", Payload = "{}" },
            new OutboxMessage { Type = "document.delete.requested", Payload = "{}" });
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        return name;
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task PendingMessages_ArePublished_AndMarkedProcessed()
    {
        var name = await SeedAsync();
        await using var db = NewContext(name);
        var publisher = new FakePublisher(fail: false);

        var processed = await new OutboxDispatcher(db, publisher, TimeProvider.System, NullLogger<OutboxDispatcher>.Instance)
            .DispatchBatchAsync(default);

        Assert.Equal(2, processed);
        Assert.Equal(2, publisher.Published.Count);
        Assert.All(await db.OutboxMessages.ToListAsync(), m => Assert.NotNull(m.ProcessedAt));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task PublishFailure_SchedulesRetryWithBackoff()
    {
        var name = await SeedAsync();
        await using var db = NewContext(name);

        await new OutboxDispatcher(db, new FakePublisher(fail: true), TimeProvider.System, NullLogger<OutboxDispatcher>.Instance)
            .DispatchBatchAsync(default);

        Assert.All(await db.OutboxMessages.ToListAsync(), m =>
        {
            Assert.Null(m.ProcessedAt);
            Assert.Equal(1, m.Attempts);
            Assert.NotNull(m.NextAttemptAt);
            Assert.Equal("broker down", m.LastError);
        });
    }
}
