python
"""Typed configuration loaded from environment variables / .env files."""

from functools import lru_cache
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import Field, SecretStr, field_validator
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict


def is_set(secret: SecretStr | None) -> bool:
    return bool(secret and secret.get_secret_value().strip())


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=("../.env", ".env"), env_file_encoding="utf-8", extra="ignore")

    app_name: str = "nexusrag-ai-service"
    environment: str = "development"
    log_level: str = "INFO"

    ai_service_token: SecretStr

    # ---- Infrastructure ----
    redis_host: str = "localhost"
    redis_port: int = 6379
    redis_password: SecretStr | None = None

    qdrant_url: str = "http://localhost:6333"
    qdrant_api_key: SecretStr | None = None
    qdrant_collection_prefix: str = "chunks"

    rabbitmq_host: str = "localhost"
    rabbitmq_port: int = 5672
    rabbitmq_default_user: str = "nexusrag"
    rabbitmq_default_pass: SecretStr | None = None

    s3_endpoint: str = "http://localhost:9000"
    s3_access_key: str = ""
    s3_secret_key: SecretStr | None = None
    s3_bucket: str = "nexusrag-documents"
    s3_region: str = "us-east-1"

    dependency_check_timeout_seconds: float = Field(default=3.0, gt=0)

    # ---- Ingestion ----
    ingestion_concurrency: int = Field(default=2, ge=1, le=16)
    ingestion_max_attempts: int = Field(default=4, ge=1, le=10)
    chunk_target_tokens: int = Field(default=350, ge=50)
    chunk_overlap_tokens: int = Field(default=50, ge=0)
    parent_target_tokens: int = Field(default=1500, ge=200)
    table_rows_per_chunk: int = Field(default=40, ge=1)
    min_chars_per_pdf_page: int = Field(default=20, ge=0)
    embed_batch_size: int = Field(default=64, ge=1, le=100)

    # ---- Chat providers (free tiers only) ----
    provider_order: str = "groq,openrouter"
    provider_timeout_seconds: float = Field(default=60.0, gt=0)

    groq_api_key: SecretStr | None = None
    groq_base_url: str = "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1"
    groq_fast_model: str = ""
    groq_strong_model: str = ""

    openrouter_api_key: SecretStr | None = None
    openrouter_base_url: str = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1"
    openrouter_fast_model: str = ""
    openrouter_strong_model: str = ""

    # ---- Embeddings ----
    embedding_provider: str = "gemini"
    gemini_api_key: SecretStr | None = None
    embedding_model: str = "gemini-embedding-001"
    embedding_dimensions: int = Field(default=768, gt=0)

    # ---- Reranking ----
    rerank_provider: Literal["none", "jina"] = "none"
    jina_api_key: SecretStr | None = None
    jina_rerank_model: str = "jina-reranker-v2-base-multilingual"

    # ---- Local quota guards (match these to your provider consoles) ----
    groq_rpm: int = 30
    groq_rpd: int = 1000
    groq_max_concurrency: int = 4
    openrouter_rpm: int = 20
    openrouter_rpd: int = 50
    openrouter_max_concurrency: int = 2
    gemini_embed_rpm: int = 100
    gemini_embed_rpd: int = 1000
    gemini_embed_tpm: int = 30000
    jina_rpm: int = 100
    jina_tpm: int = 100000
    quota_warning_ratio: float = Field(default=0.8, gt=0, le=1)

    @field_validator("ai_service_token")
    @classmethod
    def _validate_token(cls, value: SecretStr) -> SecretStr:
        if len(value.get_secret_value()) < 32:
            raise ValueError("AI_SERVICE_TOKEN must be at least 32 characters")
        return value

    @property
    def provider_order_list(self) -> list[str]:
        return [p.strip().lower() for p in self.provider_order.split(",") if p.strip()]

    def configured_chat_providers(self) -> list[str]:
        keys = {"groq": self.groq_api_key, "openrouter": self.openrouter_api_key}
        return [name for name in self.provider_order_list if is_set(keys.get(name))]

    def provider_summary(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        """Safe-to-expose configuration summary. Never includes secrets."""
        return {
            "provider_order": self.provider_order_list,
            "chat_providers": {
                "groq": {"configured": is_set(self.groq_api_key),
                         "fast_model": self.groq_fast_model or None, "strong_model": self.groq_strong_model or None},
                "openrouter": {"configured": is_set(self.openrouter_api_key),
                               "fast_model": self.openrouter_fast_model or None,
                               "strong_model": self.openrouter_strong_model or None},
            },
            "embedding": {"provider": self.embedding_provider, "model": self.embedding_model,
                          "dimensions": self.embedding_dimensions, "configured": is_set(self.gemini_api_key)},
            "rerank": {"provider": self.rerank_provider,
                       "configured": self.rerank_provider == "none" or is_set(self.jina_api_key)},
        }


@lru_cache
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    return Settings()
ai-services/app/core/errors.py
python
class IngestionError(Exception):
    """Base class for ingestion failures."""


class PermanentIngestionError(IngestionError):
    """Retrying cannot help (bad file, missing object, integrity failure, misconfiguration)."""


class TransientIngestionError(IngestionError):
    """Temporary failure (provider quota, network, dependency down). The job is retried with backoff."""
ai-services/app/core/tokens.py
python
import math


def estimate_tokens(text: str) -> int:
    """
    Heuristic: ~4 characters per token for English text. Free providers use different tokenizers,
    so this is an estimate. It errs on the safe side for budgeting; non-Latin scripts may be underestimated.
    """
    return max(1, math.ceil(len(text) / 4))

"""Message contracts shared with .NET (camelCase JSON, see Application/Documents/IngestionContracts.cs)."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from pydantic.alias_generators import to_camel

INGEST_REQUESTED = "document.ingest.requested"
DELETE_REQUESTED = "document.delete.requested"
INGESTION_STATUS = "document.ingestion.status"


class CamelModel(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(alias_generator=to_camel, populate_by_name=True, extra="ignore")


class IngestRequested(CamelModel):
    message_id: UUID
    message_type: str
    schema_version: int
    correlation_id: UUID
    tenant_id: UUID
    knowledge_base_id: UUID
    document_id: UUID
    document_version: int
    job_id: UUID
    object_key: str
    file_name: str
    content_type: str
    content_hash: str
    access_groups: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    requested_at: datetime


class DeleteRequested(CamelModel):
    message_id: UUID
    message_type: str
    schema_version: int
    tenant_id: UUID
    knowledge_base_id: UUID
    document_id: UUID
    only_versions_below: int | None = None
    requested_at: datetime


class UsageEntry(CamelModel):
    provider: str
    model: str
    operation: str
    requests: int = 0
    failures: int = 0
    rate_limited: int = 0
    input_tokens: int = 0
    output_tokens: int = 0
    embedding_tokens: int = 0


class IngestionStatusEvent(CamelModel):
    message_id: UUID = Field(default_factory=uuid4)
    message_type: str = INGESTION_STATUS
    schema_version: int = 1
    tenant_id: UUID
    document_id: UUID
    document_version: int
    job_id: UUID
    status: str
    progress: int | None = None
    chunk_count: int | None = None
    embedding_model: str | None = None
    error: str | None = None
    usage: list[UsageEntry] = Field(default_factory=list)
    occurred_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
ai-services/app/messaging/topology.py
python
"""Broker topology - MUST match backend/NexusRAG.Infrastructure/Messaging/RabbitMq.cs exactly."""

from dataclasses import dataclass

import aio_pika
from aio_pika.abc import AbstractChannel, AbstractExchange, AbstractQueue

INGESTION_EXCHANGE = "nexus.ingestion"
REQUESTS_QUEUE = "ingestion.requests"
REQUESTS_DLQ = "ingestion.requests.dlq"
STATUS_QUEUE = "ingestion.status"
STATUS_DLQ = "ingestion.status.dlq"
INGEST_ROUTING_KEY = "document.ingest.requested"
DELETE_ROUTING_KEY = "document.delete.requested"
STATUS_ROUTING_KEY = "document.ingestion.status"
RETRY_QUEUES: tuple[tuple[str, int], ...] = (
    ("ingestion.requests.retry.10s", 10_000),
    ("ingestion.requests.retry.60s", 60_000),
    ("ingestion.requests.retry.300s", 300_000),
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Topology:
    exchange: AbstractExchange
    requests: AbstractQueue


def _dead_letter_to(queue: str) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {"x-dead-letter-exchange": "", "x-dead-letter-routing-key": queue}


async def declare_topology(channel: AbstractChannel) -> Topology:
    exchange = await channel.declare_exchange(INGESTION_EXCHANGE, aio_pika.ExchangeType.TOPIC, durable=True)

    await channel.declare_queue(REQUESTS_DLQ, durable=True)
    await channel.declare_queue(STATUS_DLQ, durable=True)

    requests = await channel.declare_queue(REQUESTS_QUEUE, durable=True, arguments=_dead_letter_to(REQUESTS_DLQ))
    await requests.bind(exchange, INGEST_ROUTING_KEY)
    await requests.bind(exchange, DELETE_ROUTING_KEY)

    status = await channel.declare_queue(STATUS_QUEUE, durable=True, arguments=_dead_letter_to(STATUS_DLQ))
    await status.bind(exchange, STATUS_ROUTING_KEY)

    for name, ttl in RETRY_QUEUES:
        await channel.declare_queue(name, durable=True, arguments={
            "x-message-ttl": ttl,
            "x-dead-letter-exchange": INGESTION_EXCHANGE,
            "x-dead-letter-routing-key": INGEST_ROUTING_KEY,
        })

    return Topology(exchange=exchange, requests=requests)
Parsing and chunking (Phase 7)
ai-services/app/ingestion/models.py
python
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Literal

BlockKind = Literal["heading", "paragraph", "table"]
ChunkType = Literal["child", "parent", "table"]


@dataclass(slots=True)
class Block:
    kind: BlockKind
    text: str
    page: int | None = None
    level: int = 0                       # heading level (1 = top)
    rows: list[list[str]] | None = None  # table rows, first row = header
    caption: str | None = None           # e.g. sheet name


@dataclass(slots=True)
class ParsedDocument:
    blocks: list[Block] = field(default_factory=list)
    page_count: int | None = None


@dataclass(slots=True)
class Chunk:
    chunk_id: str
    chunk_type: ChunkType
    content: str          # what is shown to the LLM / user
    embed_text: str       # content + document/section context (better retrieval)
    chunk_index: int
    page_number: int | None
    section: str | None
    parent_id: str | None
    token_estimate: int
ai-services/app/ingestion/parsers.py
python
"""Parsers for PDF, DOCX, PPTX, XLSX, CSV, TXT and Markdown. Pure Python libraries - no AI models, no OCR."""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import io
import re
import unicodedata
from collections import Counter
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterable
from datetime import date, datetime

from app.core.errors import PermanentIngestionError
from app.ingestion.models import Block, ParsedDocument

MAX_ROWS_PER_SHEET = 50_000


class ParseError(PermanentIngestionError):
    """The file cannot be parsed; retrying will not help."""


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- shared helpers
def normalize_text(text: str) -> str:
    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text).replace("\u00a0", " ")
    text = re.sub(r"[ \t\f\v]+", " ", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)
    return text.strip()


def decode_text(data: bytes) -> str:
    for encoding in ("utf-8-sig", "cp1252"):
        try:
            return data.decode(encoding)
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            continue
    return data.decode("latin-1")


def clean_rows(rows: Iterable[Iterable[object]]) -> list[list[str]]:
    """Strip cells, drop empty rows/columns, pad to a rectangle."""
    cleaned = [[normalize_text("" if c is None else str(c)) for c in row] for row in rows]
    cleaned = [r for r in cleaned if any(r)]
    if not cleaned:
        return []
    width = max(len(r) for r in cleaned)
    cleaned = [r + [""] * (width - len(r)) for r in cleaned]
    keep = [i for i in range(width) if any(r[i] for r in cleaned)]
    return [[r[i] for i in keep] for r in cleaned]


def rows_to_markdown(rows: list[list[str]]) -> str:
    if not rows:
        return ""

    def fmt(row: list[str]) -> str:
        return "| " + " | ".join(c.replace("|", "\\|").replace("\n", " ") for c in row) + " |"

    header = rows[0]
    lines = [fmt(header), "| " + " | ".join("---" for _ in header) + " |"]
    lines.extend(fmt(r) for r in rows[1:])
    return "\n".join(lines)


_NUMBERED_HEADING = re.compile(r"^(\d+(\.\d+)*\.?|[IVXLC]+\.|[A-Z]\.)\s+\S")
_PAGE_NUMBER = re.compile(r"^(page\s*)?\d+(\s*(of|/)\s*\d+)?$", re.IGNORECASE)


def looks_like_heading(line: str) -> bool:
    """Conservative heuristic for PDFs/plain text (no font information is used)."""
    if len(line) > 90 or line.endswith((".", ",", ";")):
        return False
    words = line.split()
    if not 1 <= len(words) <= 12:
        return False
    if _NUMBERED_HEADING.match(line):
        return True
    letters = [c for c in line if c.isalpha()]
    return len(letters) >= 3 and sum(c.isupper() for c in letters) / len(letters) > 0.8


def _join_lines(lines: list[str]) -> str:
    result = ""
    for line in lines:
        if result.endswith("-") and line[:1].islower():
            result = result[:-1] + line          # re-join hyphenated words
        else:
            result = f"{result} {line}" if result else line
    return result


def _lines_to_paragraphs(lines: list[str]) -> list[tuple[str, bool]]:
    """Returns (text, is_heading) tuples."""
    out: list[tuple[str, bool]] = []
    current: list[str] = []

    def flush() -> None:
        if current:
            out.append((_join_lines(current), False))
            current.clear()

    for raw in lines:
        line = raw.strip()
        if not line or _PAGE_NUMBER.match(line):
            flush()
            continue
        if looks_like_heading(line):
            flush()
            out.append((line, True))
            continue
        current.append(line)
        if line.endswith((".", "!", "?", ":")) and len(line) < 60:
            flush()
    flush()
    return out


def _normalize_line_key(line: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"\d+", "#", line.strip().lower())


def _repeated_lines(page_texts: list[str]) -> set[str]:
    """Headers/footers: lines in the first/last 2 lines of >= 60% of pages."""
    if len(page_texts) < 3:
        return set()
    counter: Counter[str] = Counter()
    for text in page_texts:
        lines = [l for l in text.splitlines() if l.strip()]
        candidates = {_normalize_line_key(l) for l in lines[:2] + lines[-2:]}
        counter.update(candidates)
    threshold = 0.6 * len(page_texts)
    return {key for key, count in counter.items() if count >= threshold}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- PDF
def parse_pdf(data: bytes, min_chars_per_page: int = 20) -> ParsedDocument:
    import pdfplumber

    pages: list[tuple[int, str, list[list[list[str]]]]] = []
    with pdfplumber.open(io.BytesIO(data)) as pdf:
        page_count = len(pdf.pages)
        for number, page in enumerate(pdf.pages, start=1):
            tables: list[list[list[str]]] = []
            text_page = page
            try:
                found = page.find_tables()
                for table in found:
                    tables.append(table.extract())
                    text_page = text_page.outside_bbox(table.bbox)   # avoid duplicating table text
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - table detection is best effort
                text_page = page
            pages.append((number, text_page.extract_text() or "", tables))

    total_chars = sum(len(text.strip()) for _, text, _ in pages)
    if page_count and total_chars / page_count < min_chars_per_page:
        raise ParseError(
            "The PDF has no usable text layer (probably scanned). OCR is not supported because it would "
            "require a local model or an unverified free OCR API.")

    repeated = _repeated_lines([text for _, text, _ in pages])
    blocks: list[Block] = []
    for number, text, tables in pages:
        lines = [l for l in text.splitlines() if _normalize_line_key(l) not in repeated]
        for paragraph, is_heading in _lines_to_paragraphs(lines):
            blocks.append(Block("heading" if is_heading else "paragraph", paragraph, page=number,
                                level=2 if is_heading else 0))
        for raw_rows in tables:
            rows = clean_rows(raw_rows)
            if len(rows) >= 2:
                blocks.append(Block("table", " | ".join(rows[0]), page=number, rows=rows))
    return ParsedDocument(blocks=blocks, page_count=page_count)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- DOCX
def _docx_heading_level(style_name: str) -> int:
    if style_name == "Title":
        return 1
    match = re.match(r"Heading (\d)", style_name)
    return int(match.group(1)) if match else 0


def parse_docx(data: bytes) -> ParsedDocument:
    from docx import Document as DocxDocument
    from docx.table import Table
    from docx.text.paragraph import Paragraph

    doc = DocxDocument(io.BytesIO(data))
    blocks: list[Block] = []
    for child in doc.element.body.iterchildren():           # keeps paragraphs and tables in document order
        tag = child.tag.rsplit("}", 1)[-1]
        if tag == "p":
            paragraph = Paragraph(child, doc)
            text = paragraph.text.strip()
            if not text:
                continue
            style = paragraph.style.name if paragraph.style is not None else ""
            level = _docx_heading_level(style or "")
            blocks.append(Block("heading" if level else "paragraph", text, level=level))
        elif tag == "tbl":
            table = Table(child, doc)
            rows = clean_rows([[cell.text for cell in row.cells] for row in table.rows])
            if rows:
                blocks.append(Block("table", " | ".join(rows[0]), rows=rows))
    return ParsedDocument(blocks=blocks)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- PPTX
def parse_pptx(data: bytes) -> ParsedDocument:
    from pptx import Presentation
    from pptx.enum.shapes import MSO_SHAPE_TYPE

    def iter_shapes(shapes):
        for shape in shapes:
            if shape.shape_type == MSO_SHAPE_TYPE.GROUP:
                yield from iter_shapes(shape.shapes)
            else:
                yield shape

    presentation = Presentation(io.BytesIO(data))
    blocks: list[Block] = []
    slide_count = 0
    for number, slide in enumerate(presentation.slides, start=1):
        slide_count = number
        title_shape = slide.shapes.title
        title_id = title_shape.shape_id if title_shape is not None else None
        if title_shape is not None and title_shape.has_text_frame and title_shape.text_frame.text.strip():
            blocks.append(Block("heading", title_shape.text_frame.text.strip(), page=number, level=1))
        else:
            blocks.append(Block("heading", f"Slide {number}", page=number, level=1))

        for shape in iter_shapes(slide.shapes):
            if shape.shape_id == title_id:
                continue
            if getattr(shape, "has_table", False) and shape.has_table:
                rows = clean_rows([[cell.text for cell in row.cells] for row in shape.table.rows])
                if rows:
                    blocks.append(Block("table", " | ".join(rows[0]), page=number, rows=rows))
            elif getattr(shape, "has_text_frame", False) and shape.has_text_frame:
                text = "\n".join(p.text for p in shape.text_frame.paragraphs if p.text.strip())
                if text.strip():
                    blocks.append(Block("paragraph", text, page=number))

        if slide.has_notes_slide and slide.notes_slide.notes_text_frame is not None:
            notes = slide.notes_slide.notes_text_frame.text.strip()
            if notes:
                blocks.append(Block("paragraph", f"Speaker notes: {notes}", page=number))
    return ParsedDocument(blocks=blocks, page_count=slide_count)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- XLSX / CSV
def _cell_str(value: object) -> str:
    if value is None:
        return ""
    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        return value.date().isoformat() if value.time() == datetime.min.time() else value.isoformat()
    if isinstance(value, date):
        return value.isoformat()
    if isinstance(value, float) and value.is_integer():
        return str(int(value))
    return str(value)


def parse_xlsx(data: bytes) -> ParsedDocument:
    from openpyxl import load_workbook

    workbook = load_workbook(io.BytesIO(data), read_only=True, data_only=True)
    blocks: list[Block] = []
    try:
        for sheet in workbook.worksheets:
            raw_rows: list[list[str]] = []
            for row in sheet.iter_rows(values_only=True):
                cells = [_cell_str(v) for v in row]
                if any(c.strip() for c in cells):
                    raw_rows.append(cells)
                if len(raw_rows) >= MAX_ROWS_PER_SHEET:
                    break
            rows = clean_rows(raw_rows)
            if rows:
                blocks.append(Block("heading", f"Sheet: {sheet.title}", level=1))
                blocks.append(Block("table", " | ".join(rows[0]), rows=rows, caption=sheet.title))
    finally:
        workbook.close()
    return ParsedDocument(blocks=blocks)


def parse_csv(data: bytes) -> ParsedDocument:
    text = decode_text(data)
    try:
        dialect = csv.Sniffer().sniff(text[:4096], delimiters=",;\t|")
    except csv.Error:
        dialect = csv.excel
    rows = clean_rows(csv.reader(io.StringIO(text), dialect))
    if len(rows) > MAX_ROWS_PER_SHEET:
        rows = rows[:MAX_ROWS_PER_SHEET]
    return ParsedDocument(blocks=[Block("table", " | ".join(rows[0]), rows=rows)] if rows else [])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- TXT / Markdown
def parse_txt(data: bytes) -> ParsedDocument:
    text = decode_text(data)
    blocks: list[Block] = []
    for raw in re.split(r"\n\s*\n", text):
        lines = [l.strip() for l in raw.splitlines() if l.strip()]
        if not lines:
            continue
        if len(lines) == 1 and looks_like_heading(lines[0]):
            blocks.append(Block("heading", lines[0], level=2))
        else:
            blocks.append(Block("paragraph", _join_lines(lines)))
    return ParsedDocument(blocks=blocks)


_MD_HEADING = re.compile(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.+?)\s*#*\s*$")
_MD_TABLE_SEPARATOR = re.compile(r"^\|?\s*:?-{2,}:?\s*(\|\s*:?-{2,}:?\s*)*\|?$")


def parse_markdown(data: bytes) -> ParsedDocument:
    text = decode_text(data)
    blocks: list[Block] = []
    paragraph: list[str] = []
    table: list[str] = []
    code: list[str] = []
    in_code = False

    def flush_paragraph() -> None:
        if paragraph:
            blocks.append(Block("paragraph", _join_lines(paragraph)))
            paragraph.clear()

    def flush_table() -> None:
        if table:
            rows = clean_rows([l.strip().strip("|").split("|") for l in table if not _MD_TABLE_SEPARATOR.match(l)])
            if rows:
                blocks.append(Block("table", " | ".join(rows[0]), rows=rows))
            table.clear()

    for line in text.splitlines():
        stripped = line.strip()
        if stripped.startswith("```"):
            if in_code:
                blocks.append(Block("paragraph", "\n".join(code)))
                code.clear()
            else:
                flush_paragraph()
                flush_table()
            in_code = not in_code
            continue
        if in_code:
            code.append(line)
            continue
        if stripped.startswith("|"):
            flush_paragraph()
            table.append(stripped)
            continue
        flush_table()
        heading = _MD_HEADING.match(stripped)
        if heading:
            flush_paragraph()
            blocks.append(Block("heading", heading.group(2), level=len(heading.group(1))))
        elif not stripped:
            flush_paragraph()
        else:
            paragraph.append(stripped.lstrip("-*+ ").strip() if re.match(r"^[-*+]\s", stripped) else stripped)

    flush_paragraph()
    flush_table()
    if code:
        blocks.append(Block("paragraph", "\n".join(code)))
    return ParsedDocument(blocks=blocks)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- dispatcher
def parse_document(data: bytes, extension: str, min_chars_per_pdf_page: int = 20) -> ParsedDocument:
    parsers: dict[str, Callable[[bytes], ParsedDocument]] = {
        ".pdf": lambda d: parse_pdf(d, min_chars_per_pdf_page),
        ".docx": parse_docx,
        ".pptx": parse_pptx,
        ".xlsx": parse_xlsx,
        ".csv": parse_csv,
        ".txt": parse_txt,
        ".md": parse_markdown,
    }
    parser = parsers.get(extension.lower())
    if parser is None:
        raise ParseError(f"Unsupported file type '{extension}'.")
    try:
        document = parser(data)
    except ParseError:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - any library failure means the file is unreadable
        raise ParseError(f"Could not parse {extension} file ({type(exc).__name__}).") from exc

    if not any(block.text.strip() or block.rows for block in document.blocks):
        raise ParseError("No extractable text was found in the document.")
    return document
ai-services/app/ingestion/chunking.py
python
"""
Structure-aware chunking:
  * Headings build a section path ("Leave Policy > India > Carry-forward").
  * Paragraphs are packed into child chunks of ~target tokens; long paragraphs use a sliding window with overlap.
  * Consecutive children of one section are grouped into a parent chunk (context expansion, Phase 10).
  * Tables become their own chunks, split by rows with the header repeated.
  * Every embedded chunk carries document + section context (cheap "contextual retrieval", no LLM call).
Semantic (embedding-based) chunking is deliberately NOT used: it would spend free embedding quota per sentence.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from app.core.tokens import estimate_tokens
from app.ingestion.models import Block, Chunk, ParsedDocument
from app.ingestion.parsers import normalize_text, rows_to_markdown

CHUNK_NAMESPACE = uuid.UUID("6f1c2a8e-3b5d-4c7a-9e21-0d4b8f6a1c35")
_SENTENCE_SPLIT = re.compile(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+(?=[A-Z0-9\"'(\[])")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ChunkingConfig:
    target_tokens: int = 350
    overlap_tokens: int = 50
    parent_tokens: int = 1500
    table_rows_per_chunk: int = 40

    @classmethod
    def from_settings(cls, settings) -> "ChunkingConfig":
        return cls(settings.chunk_target_tokens, settings.chunk_overlap_tokens,
                   settings.parent_target_tokens, settings.table_rows_per_chunk)


@dataclass
class _State:
    id_seed: str
    file_name: str
    chunks: list[Chunk] = field(default_factory=list)
    counter: int = 0

    def next_id(self, kind: str) -> tuple[str, int]:
        index = self.counter
        self.counter += 1
        return str(uuid.uuid5(CHUNK_NAMESPACE, f"{self.id_seed}:{kind}:{index}")), index


class Chunker:
    def __init__(self, config: ChunkingConfig | None = None) -> None:
        self._c = config or ChunkingConfig()

    def chunk(self, document: ParsedDocument, *, id_seed: str, file_name: str) -> list[Chunk]:
        state = _State(id_seed=id_seed, file_name=file_name)
        stack: list[tuple[int, str]] = []
        pending: list[tuple[str, int | None]] = []

        def path() -> str | None:
            return " > ".join(title for _, title in stack) or None

        for block in document.blocks:
            if block.kind == "heading":
                self._flush(pending, path(), state)
                pending = []
                level = max(1, block.level or 2)
                while stack and stack[-1][0] >= level:
                    stack.pop()
                stack.append((level, normalize_text(block.text)[:200]))
            elif block.kind == "table":
                self._flush(pending, path(), state)
                pending = []
                self._table_chunks(block, path(), state)
            else:
                text = normalize_text(block.text)
                if text:
                    pending.append((text, block.page))
        self._flush(pending, path(), state)
        return state.chunks

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- text
    def _flush(self, paragraphs: list[tuple[str, int | None]], section: str | None, state: _State) -> None:
        if not paragraphs:
            return
        target = self._c.target_tokens

        pieces: list[tuple[str, int | None]] = []
        for text, page in paragraphs:
            if estimate_tokens(text) <= target:
                pieces.append((text, page))
            else:
                pieces.extend((window, page) for window in self._split_long(text))

        children: list[tuple[str, int | None]] = []
        buffer: list[str] = []
        buffer_tokens = 0
        buffer_page: int | None = None
        for text, page in pieces:
            tokens = estimate_tokens(text)
            if buffer and buffer_tokens + tokens > target:
                children.append(("\n\n".join(buffer), buffer_page))
                buffer, buffer_tokens = [], 0
            if not buffer:
                buffer_page = page
            buffer.append(text)
            buffer_tokens += tokens
        if buffer:
            children.append(("\n\n".join(buffer), buffer_page))

        groups: list[list[tuple[str, int | None]]] = []
        current: list[tuple[str, int | None]] = []
        current_tokens = 0
        for child in children:
            tokens = estimate_tokens(child[0])
            if current and current_tokens + tokens > self._c.parent_tokens:
                groups.append(current)
                current, current_tokens = [], 0
            current.append(child)
            current_tokens += tokens
        if current:
            groups.append(current)

        for group in groups:
            parent_id: str | None = None
            if len(group) > 1:                       # a parent only adds value when it spans several children
                parent_id, index = state.next_id("parent")
                content = "\n\n".join(text for text, _ in group)
                state.chunks.append(Chunk(parent_id, "parent", content, content, index, group[0][1], section,
                                          None, estimate_tokens(content)))
            for text, page in group:
                chunk_id, index = state.next_id("child")
                state.chunks.append(Chunk(chunk_id, "child", text, self._context(state.file_name, section, text),
                                          index, page, section, parent_id, estimate_tokens(text)))

    def _split_long(self, text: str) -> list[str]:
        target, overlap_budget = self._c.target_tokens, self._c.overlap_tokens
        sentences: list[str] = []
        for sentence in _SENTENCE_SPLIT.split(text):
            if estimate_tokens(sentence) <= target:
                sentences.append(sentence)
            else:                                      # a single huge "sentence": hard split by words
                words = sentence.split()
                step = max(20, int(target / 1.5))
                sentences.extend(" ".join(words[i:i + step]) for i in range(0, len(words), step))

        windows: list[str] = []
        current: list[str] = []
        tokens = 0
        for sentence in sentences:
            sentence_tokens = estimate_tokens(sentence)
            if current and tokens + sentence_tokens > target:
                windows.append(" ".join(current))
                overlap: list[str] = []
                overlap_tokens = 0
                for previous in reversed(current):
                    previous_tokens = estimate_tokens(previous)
                    if overlap_tokens + previous_tokens > overlap_budget:
                        break
                    overlap.insert(0, previous)
                    overlap_tokens += previous_tokens
                current, tokens = overlap, overlap_tokens
            current.append(sentence)
            tokens += sentence_tokens
        if current:
            windows.append(" ".join(current))
        return windows

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- tables
    def _table_chunks(self, block: Block, section: str | None, state: _State) -> None:
        rows = block.rows or []
        if not rows:
            return
        header, body = rows[0], rows[1:]
        table_section = section or block.caption
        header_tokens = estimate_tokens(" | ".join(header))
        max_tokens = self._c.target_tokens * 2

        def emit(group: list[list[str]]) -> None:
            markdown = rows_to_markdown([header, *group])
            chunk_id, index = state.next_id("table")
            columns = ", ".join(h for h in header if h)
            embed = (f"Document: {state.file_name}\nSection: {table_section or 'n/a'}\n"
                     f"Table columns: {columns}\n\n{markdown}")
            state.chunks.append(Chunk(chunk_id, "table", markdown, embed, index, block.page, table_section,
                                      None, estimate_tokens(markdown)))

        group: list[list[str]] = []
        group_tokens = header_tokens
        for row in body:
            row_tokens = estimate_tokens(" | ".join(row)) + 1
            if group and (len(group) >= self._c.table_rows_per_chunk or group_tokens + row_tokens > max_tokens):
                emit(group)
                group, group_tokens = [], header_tokens
            group.append(row)
            group_tokens += row_tokens
        if group or not body:
            emit(group)

    @staticmethod
    def _context(file_name: str, section: str | None, text: str) -> str:
        return f"Document: {file_name}\nSection: {section or 'n/a'}\n\n{text}"
