ai-services/app/providers/usage.py
python
"""Per-job usage accumulation via a context variable (set by the ingestion worker / request handler)."""

from contextvars import ContextVar
from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.messaging.contracts import UsageEntry


@dataclass
class _Totals:
    requests: int = 0
    failures: int = 0
    rate_limited: int = 0
    input_tokens: int = 0
    output_tokens: int = 0
    embedding_tokens: int = 0


class UsageAccumulator:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._totals: dict[tuple[str, str, str], _Totals] = {}

    def add(self, provider: str, model: str, operation: str, *, input_tokens: int = 0, output_tokens: int = 0,
            embedding_tokens: int = 0, success: bool = True, rate_limited: bool = False) -> None:
        totals = self._totals.setdefault((provider, model, operation), _Totals())
        totals.requests += 1
        totals.failures += 0 if success else 1
        totals.rate_limited += 1 if rate_limited else 0
        totals.input_tokens += input_tokens
        totals.output_tokens += output_tokens
        totals.embedding_tokens += embedding_tokens

    def entries(self) -> list[UsageEntry]:
        return [UsageEntry(provider=p, model=m, operation=o, requests=t.requests, failures=t.failures,
                           rate_limited=t.rate_limited, input_tokens=t.input_tokens, output_tokens=t.output_tokens,
                           embedding_tokens=t.embedding_tokens)
                for (p, m, o), t in self._totals.items()]


current_usage: ContextVar[UsageAccumulator | None] = ContextVar("nexusrag_usage", default=None)


def record_usage(provider: str, model: str, operation: str, **kwargs) -> None:
    accumulator = current_usage.get()
    if accumulator is not None:
        accumulator.add(provider, model, operation, **kwargs)
ai-services/app/providers/quota.py
python
"""
Local quota guard shared by all processes through Redis. Stops calls BEFORE a free tier rejects them,
which saves quota (some providers count failed calls) and keeps behavior predictable.
Fails open if Redis is unavailable (the provider's own 429s still protect the quota).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import contextlib
import logging
import math
import time
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol

from redis.asyncio import Redis

from app.providers.base import QuotaExhaustedError, RateLimitedError

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
USAGE_TTL_SECONDS = 8 * 24 * 3600


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QuotaLimits:
    rpm: int | None = None
    rpd: int | None = None
    tpm: int | None = None
    max_concurrency: int = 4


class CounterStore(Protocol):
    async def incr(self, key: str, amount: int, ttl_seconds: int) -> int: ...
    async def hincr(self, key: str, field: str, amount: int, ttl_seconds: int) -> None: ...


class RedisCounterStore:
    def __init__(self, redis: Redis) -> None:
        self._redis = redis

    async def incr(self, key: str, amount: int, ttl_seconds: int) -> int:
        async with self._redis.pipeline(transaction=True) as pipe:
            pipe.incrby(key, amount)
            pipe.expire(key, ttl_seconds)
            value, _ = await pipe.execute()
        return int(value)

    async def hincr(self, key: str, field: str, amount: int, ttl_seconds: int) -> None:
        async with self._redis.pipeline(transaction=True) as pipe:
            pipe.hincrby(key, field, amount)
            pipe.expire(key, ttl_seconds)
            await pipe.execute()


class InMemoryCounterStore:
    """For tests and single-process development."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.values: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.hashes: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}

    async def incr(self, key: str, amount: int, ttl_seconds: int) -> int:
        self.values[key] = self.values.get(key, 0) + amount
        return self.values[key]

    async def hincr(self, key: str, field: str, amount: int, ttl_seconds: int) -> None:
        bucket = self.hashes.setdefault(key, {})
        bucket[field] = bucket.get(field, 0) + amount


class QuotaGuard:
    def __init__(self, store: CounterStore, limits: Mapping[str, QuotaLimits], *,
                 warning_ratio: float = 0.8, clock: Callable[[], float] = time.time) -> None:
        self._store = store
        self._limits = dict(limits)
        self._warning_ratio = warning_ratio
        self._clock = clock
        self._semaphores: dict[str, asyncio.Semaphore] = {}

    def concurrency(self, provider: str) -> asyncio.Semaphore:
        semaphore = self._semaphores.get(provider)
        if semaphore is None:
            semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(self._limits.get(provider, QuotaLimits()).max_concurrency)
            self._semaphores[provider] = semaphore
        return semaphore

    async def acquire(self, provider: str, tokens: int = 0) -> None:
        limits = self._limits.get(provider)
        if limits is None:
            return
        now = self._clock()
        minute = int(now // 60)
        day = time.strftime("%Y%m%d", time.gmtime(now))
        wait = 60 - (now % 60)
        taken: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
        try:
            if limits.rpm:
                key = f"nx:quota:{provider}:rpm:{minute}"
                count = await self._store.incr(key, 1, 120)
                taken.append((key, 1, 120))
                if count > limits.rpm:
                    raise RateLimitedError(provider, f"local limit of {limits.rpm} requests/minute reached", wait)
            if limits.tpm and tokens:
                key = f"nx:quota:{provider}:tpm:{minute}"
                used = await self._store.incr(key, tokens, 120)
                taken.append((key, tokens, 120))
                if used > limits.tpm:
                    raise RateLimitedError(provider, f"local limit of {limits.tpm} tokens/minute reached", wait)
            if limits.rpd:
                key = f"nx:quota:{provider}:rpd:{day}"
                used = await self._store.incr(key, 1, 172_800)
                taken.append((key, 1, 172_800))
                if used > limits.rpd:
                    raise QuotaExhaustedError(provider, f"daily free quota of {limits.rpd} requests reached")
                if used == math.ceil(limits.rpd * self._warning_ratio):
                    logger.warning("Provider %s has used %d of %d daily free requests", provider, used, limits.rpd)
        except (RateLimitedError, QuotaExhaustedError):
            await self._rollback(taken)
            raise
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            logger.warning("Quota store unavailable (%s); allowing request", type(exc).__name__)

    async def record(self, provider: str, model: str, *, input_tokens: int = 0, output_tokens: int = 0,
                     success: bool = True, rate_limited: bool = False) -> None:
        day = time.strftime("%Y%m%d", time.gmtime(self._clock()))
        key = f"nx:usage:{day}:{provider}:{model}"
        try:
            await self._store.hincr(key, "requests", 1, USAGE_TTL_SECONDS)
            if not success:
                await self._store.hincr(key, "failures", 1, USAGE_TTL_SECONDS)
            if rate_limited:
                await self._store.hincr(key, "rate_limited", 1, USAGE_TTL_SECONDS)
            if input_tokens:
                await self._store.hincr(key, "input_tokens", input_tokens, USAGE_TTL_SECONDS)
            if output_tokens:
                await self._store.hincr(key, "output_tokens", output_tokens, USAGE_TTL_SECONDS)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            logger.debug("Could not record usage: %s", type(exc).__name__)

    async def _rollback(self, taken: list[tuple[str, int, int]]) -> None:
        for key, amount, ttl in taken:
            with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
                await self._store.incr(key, -amount, ttl)
ai-services/app/providers/http.py
python
"""HTTP error mapping + bounded retries with exponential backoff and jitter."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import random
from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
from typing import TypeVar

import httpx

from app.providers.base import ProviderError, ProviderUnavailableError, QuotaExhaustedError, RateLimitedError

T = TypeVar("T")


class ProviderAuthError(ProviderUnavailableError):
    """401/403 - the API key is wrong or revoked. Never retried."""


def _retry_after(response: httpx.Response) -> float | None:
    value = response.headers.get("retry-after")
    try:
        return float(value) if value is not None else None
    except ValueError:
        return None


def raise_for_provider_status(provider: str, response: httpx.Response) -> None:
    if response.is_success:
        return
    status = response.status_code
    detail = response.text[:300].replace("\n", " ")
    if status == 429:
        raise RateLimitedError(provider, f"HTTP 429: {detail}", _retry_after(response))
    if status == 402:
        raise QuotaExhaustedError(provider, f"HTTP 402: {detail}")
    if status in (401, 403):
        raise ProviderAuthError(provider, "authentication failed - check the API key")
    if status in (404, 408) or status >= 500:
        raise ProviderUnavailableError(provider, f"HTTP {status}: {detail}")
    raise ProviderError(provider, f"HTTP {status}: {detail}")


async def call_with_retries(provider: str, operation: Callable[[], Awaitable[T]], *, max_attempts: int = 3,
                            base_delay: float = 1.0, max_inline_wait: float = 8.0) -> T:
    """Retries network errors and 5xx; waits inline for short rate limits; everything else propagates."""
    for attempt in range(1, max_attempts + 1):
        try:
            return await operation()
        except RateLimitedError as exc:
            wait = exc.retry_after_seconds
            if wait is None or wait > max_inline_wait or attempt == max_attempts:
                raise
            await asyncio.sleep(wait)
            continue
        except ProviderAuthError:
            raise
        except ProviderUnavailableError:
            if attempt == max_attempts:
                raise
        except (httpx.TimeoutException, httpx.TransportError) as exc:
            if attempt == max_attempts:
                raise ProviderUnavailableError(provider, f"network error: {type(exc).__name__}") from exc
        await asyncio.sleep(base_delay * 2 ** (attempt - 1) + random.uniform(0, base_delay / 2))
    raise ProviderUnavailableError(provider, "retries exhausted")   # unreachable, keeps type checkers happy
ai-services/app/providers/openai_compatible.py
python
"""Adapter for OpenAI-compatible chat APIs: Groq and OpenRouter (free models only)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import httpx

from app.providers.base import (
    Capability,
    ChatProvider,
    ChatRequest,
    ChatResponse,
    ProviderError,
    ProviderUnavailableError,
    RateLimitedError,
    TokenUsage,
)
from app.providers.http import call_with_retries, raise_for_provider_status
from app.providers.quota import QuotaGuard
from app.providers.usage import record_usage


class OpenAICompatibleChatProvider(ChatProvider):
    def __init__(self, *, name: str, base_url: str, api_key: str, fast_model: str, strong_model: str,
                 capabilities: frozenset[Capability], http: httpx.AsyncClient, quota: QuotaGuard,
                 extra_headers: dict[str, str] | None = None, retry_base_delay: float = 1.0) -> None:
        self.name = name
        self.is_free_tier = True          # only free-tier providers are ever constructed (see registry)
        self.capabilities = capabilities
        self._base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self._fast = fast_model or strong_model
        self._strong = strong_model or fast_model
        self._http = http
        self._quota = quota
        self._retry_base_delay = retry_base_delay
        self._headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}", **(extra_headers or {})}

    def model_for(self, tier: str) -> str:
        return self._strong if tier == "strong" else self._fast

    async def chat(self, request: ChatRequest) -> ChatResponse:
        model = self.model_for(request.tier)
        if not model:
            raise ProviderUnavailableError(self.name, f"no model configured for tier '{request.tier}'")

        payload: dict[str, object] = {
            "model": model,
            "messages": [{"role": m.role, "content": m.content} for m in request.messages],
            "max_tokens": request.max_output_tokens,
            "temperature": request.temperature,
        }
        if request.json_schema is not None and Capability.STRUCTURED_OUTPUT in self.capabilities:
            payload["response_format"] = {"type": "json_object"}

        async def send() -> dict:
            async with self._quota.concurrency(self.name):
                await self._quota.acquire(self.name)
                response = await self._http.post(f"{self._base_url}/chat/completions", json=payload,
                                                 headers=self._headers)
            raise_for_provider_status(self.name, response)
            return response.json()

        try:
            data = await call_with_retries(self.name, send, base_delay=self._retry_base_delay)
            if isinstance(data.get("error"), dict):            # some gateways return errors with HTTP 200
                raise ProviderUnavailableError(self.name, f"provider error: {str(data['error'])[:200]}")
            choices = data.get("choices") or []
            if not choices:
                raise ProviderUnavailableError(self.name, "empty response (no choices)")
        except ProviderError as exc:
            limited = isinstance(exc, RateLimitedError)
            await self._quota.record(self.name, model, success=False, rate_limited=limited)
            record_usage(self.name, model, "chat", success=False, rate_limited=limited)
            raise

        text = (choices[0].get("message") or {}).get("content") or ""
        usage_data = data.get("usage") or {}
        usage = TokenUsage(int(usage_data.get("prompt_tokens", 0) or 0), int(usage_data.get("completion_tokens", 0) or 0))
        served_model = str(data.get("model") or model)

        await self._quota.record(self.name, served_model, input_tokens=usage.input_tokens, output_tokens=usage.output_tokens)
        record_usage(self.name, served_model, "chat", input_tokens=usage.input_tokens, output_tokens=usage.output_tokens)
        return ChatResponse(text=text, provider=self.name, model=served_model, usage=usage)
ai-services/app/providers/gemini.py
python
"""Google Gemini embeddings (free tier) via the REST batchEmbedContents endpoint."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import httpx

from app.core.tokens import estimate_tokens
from app.providers.base import (
    EmbeddingProvider,
    EmbeddingRequest,
    EmbeddingResponse,
    ProviderError,
    ProviderUnavailableError,
    RateLimitedError,
    TokenUsage,
)
from app.providers.http import call_with_retries, raise_for_provider_status
from app.providers.quota import QuotaGuard
from app.providers.usage import record_usage

GEMINI_BASE_URL = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta"
MAX_CHARS_PER_TEXT = 8000          # stays below the model's per-input token limit


def _l2_normalize(values: list[float]) -> list[float]:
    # Google recommends normalizing when outputDimensionality < 3072 (only full-size output is pre-normalized).
    norm = math.sqrt(sum(v * v for v in values))
    return [v / norm for v in values] if norm else values


class GeminiEmbeddingProvider(EmbeddingProvider):
    name = "gemini"
    is_free_tier = True

    def __init__(self, *, api_key: str, model: str, dimensions: int, http: httpx.AsyncClient, quota: QuotaGuard,
                 batch_size: int = 100, tpm_limit: int | None = None, retry_base_delay: float = 1.0) -> None:
        self.model = model
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self._api_key = api_key
        self._http = http
        self._quota = quota
        self._batch_size = max(1, min(batch_size, 100))
        self._token_budget = int(tpm_limit * 0.8) if tpm_limit else None    # headroom for estimation error
        self._retry_base_delay = retry_base_delay

    async def embed(self, request: EmbeddingRequest) -> EmbeddingResponse:
        texts = [t[:MAX_CHARS_PER_TEXT] for t in request.texts]
        vectors: list[list[float]] = []
        total_tokens = 0
        for batch in self._batches(texts):
            tokens = sum(estimate_tokens(t) for t in batch)
            vectors.extend(await self._embed_batch(batch, request.task, tokens))
            total_tokens += tokens
        return EmbeddingResponse(vectors=vectors, model=self.model, dimensions=self.dimensions,
                                 usage=TokenUsage(input_tokens=total_tokens))

    def _batches(self, texts: list[str]) -> list[list[str]]:
        batches: list[list[str]] = []
        current: list[str] = []
        current_tokens = 0
        for text in texts:
            tokens = estimate_tokens(text)
            over_budget = self._token_budget is not None and current_tokens + tokens > self._token_budget
            if current and (len(current) >= self._batch_size or over_budget):
                batches.append(current)
                current, current_tokens = [], 0
            current.append(text)
            current_tokens += tokens
        if current:
            batches.append(current)
        return batches

    async def _embed_batch(self, batch: list[str], task: str, tokens: int) -> list[list[float]]:
        task_type = "RETRIEVAL_QUERY" if task == "query" else "RETRIEVAL_DOCUMENT"
        payload = {"requests": [{
            "model": f"models/{self.model}",
            "content": {"parts": [{"text": text}]},
            "taskType": task_type,
            "outputDimensionality": self.dimensions,
        } for text in batch]}
        url = f"{GEMINI_BASE_URL}/models/{self.model}:batchEmbedContents"

        async def send() -> dict:
            async with self._quota.concurrency(self.name):
                await self._quota.acquire(self.name, tokens=tokens)
                response = await self._http.post(url, json=payload, headers={"x-goog-api-key": self._api_key})
            raise_for_provider_status(self.name, response)
            return response.json()

        try:
            data = await call_with_retries(self.name, send, base_delay=self._retry_base_delay)
        except ProviderError as exc:
            limited = isinstance(exc, RateLimitedError)
            await self._quota.record(self.name, self.model, success=False, rate_limited=limited)
            record_usage(self.name, self.model, "embed", success=False, rate_limited=limited)
            raise

        embeddings = data.get("embeddings") or []
        if len(embeddings) != len(batch):
            raise ProviderUnavailableError(self.name, f"expected {len(batch)} embeddings, got {len(embeddings)}")

        vectors: list[list[float]] = []
        for item in embeddings:
            values = [float(v) for v in (item.get("values") or [])]
            if len(values) != self.dimensions:
                raise ProviderError(self.name, f"expected {self.dimensions} dimensions, got {len(values)}")
            vectors.append(_l2_normalize(values))

        await self._quota.record(self.name, self.model, input_tokens=tokens)
        record_usage(self.name, self.model, "embed", embedding_tokens=tokens)
        return vectors
ai-services/app/providers/jina.py
python
"""Reranking: Jina (one-time free token grant) or a no-op fallback that keeps the RRF order."""

from __future__ import annotations

import httpx

from app.core.tokens import estimate_tokens
from app.providers.base import ProviderError, RateLimitedError, RerankProvider, RerankRequest, RerankResult
from app.providers.http import call_with_retries, raise_for_provider_status
from app.providers.quota import QuotaGuard
from app.providers.usage import record_usage

JINA_RERANK_URL = "https://api.jina.ai/v1/rerank"


class JinaReranker(RerankProvider):
    name = "jina"
    is_free_tier = True

    def __init__(self, *, api_key: str, model: str, http: httpx.AsyncClient, quota: QuotaGuard,
                 retry_base_delay: float = 1.0) -> None:
        self._api_key = api_key
        self._model = model
        self._http = http
        self._quota = quota
        self._retry_base_delay = retry_base_delay

    async def rerank(self, request: RerankRequest) -> list[RerankResult]:
        if not request.documents:
            return []
        top_n = min(request.top_n, len(request.documents))
        tokens = estimate_tokens(request.query) * len(request.documents) + sum(estimate_tokens(d) for d in request.documents)
        payload = {"model": self._model, "query": request.query, "documents": list(request.documents), "top_n": top_n}

        async def send() -> dict:
            async with self._quota.concurrency(self.name):
                await self._quota.acquire(self.name, tokens=tokens)
                response = await self._http.post(JINA_RERANK_URL, json=payload,
                                                 headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {self._api_key}"})
            raise_for_provider_status(self.name, response)
            return response.json()

        try:
            data = await call_with_retries(self.name, send, base_delay=self._retry_base_delay)
        except ProviderError as exc:
            record_usage(self.name, self._model, "rerank", success=False, rate_limited=isinstance(exc, RateLimitedError))
            raise

        record_usage(self.name, self._model, "rerank", input_tokens=int((data.get("usage") or {}).get("total_tokens", tokens)))
        results = [RerankResult(index=int(r["index"]), score=float(r["relevance_score"])) for r in data.get("results", [])]
        return sorted(results, key=lambda r: r.score, reverse=True)[:top_n]


class NoopReranker(RerankProvider):
    """Transparent fallback: keeps the incoming (RRF) order. Lower quality, zero quota."""

    name = "none"
    is_free_tier = True

    async def rerank(self, request: RerankRequest) -> list[RerankResult]:
        count = min(request.top_n, len(request.documents))
        return [RerankResult(index=i, score=1.0 / (i + 1)) for i in range(count)]
ai-services/app/providers/structured.py
python
"""Structured output on top of the chat router: JSON mode where supported, prompt-only elsewhere, one repair attempt."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from dataclasses import replace
from typing import TypeVar

from pydantic import BaseModel, ValidationError

from app.providers.base import ChatMessage, ChatRequest
from app.providers.router import ChatRouter

T = TypeVar("T", bound=BaseModel)
_FENCE = re.compile(r"^```(?:json)?\s*|\s*```$", re.IGNORECASE)


class StructuredOutputError(Exception):
    pass


def extract_json(text: str) -> str:
    cleaned = _FENCE.sub("", text.strip())
    start, end = cleaned.find("{"), cleaned.rfind("}")
    if start == -1 or end <= start:
        raise ValueError("no JSON object found in the response")
    return cleaned[start:end + 1]


class JsonStructuredOutputService:
    def __init__(self, router: ChatRouter) -> None:
        self._router = router

    async def generate(self, request: ChatRequest, schema: type[T]) -> T:
        json_schema = schema.model_json_schema()
        instruction = ChatMessage("system", "Respond with a single JSON object only - no prose, no markdown. "
                                            f"It must validate against this JSON schema:\n{json.dumps(json_schema)}")
        prepared = replace(request, messages=(instruction, *request.messages), json_schema=json_schema)

        response = await self._router.chat(prepared)
        try:
            return schema.model_validate_json(extract_json(response.text))
        except (ValidationError, ValueError) as first_error:
            repair = replace(prepared, messages=(
                *prepared.messages,
                ChatMessage("assistant", response.text[:4000]),
                ChatMessage("user", f"That was not valid JSON for the schema ({str(first_error)[:400]}). "
                                    "Return only the corrected JSON object."),
            ))
            second = await self._router.chat(repair)
            try:
                return schema.model_validate_json(extract_json(second.text))
            except (ValidationError, ValueError) as exc:
                raise StructuredOutputError(f"Model did not return valid JSON after one repair: {exc}") from exc
ai-services/app/providers/router.py (replace)
python
"""
Fallback router across FREE chat providers.
  * Refuses providers not marked is_free_tier (no silent paid fallback).
  * Skips providers lacking required capabilities.
  * Rate-limited / exhausted / unavailable providers get a cooldown.
  * Other provider errors (e.g. a request one provider rejects) fall through to the next provider.
  * Non-provider exceptions (bugs) propagate.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import time
from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence

from app.providers.base import (
    AllProvidersUnavailableError,
    ChatProvider,
    ChatRequest,
    ChatResponse,
    ProviderError,
    ProviderUnavailableError,
    QuotaExhaustedError,
    RateLimitedError,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ChatRouter:
    def __init__(self, providers: Sequence[ChatProvider], *, rate_limit_cooldown_seconds: float = 20.0,
                 unavailable_cooldown_seconds: float = 30.0, quota_cooldown_seconds: float = 3600.0,
                 clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic) -> None:
        if not providers:
            raise ValueError("At least one chat provider must be configured.")
        for provider in providers:
            if not provider.is_free_tier:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Provider '{provider.name}' is not marked free-tier. NexusRAG refuses to route to paid providers.")
        self._providers = list(providers)
        self._rate_limit_cooldown = rate_limit_cooldown_seconds
        self._unavailable_cooldown = unavailable_cooldown_seconds
        self._quota_cooldown = quota_cooldown_seconds
        self._clock = clock
        self._cooldown_until: dict[str, float] = {}

    @property
    def provider_names(self) -> list[str]:
        return [p.name for p in self._providers]

    def is_available(self, provider_name: str) -> bool:
        return self._clock() >= self._cooldown_until.get(provider_name, 0.0)

    def _cool_down(self, provider_name: str, seconds: float) -> None:
        self._cooldown_until[provider_name] = self._clock() + seconds

    async def chat(self, request: ChatRequest, *, exclude: frozenset[str] = frozenset()) -> ChatResponse:
        """`exclude` lets the verifier force a different provider than the generator (Phase 11)."""
        attempts: list[str] = []
        for provider in self._providers:
            if provider.name in exclude:
                continue
            if not request.required_capabilities <= provider.capabilities:
                attempts.append(f"{provider.name}: missing capabilities")
                continue
            if not self.is_available(provider.name):
                attempts.append(f"{provider.name}: cooling down")
                continue
            try:
                return await provider.chat(request)
            except RateLimitedError as exc:
                self._cool_down(provider.name, exc.retry_after_seconds or self._rate_limit_cooldown)
                attempts.append(f"{provider.name}: rate limited")
            except QuotaExhaustedError:
                self._cool_down(provider.name, self._quota_cooldown)
                attempts.append(f"{provider.name}: free quota exhausted")
            except ProviderUnavailableError as exc:
                self._cool_down(provider.name, self._unavailable_cooldown)
                attempts.append(f"{provider.name}: unavailable ({exc})")
            except ProviderError as exc:
                attempts.append(f"{provider.name}: rejected ({exc})")
            logger.warning("Chat provider %s failed: %s", provider.name, attempts[-1])
        raise AllProvidersUnavailableError(attempts)
ai-services/app/providers/registry.py
python
"""Builds every provider from configuration. Only free-tier providers exist here."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import httpx

from app.core.config import Settings, is_set
from app.providers.base import Capability, EmbeddingProvider, RerankProvider
from app.providers.gemini import GeminiEmbeddingProvider
from app.providers.jina import JinaReranker, NoopReranker
from app.providers.openai_compatible import OpenAICompatibleChatProvider
from app.providers.quota import CounterStore, QuotaGuard, QuotaLimits
from app.providers.router import ChatRouter
from app.providers.structured import JsonStructuredOutputService


@dataclass
class ProviderBundle:
    chat: ChatRouter | None
    structured: JsonStructuredOutputService | None
    embedder: EmbeddingProvider | None
    reranker: RerankProvider
    quota: QuotaGuard
    http: httpx.AsyncClient

    async def aclose(self) -> None:
        await self.http.aclose()


def build_providers(settings: Settings, counter_store: CounterStore) -> ProviderBundle:
    http = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(settings.provider_timeout_seconds, connect=10.0),
                             limits=httpx.Limits(max_connections=20))
    quota = QuotaGuard(counter_store, {
        "groq": QuotaLimits(rpm=settings.groq_rpm, rpd=settings.groq_rpd, max_concurrency=settings.groq_max_concurrency),
        "openrouter": QuotaLimits(rpm=settings.openrouter_rpm, rpd=settings.openrouter_rpd,
                                  max_concurrency=settings.openrouter_max_concurrency),
        "gemini": QuotaLimits(rpm=settings.gemini_embed_rpm, rpd=settings.gemini_embed_rpd,
                              tpm=settings.gemini_embed_tpm, max_concurrency=2),
        "jina": QuotaLimits(rpm=settings.jina_rpm, tpm=settings.jina_tpm, max_concurrency=2),
    }, warning_ratio=settings.quota_warning_ratio)

    chat_providers = []
    for name in settings.configured_chat_providers():
        if name == "groq":
            chat_providers.append(OpenAICompatibleChatProvider(
                name="groq", base_url=settings.groq_base_url, api_key=settings.groq_api_key.get_secret_value(),
                fast_model=settings.groq_fast_model, strong_model=settings.groq_strong_model,
                capabilities=frozenset({Capability.CHAT, Capability.STRUCTURED_OUTPUT, Capability.TOOL_CALLING}),
                http=http, quota=quota))
        elif name == "openrouter":
            # Free OpenRouter models differ in JSON-mode support, so structured output is prompt-only there.
            chat_providers.append(OpenAICompatibleChatProvider(
                name="openrouter", base_url=settings.openrouter_base_url,
                api_key=settings.openrouter_api_key.get_secret_value(),
                fast_model=settings.openrouter_fast_model, strong_model=settings.openrouter_strong_model,
                capabilities=frozenset({Capability.CHAT}), http=http, quota=quota,
                extra_headers={"X-Title": "NexusRAG"}))

    chat = ChatRouter(chat_providers) if chat_providers else None

    embedder = None
    if settings.embedding_provider == "gemini" and is_set(settings.gemini_api_key):
        embedder = GeminiEmbeddingProvider(
            api_key=settings.gemini_api_key.get_secret_value(), model=settings.embedding_model,
            dimensions=settings.embedding_dimensions, http=http, quota=quota,
            batch_size=settings.embed_batch_size, tpm_limit=settings.gemini_embed_tpm)

    reranker: RerankProvider = NoopReranker()
    if settings.rerank_provider == "jina" and is_set(settings.jina_api_key):
        reranker = JinaReranker(api_key=settings.jina_api_key.get_secret_value(),
                                model=settings.jina_rerank_model, http=http, quota=quota)

    return ProviderBundle(chat=chat, structured=JsonStructuredOutputService(chat) if chat else None,
                          embedder=embedder, reranker=reranker, quota=quota, http=http)
Vector indexing (Phase 9)
ai-services/app/retrieval/sparse.py
python
"""
Deterministic sparse (keyword) vectors - no model involved.
Term frequency is computed here; Qdrant's IDF modifier adds inverse document frequency,
which gives BM25-style keyword scoring for hybrid search.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import math
import re
from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass

_TOKEN = re.compile(r"\w[\w\-.%]*", re.UNICODE)
STOPWORDS = frozenset(
    "a an and are as at be by for from has have in is it its of on or that the to was were will with this "
    "these those not no but if then than so such into over under about can may also which who whom".split())


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SparseVector:
    indices: list[int]
    values: list[float]


def tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:
    tokens = (t.strip(".-").lower() for t in _TOKEN.findall(text))
    return [t for t in tokens if t and t not in STOPWORDS and len(t) <= 40]


def _index(token: str) -> int:
    return int.from_bytes(hashlib.blake2b(token.encode("utf-8"), digest_size=4).digest(), "big") & 0x7FFFFFFF


def encode_document(text: str) -> SparseVector:
    counts = Counter(_index(t) for t in tokenize(text)) or Counter({_index("__empty__"): 1})
    indices = sorted(counts)
    return SparseVector(indices=indices, values=[1.0 + math.log(counts[i]) for i in indices])


def encode_query(text: str) -> SparseVector:
    indices = sorted({_index(t) for t in tokenize(text)}) or [_index("__empty__")]
    return SparseVector(indices=indices, values=[1.0] * len(indices))
ai-services/app/retrieval/vector_store.py
python
"""Qdrant collection management, indexing and deletion (multi-tenant via payload partitioning)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import re
from uuid import UUID

from qdrant_client import AsyncQdrantClient, models
from qdrant_client.http.exceptions import UnexpectedResponse

from app.ingestion.models import Chunk
from app.messaging.contracts import IngestRequested
from app.retrieval.sparse import encode_document

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

DENSE = "dense"
SPARSE = "sparse"
PUBLIC_ACCESS_GROUP = "__kb__"     # document has no group restriction: everyone with knowledge-base access
UPSERT_BATCH = 64


def collection_name(prefix: str, embedding_model: str, dimensions: int) -> str:
    slug = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "-", embedding_model.lower()).strip("-")
    return f"{prefix}__{slug}__{dimensions}"


class VectorStore:
    def __init__(self, client: AsyncQdrantClient, *, embedding_model: str, dimensions: int, prefix: str = "chunks") -> None:
        self._client = client
        self.embedding_model = embedding_model
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.collection = collection_name(prefix, embedding_model, dimensions)

    async def ensure_collection(self) -> None:
        if await self._client.collection_exists(self.collection):
            return
        try:
            await self._client.create_collection(
                collection_name=self.collection,
                vectors_config={DENSE: models.VectorParams(size=self.dimensions, distance=models.Distance.COSINE,
                                                           on_disk=True)},
                sparse_vectors_config={SPARSE: models.SparseVectorParams(modifier=models.Modifier.IDF)},
                # Multitenancy: no global HNSW graph, per-tenant graphs instead (every query filters by tenant).
                hnsw_config=models.HnswConfigDiff(payload_m=16, m=0),
                on_disk_payload=True,
            )
        except UnexpectedResponse as exc:
            if await self._client.collection_exists(self.collection):   # created concurrently by another worker
                return
            raise exc

        await self._client.create_payload_index(
            self.collection, "tenant_id",
            field_schema=models.KeywordIndexParams(type=models.KeywordIndexType.KEYWORD, is_tenant=True))
        for field in ("knowledge_base_id", "document_id", "access_groups", "chunk_type", "ingestion_job_id"):
            await self._client.create_payload_index(self.collection, field, field_schema=models.PayloadSchemaType.KEYWORD)
        await self._client.create_payload_index(self.collection, "document_version",
                                                field_schema=models.PayloadSchemaType.INTEGER)
        logger.info("Created Qdrant collection %s", self.collection)

    async def upsert_document(self, msg: IngestRequested, chunks: list[Chunk], vectors: dict[str, list[float]]) -> None:
        access_groups = msg.access_groups or [PUBLIC_ACCESS_GROUP]
        points: list[models.PointStruct] = []
        for chunk in chunks:
            sparse = encode_document(chunk.content if chunk.chunk_type == "parent" else chunk.embed_text)
            vector: dict[str, object] = {SPARSE: models.SparseVector(indices=sparse.indices, values=sparse.values)}
            if chunk.chunk_id in vectors:
                vector[DENSE] = vectors[chunk.chunk_id]
            points.append(models.PointStruct(id=chunk.chunk_id, vector=vector, payload={
                "chunk_id": chunk.chunk_id,
                "tenant_id": str(msg.tenant_id),
                "knowledge_base_id": str(msg.knowledge_base_id),
                "document_id": str(msg.document_id),
                "document_version": msg.document_version,
                "ingestion_job_id": str(msg.job_id),
                "file_name": msg.file_name,
                "page_number": chunk.page_number,
                "section": chunk.section,
                "content": chunk.content,
                "chunk_type": chunk.chunk_type,
                "chunk_index": chunk.chunk_index,
                "parent_id": chunk.parent_id,
                "access_groups": access_groups,
                "token_estimate": chunk.token_estimate,
                "embedding_model": self.embedding_model,
            }))

        for start in range(0, len(points), UPSERT_BATCH):
            await self._client.upsert(self.collection, points=points[start:start + UPSERT_BATCH], wait=True)

        await self._delete_stale(msg)

    async def _delete_stale(self, msg: IngestRequested) -> None:
        """Removes older versions AND leftovers from earlier runs of this version (e.g. changed chunking)."""
        stale = models.Filter(
            must=self._document_conditions(msg.tenant_id, msg.document_id),
            should=[
                models.FieldCondition(key="document_version", range=models.Range(lt=msg.document_version)),
                models.Filter(
                    must=[models.FieldCondition(key="document_version",
                                                match=models.MatchValue(value=msg.document_version))],
                    must_not=[models.FieldCondition(key="ingestion_job_id",
                                                    match=models.MatchValue(value=str(msg.job_id)))]),
            ],
        )
        await self._client.delete(self.collection, points_selector=models.FilterSelector(filter=stale), wait=True)

    async def delete_document(self, tenant_id: UUID, document_id: UUID, only_versions_below: int | None = None) -> None:
        must: list[models.Condition] = list(self._document_conditions(tenant_id, document_id))
        if only_versions_below is not None:
            must.append(models.FieldCondition(key="document_version", range=models.Range(lt=only_versions_below)))
        await self._client.delete(self.collection, points_selector=models.FilterSelector(filter=models.Filter(must=must)),
                                  wait=True)
        logger.info("Deleted vectors for document %s (versions below %s)", document_id, only_versions_below or "all")

    @staticmethod
    def _document_conditions(tenant_id: UUID, document_id: UUID) -> list[models.FieldCondition]:
        return [
            models.FieldCondition(key="tenant_id", match=models.MatchValue(value=str(tenant_id))),
            models.FieldCondition(key="document_id", match=models.MatchValue(value=str(document_id))),
        ]
Pipeline and worker
ai-services/app/ingestion/storage.py
python
import asyncio

from botocore.exceptions import ClientError

from app.core.errors import PermanentIngestionError, TransientIngestionError


class S3ObjectReader:
    def __init__(self, s3_client, bucket: str) -> None:
        self._s3 = s3_client
        self._bucket = bucket

    async def read(self, key: str) -> bytes:
        def _get() -> bytes:
            response = self._s3.get_object(Bucket=self._bucket, Key=key)
            return response["Body"].read()

        try:
            return await asyncio.to_thread(_get)
        except ClientError as exc:
            code = exc.response.get("Error", {}).get("Code", "")
            if code in ("NoSuchKey", "404", "NotFound"):
                raise PermanentIngestionError(f"Stored file not found: {key}") from exc
            raise TransientIngestionError(f"Object storage error: {code}") from exc
ai-services/app/ingestion/pipeline.py
python
"""download -> verify -> parse -> chunk -> embed -> index. Reports progress through a StatusReporter."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import hashlib
import logging
from pathlib import PurePosixPath
from typing import Protocol
from uuid import UUID

from app.core.errors import PermanentIngestionError, TransientIngestionError
from app.ingestion.chunking import Chunker
from app.ingestion.models import Chunk
from app.ingestion.parsers import parse_document
from app.messaging.contracts import DeleteRequested, IngestRequested
from app.providers.base import (
    EmbeddingProvider,
    EmbeddingRequest,
    EmbeddingResponse,
    ProviderUnavailableError,
    QuotaExhaustedError,
    RateLimitedError,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class StatusReporter(Protocol):
    async def report(self, status: str, **kwargs) -> None: ...


class ObjectReader(Protocol):
    async def read(self, key: str) -> bytes: ...


class VectorWriter(Protocol):
    async def upsert_document(self, msg: IngestRequested, chunks: list[Chunk], vectors: dict[str, list[float]]) -> None: ...
    async def delete_document(self, tenant_id: UUID, document_id: UUID, only_versions_below: int | None = None) -> None: ...


class IngestionPipeline:
    def __init__(self, *, storage: ObjectReader, embedder: EmbeddingProvider | None, store: VectorWriter,
                 chunker: Chunker, min_chars_per_pdf_page: int = 20, embed_batch_size: int = 64) -> None:
        self._storage = storage
        self._embedder = embedder
        self._store = store
        self._chunker = chunker
        self._min_chars = min_chars_per_pdf_page
        self._batch_size = embed_batch_size

    @property
    def embedding_model(self) -> str | None:
        return self._embedder.model if self._embedder else None

    async def ingest(self, msg: IngestRequested, reporter: StatusReporter) -> int:
        if self._embedder is None:
            raise PermanentIngestionError("No embedding provider configured (set GEMINI_API_KEY).")

        await reporter.report("PARSING", progress=5)
        data = await self._storage.read(msg.object_key)
        if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != msg.content_hash.lower():
            raise PermanentIngestionError("Content hash mismatch: the stored file differs from the uploaded file.")

        extension = PurePosixPath(msg.file_name).suffix.lower()
        parsed = await asyncio.to_thread(parse_document, data, extension, self._min_chars)

        await reporter.report("CHUNKING", progress=20)
        seed = f"{msg.tenant_id}:{msg.document_id}:{msg.document_version}"
        chunks = await asyncio.to_thread(self._chunker.chunk, parsed, id_seed=seed, file_name=msg.file_name)
        embeddable = [c for c in chunks if c.chunk_type != "parent"]
        if not embeddable:
            raise PermanentIngestionError("The document produced no indexable content.")

        await reporter.report("EMBEDDING", progress=30)
        unique_texts = list(dict.fromkeys(c.embed_text for c in embeddable))      # embed repeated boilerplate once
        text_vectors: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
        for start in range(0, len(unique_texts), self._batch_size):
            batch = unique_texts[start:start + self._batch_size]
            response = await self._embed_with_wait(batch)
            text_vectors.update(zip(batch, response.vectors, strict=True))
            await reporter.report("EMBEDDING", progress=30 + int(55 * len(text_vectors) / len(unique_texts)))
        vectors = {c.chunk_id: text_vectors[c.embed_text] for c in embeddable}

        await reporter.report("INDEXING", progress=90)
        await self._store.upsert_document(msg, chunks, vectors)
        logger.info("Indexed document %s v%d: %d chunks (%d parents)", msg.document_id, msg.document_version,
                    len(embeddable), len(chunks) - len(embeddable))
        return len(embeddable)

    async def delete(self, msg: DeleteRequested) -> None:
        await self._store.delete_document(msg.tenant_id, msg.document_id, msg.only_versions_below)

    async def _embed_with_wait(self, texts: list[str]) -> EmbeddingResponse:
        """Backpressure: waits out per-minute limits; daily exhaustion becomes a delayed job retry."""
        for _ in range(6):
            try:
                return await self._embedder.embed(EmbeddingRequest(texts=tuple(texts), task="document"))
            except RateLimitedError as exc:
                await asyncio.sleep(min(exc.retry_after_seconds or 15.0, 60.0))
            except QuotaExhaustedError as exc:
                raise TransientIngestionError(f"Embedding free quota exhausted: {exc}") from exc
            except ProviderUnavailableError as exc:
                raise TransientIngestionError(f"Embedding provider unavailable: {exc}") from exc
        raise TransientIngestionError("Embedding provider kept rate-limiting; the job will be retried later.")
ai-services/app/ingestion/worker.py
python
"""RabbitMQ consumer: idempotent, bounded concurrency, delayed retries, dead-lettering, status reporting."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import contextlib
import json
import logging
import os
import socket

import aio_pika
from aio_pika.abc import AbstractChannel, AbstractExchange, AbstractIncomingMessage
from pydantic import ValidationError
from redis.asyncio import Redis

from app.core.config import Settings
from app.core.errors import PermanentIngestionError
from app.ingestion.pipeline import IngestionPipeline
from app.messaging.contracts import (
    DELETE_REQUESTED,
    INGEST_REQUESTED,
    DeleteRequested,
    IngestionStatusEvent,
    IngestRequested,
    UsageEntry,
)
from app.messaging.topology import REQUESTS_DLQ, REQUESTS_QUEUE, RETRY_QUEUES, STATUS_ROUTING_KEY, declare_topology
from app.providers.usage import UsageAccumulator, current_usage

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
DONE_TTL_SECONDS = 7 * 24 * 3600
LOCK_TTL_SECONDS = 30 * 60


class RabbitStatusReporter:
    def __init__(self, exchange: AbstractExchange, msg: IngestRequested) -> None:
        self._exchange = exchange
        self._msg = msg

    async def report(self, status: str, *, progress: int | None = None, chunk_count: int | None = None,
                     error: str | None = None, embedding_model: str | None = None,
                     usage: list[UsageEntry] | None = None) -> None:
        event = IngestionStatusEvent(
            tenant_id=self._msg.tenant_id, document_id=self._msg.document_id,
            document_version=self._msg.document_version, job_id=self._msg.job_id, status=status,
            progress=progress, chunk_count=chunk_count, embedding_model=embedding_model,
            error=error[:1000] if error else None, usage=usage or [])
        await self._exchange.publish(
            aio_pika.Message(body=event.model_dump_json(by_alias=True).encode(), content_type="application/json",
                             delivery_mode=aio_pika.DeliveryMode.PERSISTENT, message_id=str(event.message_id)),
            routing_key=STATUS_ROUTING_KEY)


class IngestionWorker:
    def __init__(self, *, settings: Settings, amqp_url: str, redis: Redis, pipeline: IngestionPipeline) -> None:
        self._settings = settings
        self._amqp_url = amqp_url
        self._redis = redis
        self._pipeline = pipeline
        self._worker_id = f"{socket.gethostname()}:{os.getpid()}"
        self._channel: AbstractChannel | None = None
        self._exchange: AbstractExchange | None = None
        self._inflight = 0
        self._idle = asyncio.Event()
        self._idle.set()

    async def run(self, stop: asyncio.Event) -> None:
        connection = await aio_pika.connect_robust(
            self._amqp_url, client_properties={"connection_name": f"nexusrag-ai-worker:{self._worker_id}"})
        try:
            channel = await connection.channel()
            await channel.set_qos(prefetch_count=self._settings.ingestion_concurrency)
            topology = await declare_topology(channel)
            self._channel, self._exchange = channel, topology.exchange

            consumer_tag = await topology.requests.consume(self._on_message)
            logger.info("Ingestion worker %s consuming %s (concurrency=%d)",
                        self._worker_id, REQUESTS_QUEUE, self._settings.ingestion_concurrency)

            await stop.wait()
            logger.info("Shutting down: no new messages, waiting for in-flight jobs")
            await topology.requests.cancel(consumer_tag)
            with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.TimeoutError):
                await asyncio.wait_for(self._idle.wait(), timeout=60)
        finally:
            await connection.close()

    async def _on_message(self, message: AbstractIncomingMessage) -> None:
        self._inflight += 1
        self._idle.clear()
        try:
            await self._handle(message)
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - never let one message kill the consumer
            logger.exception("Unhandled error while processing message %s", message.message_id)
        finally:
            self._inflight -= 1
            if self._inflight == 0:
                self._idle.set()

    async def _handle(self, message: AbstractIncomingMessage) -> None:
        try:
            envelope = json.loads(message.body)
            message_type = envelope.get("messageType")
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
            logger.error("Malformed message %s; dead-lettering", message.message_id)
            await message.reject(requeue=False)
            return

        attempt = int((message.headers or {}).get("x-attempt", 0) or 0)

        if message_type == DELETE_REQUESTED:
            await self._handle_delete(message, envelope, attempt)
        elif message_type == INGEST_REQUESTED:
            await self._handle_ingest(message, envelope, attempt)
        else:
            logger.error("Unknown message type %s; dead-lettering", message_type)
            await message.reject(requeue=False)

    async def _handle_delete(self, message: AbstractIncomingMessage, envelope: dict, attempt: int) -> None:
        try:
            msg = DeleteRequested.model_validate(envelope)
        except ValidationError:
            await message.reject(requeue=False)
            return
        try:
            await self._pipeline.delete(msg)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            logger.warning("Vector deletion failed for %s: %s", msg.document_id, exc)
            await self._retry_or_dead_letter(message, attempt, str(exc))
        await message.ack()

    async def _handle_ingest(self, message: AbstractIncomingMessage, envelope: dict, attempt: int) -> None:
        try:
            msg = IngestRequested.model_validate(envelope)
        except ValidationError as exc:
            logger.error("Invalid ingest message: %s", exc)
            await message.reject(requeue=False)
            return

        done_key, lock_key = f"nx:ingest:done:{msg.job_id}", f"nx:ingest:lock:{msg.job_id}"
        if await self._redis_safe(self._redis.exists(done_key), default=0):
            logger.info("Job %s already completed; skipping duplicate", msg.job_id)
            await message.ack()
            return
        locked = await self._redis_safe(self._redis.set(lock_key, self._worker_id, nx=True, ex=LOCK_TTL_SECONDS),
                                        default=True)
        if not locked:
            logger.info("Job %s is being processed elsewhere; re-checking in 60s", msg.job_id)
            await self._publish_to(RETRY_QUEUES[1][0], message, attempt)
            await message.ack()
            return

        reporter = RabbitStatusReporter(self._exchange, msg)
        usage = UsageAccumulator()
        token = current_usage.set(usage)
        try:
            await reporter.report("QUEUED")
            chunk_count = await self._pipeline.ingest(msg, reporter)
            await reporter.report("INDEXED", progress=100, chunk_count=chunk_count,
                                  embedding_model=self._pipeline.embedding_model, usage=usage.entries())
            await self._redis_safe(self._redis.set(done_key, "1", ex=DONE_TTL_SECONDS), default=None)
        except PermanentIngestionError as exc:
            logger.warning("Job %s failed permanently: %s", msg.job_id, exc)
            await reporter.report("FAILED", error=str(exc), usage=usage.entries())
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - transient or unexpected: bounded retries
            reason = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            if attempt + 1 < self._settings.ingestion_max_attempts:
                logger.warning("Job %s attempt %d failed (%s); scheduling retry", msg.job_id, attempt + 1, reason)
                await reporter.report("RETRYING", error=reason, usage=usage.entries())
                await self._publish_to(RETRY_QUEUES[min(attempt, len(RETRY_QUEUES) - 1)][0], message, attempt + 1)
            else:
                logger.error("Job %s exhausted retries (%s); dead-lettering", msg.job_id, reason)
                await reporter.report("FAILED", error=f"Retries exhausted. Last error: {reason}", usage=usage.entries())
                await self._publish_to(REQUESTS_DLQ, message, attempt, reason=reason)
        finally:
            current_usage.reset(token)
            await self._redis_safe(self._redis.delete(lock_key), default=None)
        await message.ack()

    async def _retry_or_dead_letter(self, message: AbstractIncomingMessage, attempt: int, reason: str) -> None:
        if attempt + 1 < self._settings.ingestion_max_attempts:
            await self._publish_to(RETRY_QUEUES[min(attempt, len(RETRY_QUEUES) - 1)][0], message, attempt + 1)
        else:
            await self._publish_to(REQUESTS_DLQ, message, attempt, reason=reason)

    async def _publish_to(self, queue: str, message: AbstractIncomingMessage, attempt: int, reason: str | None = None) -> None:
        headers = dict(message.headers or {})
        headers["x-attempt"] = attempt
        if reason:
            headers["x-last-error"] = reason[:500]
        await self._channel.default_exchange.publish(
            aio_pika.Message(body=message.body, content_type="application/json", headers=headers,
                             delivery_mode=aio_pika.DeliveryMode.PERSISTENT, message_id=message.message_id),
            routing_key=queue)

    @staticmethod
    async def _redis_safe(awaitable, *, default):
        """Idempotency helpers degrade gracefully if Redis is down (deterministic IDs still prevent duplicates)."""
        try:
            return await awaitable
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            logger.warning("Redis unavailable for idempotency check (%s)", type(exc).__name__)
            return default
ai-services/app/worker.py (entry point: python -m app.worker)
python
"""Ingestion worker process. Runs separately from the FastAPI service so heavy parsing never blocks the API."""

import asyncio
import contextlib
import logging
import signal

from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.infra import Infrastructure
from app.core.logging import configure_logging
from app.ingestion.chunking import Chunker, ChunkingConfig
from app.ingestion.pipeline import IngestionPipeline
from app.ingestion.storage import S3ObjectReader
from app.ingestion.worker import IngestionWorker
from app.providers.quota import RedisCounterStore
from app.providers.registry import build_providers
from app.retrieval.vector_store import VectorStore

logger = logging.getLogger("nexusrag.worker")


async def _ensure_collection_with_retry(store: VectorStore) -> None:
    delay = 2.0
    while True:
        try:
            await store.ensure_collection()
            return
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            logger.warning("Qdrant not ready (%s); retrying in %.0fs", type(exc).__name__, delay)
            await asyncio.sleep(delay)
            delay = min(delay * 2, 30)


async def main() -> None:
    settings = get_settings()
    configure_logging(settings.log_level)

    infra = Infrastructure(settings)
    providers = build_providers(settings, RedisCounterStore(infra.redis))
    if providers.embedder is None:
        logger.warning("No embedding provider configured - ingestion jobs will fail until GEMINI_API_KEY is set")

    store = VectorStore(infra.qdrant, embedding_model=settings.embedding_model,
                        dimensions=settings.embedding_dimensions, prefix=settings.qdrant_collection_prefix)
    await _ensure_collection_with_retry(store)

    pipeline = IngestionPipeline(
        storage=S3ObjectReader(infra.s3, settings.s3_bucket), embedder=providers.embedder, store=store,
        chunker=Chunker(ChunkingConfig.from_settings(settings)),
        min_chars_per_pdf_page=settings.min_chars_per_pdf_page, embed_batch_size=settings.embed_batch_size)
    worker = IngestionWorker(settings=settings, amqp_url=infra.amqp_url, redis=infra.redis, pipeline=pipeline)

    stop = asyncio.Event()
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):
        with contextlib.suppress(NotImplementedError):          # not available on Windows
            loop.add_signal_handler(sig, stop.set)

    try:
        await worker.run(stop)
    finally:
        await providers.aclose()
        await infra.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    with contextlib.suppress(KeyboardInterrupt):
        asyncio.run(main())

import json

from app.messaging.contracts import IngestionStatusEvent, IngestRequested

DOTNET_JSON = """{
  "messageId": "0193a1b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000001", "messageType": "document.ingest.requested",
  "schemaVersion": 1, "correlationId": "0193a1b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000002",
  "tenantId": "0193a1b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000003", "knowledgeBaseId": "0193a1b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000004",
  "documentId": "0193a1b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000005", "documentVersion": 2,
  "jobId": "0193a1b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000006", "objectKey": "tenants/x/documents/y/v2/original.pdf",
  "fileName": "leave.pdf", "contentType": "application/pdf", "contentHash": "abc",
  "accessGroups": ["hr-india"], "requestedAt": "2026-11-21T10:00:00+00:00"
}"""


def test_parses_dotnet_ingest_message():
    msg = IngestRequested.model_validate_json(DOTNET_JSON)
    assert msg.document_version == 2
    assert msg.access_groups == ["hr-india"]


def test_status_event_serializes_camel_case():
    msg = IngestRequested.model_validate_json(DOTNET_JSON)
    event = IngestionStatusEvent(tenant_id=msg.tenant_id, document_id=msg.document_id, document_version=2,
                                 job_id=msg.job_id, status="INDEXED", chunk_count=10)
    body = json.loads(event.model_dump_json(by_alias=True))
    assert body["jobId"] == str(msg.job_id)
    assert body["chunkCount"] == 10
    assert body["messageType"] == "document.ingestion.status"
ai-services/tests/test_parsers.py
python
import io

import pytest

from app.ingestion.parsers import ParseError, parse_document


def test_txt_paragraphs_and_heading():
    data = b"LEAVE POLICY\n\nEmployees receive 18 days.\nLeave accrues monthly.\n\nSick leave is 12 days."
    blocks = parse_document(data, ".txt").blocks
    assert [b.kind for b in blocks] == ["heading", "paragraph", "paragraph"]
    assert blocks[1].text == "Employees receive 18 days. Leave accrues monthly."


def test_markdown_headings_and_table():
    data = b"# Leave\n## India\nAnnual leave rules.\n\n| Type | Days |\n|---|---|\n| Annual | 18 |\n"
    blocks = parse_document(data, ".md").blocks
    assert [b.kind for b in blocks] == ["heading", "heading", "paragraph", "table"]
    assert blocks[3].rows == [["Type", "Days"], ["Annual", "18"]]


def test_csv_semicolon_dialect():
    blocks = parse_document(b"country;days\nIndia;18\nUS;15\n", ".csv").blocks
    assert blocks[0].rows == [["country", "days"], ["India", "18"], ["US", "15"]]


def test_docx_keeps_order_of_headings_paragraphs_tables():
    from docx import Document
    doc = Document()
    doc.add_heading("Leave Policy", level=1)
    doc.add_paragraph("Employees receive 18 days of annual leave.")
    table = doc.add_table(rows=2, cols=2)
    table.cell(0, 0).text, table.cell(0, 1).text = "Country", "Days"
    table.cell(1, 0).text, table.cell(1, 1).text = "India", "18"
    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    doc.save(buffer)

    blocks = parse_document(buffer.getvalue(), ".docx").blocks
    assert [b.kind for b in blocks] == ["heading", "paragraph", "table"]
    assert blocks[2].rows == [["Country", "Days"], ["India", "18"]]


def test_xlsx_sheet_becomes_table():
    from openpyxl import Workbook
    wb = Workbook()
    ws = wb.active
    ws.title = "Leave"
    ws.append(["Country", "Days"])
    ws.append(["India", 18])
    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    wb.save(buffer)

    blocks = parse_document(buffer.getvalue(), ".xlsx").blocks
    assert blocks[0].text == "Sheet: Leave"
    assert blocks[1].rows == [["Country", "Days"], ["India", "18"]]


def test_pptx_slide_title_and_body():
    from pptx import Presentation
    prs = Presentation()
    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(prs.slide_layouts[1])
    slide.shapes.title.text = "Q3 Results"
    slide.placeholders[1].text = "Revenue grew 12%"
    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    prs.save(buffer)

    blocks = parse_document(buffer.getvalue(), ".pptx").blocks
    assert blocks[0].kind == "heading" and blocks[0].text == "Q3 Results" and blocks[0].page == 1
    assert any("Revenue grew 12%" in b.text for b in blocks[1:])


def test_unsupported_and_empty_files_are_permanent_errors():
    with pytest.raises(ParseError):
        parse_document(b"x", ".exe")
    with pytest.raises(ParseError):
        parse_document(b"   \n\n  ", ".txt")
ai-services/tests/test_chunking.py
python
from app.core.tokens import estimate_tokens
from app.ingestion.chunking import Chunker, ChunkingConfig
from app.ingestion.models import Block, ParsedDocument


def _doc() -> ParsedDocument:
    long_text = " ".join(f"Sentence number {i} explains a detail of the leave policy." for i in range(120))
    return ParsedDocument(blocks=[
        Block("heading", "Leave Policy", level=1),
        Block("heading", "India", level=2),
        Block("paragraph", long_text, page=3),
        Block("heading", "United States", level=2),
        Block("paragraph", "Employees receive 15 days.", page=4),
        Block("table", "Type | Days", rows=[["Type", "Days"]] + [[f"T{i}", str(i)] for i in range(100)]),
    ])


def test_sections_track_heading_hierarchy():
    chunks = Chunker(ChunkingConfig(target_tokens=120)).chunk(_doc(), id_seed="t:d:1", file_name="policy.docx")
    sections = {c.section for c in chunks if c.chunk_type == "child"}
    assert "Leave Policy > India" in sections
    assert "Leave Policy > United States" in sections


def test_children_respect_target_size_and_get_parents():
    chunks = Chunker(ChunkingConfig(target_tokens=120, overlap_tokens=20)).chunk(_doc(), id_seed="t:d:1", file_name="p")
    children = [c for c in chunks if c.chunk_type == "child" and c.section == "Leave Policy > India"]
    assert len(children) > 1
    assert all(estimate_tokens(c.content) <= 120 + 30 for c in children)
    parent_ids = {c.parent_id for c in children}
    assert None not in parent_ids
    assert parent_ids <= {c.chunk_id for c in chunks if c.chunk_type == "parent"}


def test_tables_repeat_header_in_every_chunk():
    chunks = Chunker(ChunkingConfig(table_rows_per_chunk=30)).chunk(_doc(), id_seed="t:d:1", file_name="p")
    tables = [c for c in chunks if c.chunk_type == "table"]
    assert len(tables) == 4
    assert all(t.content.startswith("| Type | Days |") for t in tables)


def test_chunk_ids_are_deterministic():
    a = Chunker().chunk(_doc(), id_seed="t:d:1", file_name="p")
    b = Chunker().chunk(_doc(), id_seed="t:d:1", file_name="p")
    c = Chunker().chunk(_doc(), id_seed="t:d:2", file_name="p")
    assert [x.chunk_id for x in a] == [x.chunk_id for x in b]
    assert a[0].chunk_id != c[0].chunk_id


def test_embed_text_contains_context():
    chunk = next(c for c in Chunker().chunk(_doc(), id_seed="s", file_name="policy.docx") if c.chunk_type == "child")
    assert chunk.embed_text.startswith("Document: policy.docx\nSection: Leave Policy")
ai-services/tests/test_sparse.py
python
from app.retrieval.sparse import encode_document, encode_query, tokenize


def test_tokenize_removes_stopwords_and_keeps_numbers():
    assert tokenize("The carry-forward limit is 12.5 days for India.") == ["carry-forward", "limit", "12.5", "days", "india"]


def test_encoding_is_deterministic_sorted_and_unique():
    a = encode_document("annual leave annual leave sick")
    b = encode_document("annual leave annual leave sick")
    assert a == b
    assert a.indices == sorted(set(a.indices))
    assert max(a.values) > 1.0          # repeated terms weigh more


def test_query_uses_unit_weights():
    q = encode_query("annual annual leave")
    assert len(q.indices) == 2 and set(q.values) == {1.0}
ai-services/tests/test_quota.py
python
import asyncio

import pytest

from app.providers.base import QuotaExhaustedError, RateLimitedError
from app.providers.quota import InMemoryCounterStore, QuotaGuard, QuotaLimits


def test_rpm_limit_raises_and_rolls_back():
    store = InMemoryCounterStore()
    guard = QuotaGuard(store, {"p": QuotaLimits(rpm=2)}, clock=lambda: 1_000_000.0)

    async def scenario():
        await guard.acquire("p")
        await guard.acquire("p")
        with pytest.raises(RateLimitedError) as exc:
            await guard.acquire("p")
        return exc.value

    error = asyncio.run(scenario())
    assert error.retry_after_seconds is not None
    assert max(store.values.values()) == 2         # the rejected request was rolled back


def test_daily_limit_raises_quota_exhausted():
    guard = QuotaGuard(InMemoryCounterStore(), {"p": QuotaLimits(rpd=1)}, clock=lambda: 1_000_000.0)

    async def scenario():
        await guard.acquire("p")
        await guard.acquire("p")

    with pytest.raises(QuotaExhaustedError):
        asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_token_per_minute_limit():
    guard = QuotaGuard(InMemoryCounterStore(), {"p": QuotaLimits(tpm=1000)}, clock=lambda: 1_000_000.0)

    async def scenario():
        await guard.acquire("p", tokens=800)
        await guard.acquire("p", tokens=300)

    with pytest.raises(RateLimitedError):
        asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_unknown_provider_is_unlimited():
    asyncio.run(QuotaGuard(InMemoryCounterStore(), {}).acquire("anything", tokens=10**9))
ai-services/tests/test_providers.py
python
import asyncio
import json

import httpx
import pytest

from app.providers.base import (
    Capability, ChatMessage, ChatRequest, EmbeddingRequest, ProviderError, RateLimitedError, TokenUsage,
)
from app.providers.gemini import GeminiEmbeddingProvider
from app.providers.http import ProviderAuthError
from app.providers.openai_compatible import OpenAICompatibleChatProvider
from app.providers.quota import InMemoryCounterStore, QuotaGuard
from app.providers.usage import UsageAccumulator, current_usage

REQ = ChatRequest(messages=(ChatMessage("user", "hi"),))


def _chat(handler) -> OpenAICompatibleChatProvider:
    return OpenAICompatibleChatProvider(
        name="groq", base_url="https://api.test/v1", api_key="k", fast_model="fast-m", strong_model="strong-m",
        capabilities=frozenset({Capability.CHAT, Capability.STRUCTURED_OUTPUT}),
        http=httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)),
        quota=QuotaGuard(InMemoryCounterStore(), {}), retry_base_delay=0)


def _ok(model="fast-m"):
    return httpx.Response(200, json={"model": model, "choices": [{"message": {"content": "hello"}}],
                                     "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 5, "completion_tokens": 2}})


def test_chat_success_parses_text_and_usage():
    def handler(request):
        body = json.loads(request.content)
        assert body["model"] == "fast-m"
        assert request.headers["authorization"] == "Bearer k"
        return _ok()

    response = asyncio.run(_chat(handler).chat(REQ))
    assert response.text == "hello"
    assert response.usage == TokenUsage(5, 2)


def test_strong_tier_and_json_mode():
    def handler(request):
        body = json.loads(request.content)
        assert body["model"] == "strong-m"
        assert body["response_format"] == {"type": "json_object"}
        return _ok("strong-m")

    asyncio.run(_chat(handler).chat(ChatRequest(messages=REQ.messages, tier="strong", json_schema={})))


def test_429_maps_to_rate_limited_with_retry_after():
    handler = lambda request: httpx.Response(429, headers={"retry-after": "30"}, text="slow down")
    with pytest.raises(RateLimitedError) as exc:
        asyncio.run(_chat(handler).chat(REQ))
    assert exc.value.retry_after_seconds == 30


def test_401_is_not_retried():
    calls = []

    def handler(request):
        calls.append(1)
        return httpx.Response(401)

    with pytest.raises(ProviderAuthError):
        asyncio.run(_chat(handler).chat(REQ))
    assert len(calls) == 1


def test_503_is_retried_then_succeeds():
    responses = [httpx.Response(503), _ok()]
    response = asyncio.run(_chat(lambda request: responses.pop(0)).chat(REQ))
    assert response.text == "hello"


def test_usage_is_recorded_in_context():
    async def scenario():
        accumulator = UsageAccumulator()
        token = current_usage.set(accumulator)
        try:
            await _chat(lambda request: _ok()).chat(REQ)
        finally:
            current_usage.reset(token)
        return accumulator.entries()

    entries = asyncio.run(scenario())
    assert entries[0].provider == "groq" and entries[0].input_tokens == 5


def _gemini(handler, batch_size=2):
    return GeminiEmbeddingProvider(api_key="g", model="gemini-embedding-001", dimensions=3,
                                   http=httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)),
                                   quota=QuotaGuard(InMemoryCounterStore(), {}), batch_size=batch_size,
                                   retry_base_delay=0)


def test_gemini_batches_and_normalizes():
    sizes = []

    def handler(request):
        body = json.loads(request.content)
        sizes.append(len(body["requests"]))
        assert request.headers["x-goog-api-key"] == "g"
        assert body["requests"][0]["outputDimensionality"] == 3
        assert body["requests"][0]["taskType"] == "RETRIEVAL_DOCUMENT"
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"embeddings": [{"values": [3.0, 4.0, 0.0]} for _ in body["requests"]]})

    result = asyncio.run(_gemini(handler).embed(EmbeddingRequest(texts=("a", "b", "c"))))
    assert sizes == [2, 1]
    assert result.vectors[0] == pytest.approx([0.6, 0.8, 0.0])


def test_gemini_dimension_mismatch_is_an_error():
    handler = lambda request: httpx.Response(200, json={"embeddings": [{"values": [1.0, 2.0]}]})
    with pytest.raises(ProviderError):
        asyncio.run(_gemini(handler).embed(EmbeddingRequest(texts=("a",))))
ai-services/tests/test_pipeline.py
python
import asyncio
import hashlib
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from app.core.errors import PermanentIngestionError
from app.ingestion.chunking import Chunker
from app.ingestion.pipeline import IngestionPipeline
from app.messaging.contracts import INGEST_REQUESTED, IngestRequested
from app.providers.base import EmbeddingProvider, EmbeddingResponse, TokenUsage

DATA = b"LEAVE POLICY\n\nEmployees in India receive 18 days of annual leave.\n\nSick leave is 12 days."


class FakeStorage:
    def __init__(self, data: bytes) -> None:
        self.data = data

    async def read(self, key: str) -> bytes:
        return self.data


class FakeEmbedder(EmbeddingProvider):
    name, is_free_tier, model, dimensions = "fake", True, "fake-embed", 3

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0

    async def embed(self, request):
        self.calls += 1
        return EmbeddingResponse([[0.1, 0.2, 0.3] for _ in request.texts], self.model, 3, TokenUsage(len(request.texts)))


class FakeStore:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.upserts = []

    async def upsert_document(self, msg, chunks, vectors):
        self.upserts.append((chunks, vectors))

    async def delete_document(self, tenant_id, document_id, only_versions_below=None):
        pass


class Recorder:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.statuses = []

    async def report(self, status, **kwargs):
        self.statuses.append(status)


def _msg(data: bytes, content_hash: str | None = None) -> IngestRequested:
    return IngestRequested(
        message_id=uuid4(), message_type=INGEST_REQUESTED, schema_version=1, correlation_id=uuid4(),
        tenant_id=uuid4(), knowledge_base_id=uuid4(), document_id=uuid4(), document_version=1, job_id=uuid4(),
        object_key="k", file_name="policy.txt", content_type="text/plain",
        content_hash=content_hash or hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), requested_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc))


def _pipeline(store: FakeStore, embedder=None, data: bytes = DATA) -> IngestionPipeline:
    return IngestionPipeline(storage=FakeStorage(data), embedder=embedder or FakeEmbedder(), store=store,
                             chunker=Chunker())


def test_ingest_reports_progress_and_indexes_all_chunks():
    store, recorder = FakeStore(), Recorder()
    count = asyncio.run(_pipeline(store).ingest(_msg(DATA), recorder))

    assert count >= 1
    assert recorder.statuses[:3] == ["PARSING", "CHUNKING", "EMBEDDING"]
    assert recorder.statuses[-1] == "INDEXING"
    chunks, vectors = store.upserts[0]
    assert set(vectors) == {c.chunk_id for c in chunks if c.chunk_type != "parent"}


def test_hash_mismatch_is_permanent():
    with pytest.raises(PermanentIngestionError, match="hash mismatch"):
        asyncio.run(_pipeline(FakeStore()).ingest(_msg(DATA, content_hash="0" * 64), Recorder()))


def test_missing_embedder_is_permanent():
    pipeline = IngestionPipeline(storage=FakeStorage(DATA), embedder=None, store=FakeStore(), chunker=Chunker())
    with pytest.raises(PermanentIngestionError, match="GEMINI_API_KEY"):
        asyncio.run(pipeline.ingest(_msg(DATA), Recorder()))

from app.providers.base import ProviderError
