python
class FakeRetriever:
    def __init__(self, items: list[Evidence]) -> None:
        self.items = items
        self.calls: list[list[str]] = []

    async def retrieve(self, queries, scope, *, limit):
        self.calls.append(list(queries))
        return RetrievalResult(self.items[:limit], [])

    async def retrieve_per_query(self, queries, scope, *, limit_per_query):
        self.calls.append(list(queries))
        return [self.items[:limit_per_query] for _ in queries], []

    async def fetch_parents(self, parent_ids, scope):
        return {}


class ScriptedGenerator:
    def __init__(self, answers: list[str], provider: str = "groq") -> None:
        self.answers = answers
        self.provider = provider
        self.calls = 0
        self.feedback: list[str | None] = []
        self.guidance: list[str | None] = []

    async def generate(self, question, items, feedback=None, guidance=None):
        text = self.answers[min(self.calls, len(self.answers) - 1)]
        self.calls += 1
        self.feedback.append(feedback)
        self.guidance.append(guidance)
        return GenerationResult(text, self.provider, "gen-model")
ai-services/tests/test_agents.py
python
import asyncio

from app.agents.graph import AgentWorkflow
from app.caching.answer_cache import AnswerCache, InMemorySemanticIndex
from app.caching.store import InMemoryCacheStore
from app.core.budget import TokenBudget
from app.providers.base import EmbeddingProvider, EmbeddingResponse, TokenUsage
from app.providers.jina import NoopReranker
from app.rag.models import QueryRequest
from app.rag.query_understanding import QueryAnalyzer
from app.rag.service import RagService
from app.verification.engine import ReliabilityEngine
from tests.rag_fakes import KB, FakeRetriever, FakeVerifier, ScriptedGenerator, evidence, scope

EVIDENCE = [evidence("c1", "Employees in India receive 18 days of annual leave per year.")]
GOOD = "Employees in India receive 18 days of annual leave [1]."
SPEC = ("Compare India and US leave policies based on annual leave and sick leave.")


def _workflow(retriever, generator, verifier, cache=None) -> AgentWorkflow:
    service = RagService(analyzer=QueryAnalyzer(None), retriever=retriever, reranker=NoopReranker(),
                         generator=generator, engine=ReliabilityEngine(verifier))
    return AgentWorkflow(service, budget=TokenBudget(request_ceiling=8000, output_reserve=1200, max_context_tokens=6000),
                         answer_cache=cache)


def _versioned_request(question: str, version: int = 1) -> QueryRequest:
    s = scope().model_copy(update={"knowledge_base_versions": {str(KB): version}})
    return QueryRequest(question=question, scope=s)


class ConstantEmbedder(EmbeddingProvider):
    name, is_free_tier, model, dimensions = "fake", True, "fake", 3

    async def embed(self, request):
        return EmbeddingResponse([[1.0, 0.0, 0.0] for _ in request.texts], "fake", 3, TokenUsage())


def test_simple_question_takes_the_short_path():
    answer = asyncio.run(_workflow(FakeRetriever(EVIDENCE), ScriptedGenerator([GOOD]), FakeVerifier())
                         .answer(_versioned_request("How many days of annual leave in India?")))
    assert answer.workflow == "simple"
    assert [t.node for t in answer.trace] == ["understand", "supervisor", "retrieve", "respond", "verify"]
    assert answer.verification.status.value == "SUPPORTED"


def test_comparison_uses_research_and_analysis_agents():
    generator = ScriptedGenerator(["India grants 18 days of annual leave [1]. Sick leave is not in the documents."])
    answer = asyncio.run(_workflow(FakeRetriever(EVIDENCE), generator, FakeVerifier())
                         .answer(_versioned_request(SPEC)))
    nodes = [t.node for t in answer.trace]
    assert answer.workflow == "multi_agent"
    assert nodes[:4] == ["understand", "supervisor", "research", "analyze"]
    assert generator.guidance[0].startswith("Coverage plan")
    assert "US / sick leave: no matching evidence" in generator.guidance[0]


def test_correction_loop_is_bounded_in_the_graph():
    generator = ScriptedGenerator([GOOD])
    answer = asyncio.run(_workflow(FakeRetriever(EVIDENCE), generator, FakeVerifier(default="INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE"))
                         .answer(_versioned_request("How many days of annual leave in India?")))
    assert [t.node for t in answer.trace].count("correct") == 2
    assert generator.calls == 3
    assert answer.verification.abstained is True


def test_no_evidence_ends_without_generation():
    generator = ScriptedGenerator([GOOD])
    answer = asyncio.run(_workflow(FakeRetriever([]), generator, FakeVerifier())
                         .answer(_versioned_request("Anything?")))
    assert generator.calls == 0
    assert [t.node for t in answer.trace] == ["understand", "supervisor", "retrieve"]


def test_verified_answers_are_cached_and_invalidated_by_version():
    cache = AnswerCache(InMemoryCacheStore(), InMemorySemanticIndex(0.95), ConstantEmbedder(),
                        ttl_seconds=60, config_version="v1")
    generator = ScriptedGenerator([GOOD])
    workflow = _workflow(FakeRetriever(EVIDENCE), generator, FakeVerifier(), cache)
    question = "How many days of annual leave in India?"

    asyncio.run(workflow.answer(_versioned_request(question)))
    exact = asyncio.run(workflow.answer(_versioned_request("  how many DAYS of annual leave in india ")))
    semantic = asyncio.run(workflow.answer(_versioned_request("What is India's annual leave entitlement?")))
    after_update = asyncio.run(workflow.answer(_versioned_request(question, version=2)))

    assert exact.cache == "exact"
    assert semantic.cache == "semantic"
    assert after_update.cache is None
    assert generator.calls == 2            # first call + after the knowledge-base version changed


def test_unverified_answers_are_not_cached():
    cache = AnswerCache(InMemoryCacheStore(), None, None, ttl_seconds=60, config_version="v1")
    generator = ScriptedGenerator([GOOD])
    workflow = _workflow(FakeRetriever(EVIDENCE), generator, FakeVerifier(default="INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE"), cache)
    asyncio.run(workflow.answer(_versioned_request("Q?")))
    second = asyncio.run(workflow.answer(_versioned_request("Q?")))
    assert second.cache is None
ai-services/tests/test_cache.py
python
import asyncio

from app.caching.embedding_cache import CachingEmbedder
from app.caching.keys import current_tenant, scope_cacheable
from app.caching.llm_cache import CachingChatRouter
from app.caching.store import InMemoryCacheStore
from app.core.budget import TokenBudget
from app.providers.base import (ChatMessage, ChatRequest, ChatResponse, EmbeddingProvider, EmbeddingRequest,
                                EmbeddingResponse, TokenUsage)
from tests.rag_fakes import KB, scope


class CountingEmbedder(EmbeddingProvider):
    name, is_free_tier, model, dimensions = "fake", True, "fake", 2

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.texts: list[str] = []

    async def embed(self, request):
        self.texts.extend(request.texts)
        return EmbeddingResponse([[float(len(t)), 1.0] for t in request.texts], "fake", 2, TokenUsage(len(request.texts)))


class CountingRouter:
    provider_names = ["groq"]

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0

    async def chat(self, request, *, exclude=frozenset()):
        self.calls += 1
        return ChatResponse(text="ok", provider="groq", model="m", usage=TokenUsage(1, 1))


def test_embedding_cache_only_sends_misses_and_is_tenant_scoped():
    inner = CountingEmbedder()
    cached = CachingEmbedder(inner, InMemoryCacheStore(), ttl_seconds=60)

    async def scenario():
        token = current_tenant.set("tenant-a")
        await cached.embed(EmbeddingRequest(texts=("a", "bb"), task="query"))
        result = await cached.embed(EmbeddingRequest(texts=("a", "bb", "ccc"), task="query"))
        current_tenant.reset(token)
        token = current_tenant.set("tenant-b")
        await cached.embed(EmbeddingRequest(texts=("a",), task="query"))
        current_tenant.reset(token)
        return result

    result = asyncio.run(scenario())
    assert inner.texts == ["a", "bb", "ccc", "a"]          # tenant-b does not reuse tenant-a's entry
    assert [v[0] for v in result.vectors] == [1.0, 2.0, 3.0]


def test_llm_cache_caches_only_deterministic_calls():
    inner = CountingRouter()
    router = CachingChatRouter(inner, InMemoryCacheStore(), ttl_seconds=60, version="v1")
    deterministic = ChatRequest(messages=(ChatMessage("user", "verify"),), temperature=0.0)
    creative = ChatRequest(messages=(ChatMessage("user", "answer"),), temperature=0.1)

    async def scenario():
        for _ in range(2):
            await router.chat(deterministic)
            await router.chat(creative)

    asyncio.run(scenario())
    assert inner.calls == 3


def test_scope_without_versions_is_not_cacheable():
    s = scope()
    assert not scope_cacheable(s)
    assert scope_cacheable(s.model_copy(update={"knowledge_base_versions": {str(KB): 4}}))


def test_token_budget_shrinks_with_long_questions_but_has_a_floor():
    budget = TokenBudget(request_ceiling=8000, output_reserve=1200, max_context_tokens=6000)
    short = budget.context_tokens(system_prompt="s" * 400, question="short?")
    long = budget.context_tokens(system_prompt="s" * 400, question="q" * 20000)
    assert short == 6000
    assert long < short
    assert budget.context_tokens(system_prompt="s", question="q" * 100000) == 500

backend/NexusRAG.Application/Usage/UsageRecorder.cs
csharp
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Documents;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Operations;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Usage;

/// <summary>Persists per-request usage and the daily per-provider aggregate (free-quota analytics).</summary>
internal static class UsageRecorder
{
    public static async Task RecordAsync(IApplicationDbContext db, Guid tenantId, Guid? userId,
        IReadOnlyList<ProviderUsageEntry>? usage, DateTimeOffset now, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        if (usage is not { Count: > 0 }) return;
        var day = DateOnly.FromDateTime(now.UtcDateTime);

        foreach (var u in usage)
        {
            var provider = Trim(u.Provider, 50);
            var model = Trim(u.Model, 200);

            db.UsageRecords.Add(new UsageRecord
            {
                TenantId = tenantId,
                UserId = userId,
                Operation = Trim(u.Operation, 50),
                Provider = provider,
                Model = model,
                InputTokens = u.InputTokens,
                OutputTokens = u.OutputTokens,
                EmbeddingTokens = u.EmbeddingTokens,
            });

            var aggregate = db.ProviderUsages.Local.FirstOrDefault(p => p.Provider == provider && p.Model == model && p.Day == day)
                ?? await db.ProviderUsages.FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.Provider == provider && p.Model == model && p.Day == day, ct);
            if (aggregate is null)
            {
                aggregate = new ProviderUsage { Provider = provider, Model = model, Day = day };
                db.ProviderUsages.Add(aggregate);
            }

            aggregate.Requests += u.Requests;
            aggregate.Failures += u.Failures;
            aggregate.RateLimitedCount += u.RateLimited;
            aggregate.InputTokens += u.InputTokens + u.EmbeddingTokens;
            aggregate.OutputTokens += u.OutputTokens;
        }
    }

    private static string Trim(string value, int max) => value.Length <= max ? value : value[..max];
}

backend/NexusRAG.Application/Chat/ChatContracts.cs
csharp
using FluentValidation;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Chat;

public sealed record CreateSessionRequest(string? Title, Guid[]? KnowledgeBaseIds);

public sealed record SessionDto(
    Guid Id, string Title, Guid[] KnowledgeBaseIds, int MessageCount, DateTimeOffset CreatedAt, DateTimeOffset? UpdatedAt);

public sealed record ChatQueryRequest(Guid SessionId, string Question, int TopK = 8, bool Verify = true);

public sealed record ChatAnswerDto(
    Guid MessageId, Guid SessionId, string Answer, IReadOnlyList<AiCitation> Citations,
    AiVerificationReport? Verification, IReadOnlyList<string> Warnings, string Workflow, string QueryType,
    int LatencyMs, string? Cache, IReadOnlyList<AiTraceStep> Trace);

public sealed record CitationDto(
    int Ordinal, Guid DocumentId, int DocumentVersion, string FileName, int? PageNumber, string? Section,
    string Snippet, double Score);

public sealed record MessageDto(
    Guid Id, string Role, string Content, string? VerificationStatus, double? Confidence, bool CorrectionTriggered,
    bool Abstained, string? WorkflowType, int? LatencyMs, DateTimeOffset CreatedAt, IReadOnlyList<CitationDto> Citations);

public sealed record SessionDetailDto(SessionDto Session, IReadOnlyList<MessageDto> Messages);

public interface IChatService
{
    Task<SessionDto> CreateSessionAsync(CreateSessionRequest request, CancellationToken ct);
    Task<IReadOnlyList<SessionDto>> ListSessionsAsync(CancellationToken ct);
    Task<SessionDetailDto> GetSessionAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct);
    Task<ChatAnswerDto> QueryAsync(ChatQueryRequest request, CancellationToken ct);
}

internal sealed class CreateSessionRequestValidator : AbstractValidator<CreateSessionRequest>
{
    public CreateSessionRequestValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.Title).MaximumLength(300);
        RuleFor(x => x.KnowledgeBaseIds).Must(ids => ids is null || ids.Length <= 50)
            .WithMessage("A session can include at most 50 knowledge bases.");
    }
}

internal sealed class ChatQueryRequestValidator : AbstractValidator<ChatQueryRequest>
{
    public ChatQueryRequestValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.SessionId).NotEmpty();
        RuleFor(x => x.Question).NotEmpty().MaximumLength(4000);
        RuleFor(x => x.TopK).InclusiveBetween(1, 20);
    }
}
backend/NexusRAG.Application/Chat/ChatService.cs
csharp
using System.Text.Json;
using FluentValidation;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;
using NexusRAG.Application.Security;
using NexusRAG.Application.Usage;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Chat;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Operations;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Chat;

internal sealed class ChatService(
    IApplicationDbContext db,
    ICurrentUser currentUser,
    IAiServiceClient ai,
    TimeProvider time,
    IValidator<CreateSessionRequest> sessionValidator,
    IValidator<ChatQueryRequest> queryValidator,
    ILogger<ChatService> logger) : IChatService
{
    private const string DefaultTitle = "New conversation";
    private const int HistoryMessages = 6;
    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new(JsonSerializerDefaults.Web);

    public async Task<SessionDto> CreateSessionAsync(CreateSessionRequest request, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        await sessionValidator.ValidateAndThrowAsync(request, ct);
        var scope = AccessScope.From(currentUser);
        var accessible = await db.KnowledgeBases.WhereAccessible(scope).Select(k => k.Id).ToListAsync(ct);

        Guid[] knowledgeBaseIds;
        if (request.KnowledgeBaseIds is { Length: > 0 } requested)
        {
            var missing = requested.Except(accessible).ToArray();
            if (missing.Length > 0) throw new NotFoundException("Knowledge base", missing[0]);
            knowledgeBaseIds = requested.Distinct().ToArray();
        }
        else
        {
            knowledgeBaseIds = accessible.ToArray();
        }
        if (knowledgeBaseIds.Length == 0) throw new ValidationException("You do not have access to any knowledge base.");

        var conversation = new Conversation
        {
            TenantId = scope.TenantId,
            UserId = scope.UserId,
            Title = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Title) ? DefaultTitle : request.Title.Trim(),
            KnowledgeBaseIds = knowledgeBaseIds,
        };
        db.Conversations.Add(conversation);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        return new SessionDto(conversation.Id, conversation.Title, conversation.KnowledgeBaseIds, 0, conversation.CreatedAt, null);
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<SessionDto>> ListSessionsAsync(CancellationToken ct) =>
        await db.Conversations.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(c => c.UserId == currentUser.UserId)
            .OrderByDescending(c => c.CreatedAt).Take(50)
            .Select(c => new SessionDto(c.Id, c.Title, c.KnowledgeBaseIds, c.Messages.Count, c.CreatedAt, c.UpdatedAt))
            .ToListAsync(ct);

    public async Task<SessionDetailDto> GetSessionAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var conversation = await db.Conversations.AsNoTracking()
            .Include(c => c.Messages).ThenInclude(m => m.Citations)
            .Include(c => c.Messages).ThenInclude(m => m.Verification)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.Id == id && c.UserId == currentUser.UserId, ct)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException("Conversation", id);

        var messages = conversation.Messages
            .OrderBy(m => m.CreatedAt).ThenBy(m => m.Id)
            .Select(m => new MessageDto(
                m.Id, m.Role == MessageRole.User ? "user" : "assistant", m.Content, m.VerificationStatus,
                m.Verification?.Confidence, m.CorrectionTriggered, m.Verification?.Abstained ?? false,
                m.WorkflowType, m.LatencyMs, m.CreatedAt,
                m.Citations.OrderBy(c => c.Ordinal).Select(c => new CitationDto(
                    c.Ordinal, c.DocumentId, c.DocumentVersion, c.FileName, c.PageNumber, c.Section, c.Snippet, c.Score)).ToList()))
            .ToList();

        return new SessionDetailDto(
            new SessionDto(conversation.Id, conversation.Title, conversation.KnowledgeBaseIds, messages.Count,
                conversation.CreatedAt, conversation.UpdatedAt),
            messages);
    }

    public async Task<ChatAnswerDto> QueryAsync(ChatQueryRequest request, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        await queryValidator.ValidateAndThrowAsync(request, ct);
        var scope = AccessScope.From(currentUser);

        var conversation = await db.Conversations
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.Id == request.SessionId && c.UserId == scope.UserId, ct)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException("Conversation", request.SessionId);

        // Permissions are re-evaluated on every query: access may have changed since the session was created.
        var sessionKnowledgeBases = conversation.KnowledgeBaseIds;
        var knowledgeBases = await db.KnowledgeBases.WhereAccessible(scope)
            .Where(k => sessionKnowledgeBases.Contains(k.Id))
            .Select(k => new { k.Id, k.ContentVersion })
            .ToListAsync(ct);
        if (knowledgeBases.Count == 0)
            throw new ForbiddenException("You no longer have access to the knowledge bases in this conversation.");

        var history = await db.Messages.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(m => m.ConversationId == conversation.Id)
            .OrderByDescending(m => m.CreatedAt).Take(HistoryMessages)
            .Select(m => new { m.Role, m.Content })
            .ToListAsync(ct);
        history.Reverse();

        var question = request.Question.Trim();
        db.Messages.Add(new Message { TenantId = scope.TenantId, ConversationId = conversation.Id, Role = MessageRole.User, Content = question });
        if (conversation.Title == DefaultTitle) conversation.Title = question.Length > 80 ? question[..80] + "…" : question;
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);

        var aiRequest = new AiQueryRequest(
            question,
            new AiRetrievalScope(scope.TenantId, scope.UserId, knowledgeBases.Select(k => k.Id).ToList(),
                scope.Groups.ToList(), scope.IsTenantAdmin,
                knowledgeBases.ToDictionary(k => k.Id.ToString(), k => k.ContentVersion)),
            history.Select(h => new AiHistoryMessage(h.Role == MessageRole.User ? "user" : "assistant",
                h.Content.Length > 2000 ? h.Content[..2000] : h.Content)).ToList(),
            new AiQueryOptions(request.TopK, request.Verify));

        var response = await ai.QueryAsync(aiRequest, ct);

        // Defense in depth: drop citations to documents that are deleted or no longer accessible.
        var citedIds = response.Citations.Select(c => c.DocumentId).Distinct().ToList();
        var allowedIds = (await db.Documents.WhereAccessible(scope).Where(d => citedIds.Contains(d.Id))
            .Select(d => d.Id).ToListAsync(ct)).ToHashSet();
        var citations = response.Citations.Where(c => allowedIds.Contains(c.DocumentId)).ToList();
        var warnings = response.Warnings.ToList();
        if (citations.Count < response.Citations.Count)
            warnings.Add("Some sources were removed because they are no longer available to you.");

        var verification = response.Verification;
        var assistant = new Message
        {
            TenantId = scope.TenantId,
            ConversationId = conversation.Id,
            Role = MessageRole.Assistant,
            Content = response.Answer,
            VerificationStatus = verification?.Status,
            CorrectionTriggered = verification?.CorrectionTriggered ?? false,
            WorkflowType = response.Workflow,
            LatencyMs = response.LatencyMs,
        };
        assistant.Citations.AddRange(citations.Select(c => new Citation
        {
            TenantId = scope.TenantId,
            MessageId = assistant.Id,
            Ordinal = c.Ordinal,
            DocumentId = c.DocumentId,
            DocumentVersion = c.DocumentVersion,
            ChunkId = Trim(c.ChunkId, 100),
            FileName = Trim(c.FileName, 500),
            PageNumber = c.PageNumber,
            Section = c.Section is null ? null : Trim(c.Section, 500),
            Snippet = Trim(c.Snippet, 4000),
            Score = c.Score,
        }));
        if (verification is not null)
        {
            assistant.Verification = new VerificationResult
            {
                TenantId = scope.TenantId,
                MessageId = assistant.Id,
                OverallStatus = verification.Status,
                Confidence = verification.Confidence,
                CorrectionAttempts = verification.CorrectionAttempts,
                Abstained = verification.Abstained,
                ClaimsJson = JsonSerializer.Serialize(verification.Claims, JsonOptions),
            };
        }
        db.Messages.Add(assistant);

        await UsageRecorder.RecordAsync(db, scope.TenantId, scope.UserId, response.Usage, time.GetUtcNow(), ct);
        db.AuditLogs.Add(AuditLog.Create(scope.TenantId, scope.UserId, "chat.query", "Conversation", conversation.Id.ToString(),
            new { response.Workflow, verification = verification?.Status, response.LatencyMs, response.Cache }));
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);

        logger.LogInformation("Chat answer {MessageId}: workflow={Workflow} verification={Status} cache={Cache} latency={Latency}ms",
            assistant.Id, response.Workflow, verification?.Status, response.Cache, response.LatencyMs);

        return new ChatAnswerDto(assistant.Id, conversation.Id, response.Answer, citations, verification, warnings,
            response.Workflow, response.QueryType, response.LatencyMs, response.Cache, response.Trace ?? []);
    }

    private static string Trim(string value, int max) => value.Length <= max ? value : value[..max];
}

backend/NexusRAG.Infrastructure/AiService/AiServiceClient.cs (replace)
csharp
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using System.Text.Json;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;
using Polly.CircuitBreaker;
using Polly.Timeout;

namespace NexusRAG.Infrastructure.AiService;

internal sealed class AiServiceClient(HttpClient httpClient, ILogger<AiServiceClient> logger) : IAiServiceClient
{
    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new(JsonSerializerDefaults.Web);

    public async Task<AiServiceHealth> GetHealthAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        try
        {
            using var response = await httpClient.GetAsync("health/ready", cancellationToken);
            if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
                return new AiServiceHealth(false, "unhealthy", $"HTTP {(int)response.StatusCode}");
            var payload = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<HealthPayload>(JsonOptions, cancellationToken);
            return new AiServiceHealth(true, payload?.Status ?? "ok");
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            throw;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            logger.LogWarning(ex, "AI service health check failed");
            return new AiServiceHealth(false, "unreachable", ex.GetType().Name);
        }
    }

    public async Task<AiQueryResponse> QueryAsync(AiQueryRequest request, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        HttpResponseMessage response;
        try
        {
            response = await httpClient.PostAsJsonAsync("internal/v1/rag/query", request, JsonOptions, cancellationToken);
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            throw;
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is HttpRequestException or TimeoutRejectedException or BrokenCircuitException or TaskCanceledException)
        {
            logger.LogWarning(ex, "AI service unreachable or timed out");
            throw new ServiceUnavailableException("The AI service is unreachable or timed out. Please retry shortly.");
        }

        using (response)
        {
            if (response.StatusCode is HttpStatusCode.ServiceUnavailable or HttpStatusCode.GatewayTimeout)
                throw new ServiceUnavailableException(await ReadDetailAsync(response, cancellationToken)
                                                      ?? "AI providers are temporarily unavailable.");

            if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
            {
                logger.LogError("AI service returned HTTP {Status}", (int)response.StatusCode);
                throw new InvalidOperationException($"AI service error (HTTP {(int)response.StatusCode}).");
            }

            return await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<AiQueryResponse>(JsonOptions, cancellationToken)
                   ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("The AI service returned an empty response.");
        }
    }

    private static async Task<string?> ReadDetailAsync(HttpResponseMessage response, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        try
        {
            using var document = JsonDocument.Parse(await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync(ct));
            return document.RootElement.TryGetProperty("detail", out var detail) && detail.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.String
                ? detail.GetString()
                : null;
        }
        catch (JsonException)
        {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private sealed record HealthPayload(string Status);
}
Edit backend/NexusRAG.Infrastructure/DependencyInjection.cs (AI client registration)

Add using Polly; at the top, and replace .AddStandardResilienceHandler(); on the AI client with:

csharp
            .AddStandardResilienceHandler(options =>
            {
                // Never re-run a RAG query automatically: each attempt spends free AI quota.
                options.Retry.ShouldHandle = static _ => PredicateResult.False();
                // Python enforces 120 s per question; allow slightly more here.
                options.AttemptTimeout.Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(130);
                options.TotalRequestTimeout.Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(140);
                options.CircuitBreaker.SamplingDuration = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(280);   // must be >= 2x attempt timeout
            });

        }));
backend/NexusRAG.API/Controllers/ChatController.cs
csharp
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.RateLimiting;
using NexusRAG.API.Security;
using NexusRAG.Application.Chat;

namespace NexusRAG.API.Controllers;

[ApiController]
[Route("api/chat")]
[Authorize(Policy = Policies.Reader)]
[EnableRateLimiting("api")]
public sealed class ChatController(IChatService chat) : ControllerBase
{
    [HttpPost("sessions")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<SessionDto>> CreateSession(CreateSessionRequest request, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var session = await chat.CreateSessionAsync(request, ct);
        return CreatedAtAction(nameof(GetSession), new { id = session.Id }, session);
    }

    [HttpGet("sessions")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<IReadOnlyList<SessionDto>>> ListSessions(CancellationToken ct) =>
        Ok(await chat.ListSessionsAsync(ct));

    [HttpGet("sessions/{id:guid}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<SessionDetailDto>> GetSession(Guid id, CancellationToken ct) =>
        Ok(await chat.GetSessionAsync(id, ct));

    [HttpPost("query"), EnableRateLimiting("chat")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ChatAnswerDto>> Query(ChatQueryRequest request, CancellationToken ct) =>
        Ok(await chat.QueryAsync(request, ct));
}
backend/NexusRAG.Tests/Chat/ChatServiceTests.cs
csharp
using FluentValidation;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;
using Moq;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Chat;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;
using NexusRAG.Application.Documents;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Documents;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Identity;
using NexusRAG.Domain.KnowledgeBases;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Persistence;

namespace NexusRAG.Tests.Chat;

public class ChatServiceTests
{
    private sealed class FixedTenant(Guid tenantId) : ITenantContext
    {
        public Guid? TenantId => tenantId;
        public bool BypassTenantFilter => false;
    }

    private sealed record World(string Db, Guid Tenant, Guid User, Guid HrKb, Guid FinanceKb, Guid LiveDoc, Guid DeletedDoc);

    private static NexusRagDbContext NewContext(World w) =>
        new(new DbContextOptionsBuilder<NexusRagDbContext>().UseInMemoryDatabase(w.Db).Options,
            new FixedTenant(w.Tenant), TimeProvider.System);

    private static async Task<World> SeedAsync()
    {
        var tenant = Guid.NewGuid();
        var hr = new KnowledgeBase { TenantId = tenant, Name = "HR", AllowedGroups = ["hr"] };
        var finance = new KnowledgeBase { TenantId = tenant, Name = "Finance", AllowedGroups = ["finance"] };
        var live = new Document { TenantId = tenant, KnowledgeBaseId = hr.Id, FileName = "leave.pdf", ContentType = "application/pdf", FileExtension = ".pdf", CurrentVersion = 1 };
        var deleted = new Document { TenantId = tenant, KnowledgeBaseId = hr.Id, FileName = "old.pdf", ContentType = "application/pdf", FileExtension = ".pdf", CurrentVersion = 1, IsDeleted = true };
        var world = new World(Guid.NewGuid().ToString(), tenant, Guid.NewGuid(), hr.Id, finance.Id, live.Id, deleted.Id);

        await using var db = NewContext(world);
        db.AddRange(hr, finance, live, deleted);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        return world;
    }

    private static ICurrentUser Viewer(World w, params string[] groups)
    {
        var user = new Mock<ICurrentUser>();
        user.SetupGet(u => u.IsAuthenticated).Returns(true);
        user.SetupGet(u => u.TenantId).Returns(w.Tenant);
        user.SetupGet(u => u.UserId).Returns(w.User);
        user.SetupGet(u => u.Roles).Returns(new[] { RoleNames.Viewer });
        user.SetupGet(u => u.AccessGroups).Returns(groups);
        user.Setup(u => u.IsInRole(It.IsAny<string>())).Returns<string>(r => r == RoleNames.Viewer);
        return user.Object;
    }

    private static ChatService Service(NexusRagDbContext db, ICurrentUser user, IAiServiceClient ai) =>
        new(db, user, ai, TimeProvider.System, new CreateSessionRequestValidator(), new ChatQueryRequestValidator(),
            NullLogger<ChatService>.Instance);

    private static AiQueryResponse Response(World w) => new(
        "India grants 18 days [1]. Old policy said 15 days [2].",
        [
            new AiCitation(1, "c1", w.LiveDoc, 1, "leave.pdf", 3, "India", "18 days", 0.9),
            new AiCitation(2, "c2", w.DeletedDoc, 1, "old.pdf", 1, null, "15 days", 0.5),
        ],
        new AiVerificationReport("SUPPORTED", 0.95, [new AiClaimResult(1, "India grants 18 days", "SUPPORTED", [1], "ok", [])],
            [], [], [], 0, false, false, "openrouter/m", true, "note"),
        "simple", "simple_fact", "q", [], [],
        [new ProviderUsageEntry("groq", "llama", "chat", 1, 0, 0, 900, 120, 0)],
        1500, null, [new AiTraceStep("respond", 900, "groq/llama")]);

    [Fact]
    public async Task CreateSession_WithInaccessibleKnowledgeBase_IsNotFound()
    {
        var w = await SeedAsync();
        await using var db = NewContext(w);
        var service = Service(db, Viewer(w, "hr"), Mock.Of<IAiServiceClient>());

        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<NotFoundException>(() =>
            service.CreateSessionAsync(new CreateSessionRequest("t", [w.FinanceKb]), default));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task CreateSession_WithoutAnyAccess_IsRejected()
    {
        var w = await SeedAsync();
        await using var db = NewContext(w);
        var service = Service(db, Viewer(w), Mock.Of<IAiServiceClient>());

        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<ValidationException>(() => service.CreateSessionAsync(new CreateSessionRequest(null, null), default));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Query_SendsOnlyAccessibleScope_DropsDeletedSources_AndPersistsEverything()
    {
        var w = await SeedAsync();
        await using var db = NewContext(w);
        AiQueryRequest? sent = null;
        var ai = new Mock<IAiServiceClient>();
        ai.Setup(a => a.QueryAsync(It.IsAny<AiQueryRequest>(), It.IsAny<CancellationToken>()))
            .Callback<AiQueryRequest, CancellationToken>((r, _) => sent = r)
            .ReturnsAsync(Response(w));
        var service = Service(db, Viewer(w, "hr"), ai.Object);

        var session = await service.CreateSessionAsync(new CreateSessionRequest(null, null), default);
        var answer = await service.QueryAsync(new ChatQueryRequest(session.Id, "How many leave days in India?"), default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { w.HrKb }, sent!.Scope.KnowledgeBaseIds);              // finance KB never sent
        Assert.True(sent.Scope.KnowledgeBaseVersions.ContainsKey(w.HrKb.ToString()));
        Assert.Single(answer.Citations);                                            // deleted doc's citation dropped
        Assert.Contains(answer.Warnings, x => x.Contains("no longer available"));

        Assert.Equal(2, await db.Messages.CountAsync());
        Assert.Equal(1, await db.Citations.CountAsync());
        Assert.Equal("SUPPORTED", (await db.VerificationResults.SingleAsync()).OverallStatus);
        Assert.Equal(1, await db.UsageRecords.CountAsync());
        Assert.Equal("How many leave days in India?", (await db.Conversations.SingleAsync()).Title);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Query_OnSomeoneElsesSession_IsNotFound()
    {
        var w = await SeedAsync();
        await using var db = NewContext(w);
        var owner = Service(db, Viewer(w, "hr"), Mock.Of<IAiServiceClient>());
        var session = await owner.CreateSessionAsync(new CreateSessionRequest(null, null), default);

        var intruder = Service(db, Viewer(w with { User = Guid.NewGuid() }, "hr"), Mock.Of<IAiServiceClient>());
        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<NotFoundException>(() =>
            intruder.QueryAsync(new ChatQueryRequest(session.Id, "hi there"), default));
    }
}

SESSION=$(curl -s -X POST $API/api/chat/sessions -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "{\"knowledgeBaseIds\":[\"$KB\"]}" | jq -r .id)

  -d "{\"sessionId\":\"$SESSION\",\"question\":\"How many days of annual leave do employees in India get?\"}" \
  | jq '{answer, workflow, cache, status: .verification.status, confidence: .verification.confidence, trace}'

# 2) Same question again -> "cache": "exact", near-zero latency, no AI quota used
# 3) Comparison -> "workflow": "multi_agent"; trace shows research + analyze
curl -s -X POST $API/api/chat/query -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d "{\"sessionId\":\"$SESSION\",\"question\":\"Compare India and US leave policies based on annual leave, sick leave, eligibility, carry-forward rules, and exceptions.\"}" \

# 4) Follow-up (uses history; never served from the answer cache)

  -d "{\"sessionId\":\"$SESSION\",\"question\":\"And what about sick leave?\"}" | jq .answer

# 5) Full persisted history with citations and verification

Add to ARCHITECTURE.md (new section 16)
markdown
## 16. Agents, caching and chat (Phases 12–13)

**LangGraph workflow** (`app/agents/graph.py`): understand → supervisor →
simple: retrieve → respond | complex (comparison, multi-hop, summary, >1 sub-query): research → analyze → respond;
respond → verify → (correct → verify, max 2) → END. Each node has a timeout (`AGENT_NODE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS`) and
appends a trace step (node, duration, detail) returned to the UI. `recursion_limit=25` is a safety net on top of
the explicit correction bound. Agents are read-only; their only tool is scope-filtered retrieval, so no
human-approval step is needed inside the graph. Sensitive operations stay in the .NET API behind RBAC.
Checkpointing is not used: runs are request-scoped and finish within the request timeout.

**Research agent** retrieves per sub-query (one batched embedding call) and merges round-robin so every
entity/aspect contributes evidence. **Analysis agent** builds a deterministic entity × aspect coverage matrix
that tells the response agent where evidence exists and where it must state a gap (no LLM call).

**Token budget**: context = min(MAX_CONTEXT_TOKENS, REQUEST_TOKEN_CEILING − OUTPUT_TOKEN_RESERVE − system −
question − guidance − overhead), floor 500. The ceiling keeps a single request under free-tier TPM limits.

**Caches** (Redis, fail-open): exact answer, semantic answer (vectors in Qdrant collection `semantic_cache__*`,
answers in Redis), embeddings (tenant-scoped), retrieval, and deterministic LLM calls. Keys include tenant,
permission hash, KB ContentVersions, and a config version (prompts, models, embedding, retrieval settings).
Answers are cached only when SUPPORTED, not abstained, without warnings, and without chat history.
No KB versions → no answer/retrieval caching.

**.NET chat**: `POST /api/chat/sessions`, `GET /api/chat/sessions`, `GET /api/chat/sessions/{id}`,
`POST /api/chat/query`. The scope is recomputed from the JWT on every query; citations to deleted or
inaccessible documents are dropped; messages, citations, verification results, usage and an audit entry are
persisted. RAG calls are never auto-retried (quota) and map AI unavailability to HTTP 503.

Status and known limitations

Verification: As in previous batches, I couldn't run this code, because my environment has no package network access. The parts most likely to need a small adjustment:

LangGraph state handling. If your installed version rejects the TypedDict reducers or pydantic objects in state, paste the error and I'll adapt it.
Polly PredicateResult in the resilience configuration, depending on your package version.
Qdrant score_threshold in the semantic cache query.

Known limitations:

The semantic cache threshold (0.95) is uncalibrated. Too low risks returning an answer to a subtly different question; Phase 15 calibrates it against evaluation data. You can set SEMANTIC_CACHE_ENABLED=false in the meantime.
No conversation summarization. Follow-ups use the last 6 messages (2,000 characters each) for the rewrite only; generation itself never sees raw history. Long-conversation summarization would cost an extra LLM call per turn, so it is left out.
Document embedding cache is off by default. On Upstash's free 256 MB, caching document vectors would fill memory. Turn it on locally with EMBEDDING_CACHE_DOCUMENTS=true.
A failed AI call leaves the user's question saved without an answer in the session history. This is intentional (nothing is lost), but the UI should show it as "unanswered".
Typical free-tier capacity per question, before caching: 1 embedding call, 1 generation, and 1 verification call. Complex questions may add 1 decomposition call, and each correction adds up to 2 more calls. Repeated questions cost nothing.

It will include a login page, dashboard, knowledge bases, document upload with live ingestion status, the chat UI with citations and a source viewer, verification badges and the agent trace, usage and quota analytics, and user management. Like the other batches, it will be complete code with exact paths, delivered across two replies.

Phase 14 (part 1 of 2): Angular 21 application

Phase 14 is split across two replies:

Reply	Contents
This one	Project setup, authentication (login, register, silent token refresh), app shell and navigation, dashboard, knowledge bases, documents (drag-and-drop and bulk upload, live ingestion status, versions, re-index, delete, download), and the full chat UI (sessions, verified answers with clickable citations, source viewer, verification details, agent trace)
Next	The .NET analytics endpoints plus the Analytics, Provider quota, Cache, AI quality and Verification history screens; user and role management; tenant admin; Vitest tests; production Docker/nginx for the frontend
Architecture decisions
Decision	Why
Standalone components, signals for all state, input() / output(), new control flow (@if, @for)	Current Angular idioms. It also works zoneless (provideZonelessChangeDetection).
Functional interceptors: auth (Bearer token + single-flight refresh on 401) and errors (ProblemDetails → toast)	Every component gets authentication and error handling for free.
Access token in memory only; refresh token in localStorage	A trade-off: the session survives reloads. The safer option (an httpOnly cookie) needs a backend change; noted in the known limitations.
Dev proxy /api → http://localhost:5080	No CORS setup needed. Production uses the same pattern with nginx in the next reply.
No UI component library; a small design system in styles.scss (light and dark mode)	Zero extra dependencies, and full control over the look.
marked for answer markdown; Angular's built-in HTML sanitizer cleans the output	Answers contain tables. Any scripts or event handlers in model output are stripped by Angular.
The UI never calls the AI service	All AI access goes through the .NET API, so no AI key can ever reach the browser.

import {
  ApplicationConfig,
  inject,
  provideAppInitializer,
  provideBrowserGlobalErrorListeners,
  provideZonelessChangeDetection,
} from '@angular/core';
import { provideHttpClient, withInterceptors } from '@angular/common/http';
import { provideRouter, withComponentInputBinding } from '@angular/router';
import { routes } from './app.routes';
import { authInterceptor } from './core/auth/auth.interceptor';
import { AuthService } from './core/auth/auth.service';
import { errorInterceptor } from './core/http/error.interceptor';

export const appConfig: ApplicationConfig = {
  providers: [
    provideBrowserGlobalErrorListeners(),
    provideZonelessChangeDetection(),
    provideRouter(routes, withComponentInputBinding()),     // route params arrive as signal inputs
    // XHR backend (not withFetch) so upload progress events work.
    provideHttpClient(withInterceptors([authInterceptor, errorInterceptor])),
    // Restores the session from the refresh token before the first navigation.
    provideAppInitializer(() => inject(AuthService).restoreSession()),
  ],
};

// Mirrors the .NET DTOs (camelCase JSON; enums serialized as SNAKE_CASE_UPPER strings).

export type IngestionStatus =
  | 'UPLOADED' | 'QUEUED' | 'PARSING' | 'CHUNKING' | 'EMBEDDING'
  | 'INDEXING' | 'INDEXED' | 'FAILED' | 'RETRYING' | 'CANCELLED';

export const TERMINAL_STATUSES: readonly IngestionStatus[] = ['INDEXED', 'FAILED', 'CANCELLED'];

export type VerificationStatus = 'SUPPORTED' | 'CONTRADICTED' | 'INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE' | 'UNCERTAIN';
export type RoleName = 'PlatformAdmin' | 'TenantAdmin' | 'Editor' | 'Viewer';

export interface ProblemDetails {
  title?: string;
  detail?: string;
  status?: number;
  traceId?: string;
  errors?: Record<string, string[]>;
}

export interface PagedResult<T> {
  items: T[];
  page: number;
  pageSize: number;
  totalCount: number;
}

  documentId: string;

frontend/src/app/core/http/http-context.ts
ts
import { HttpContext, HttpContextToken } from '@angular/common/http';

/** Set on requests whose errors the calling component displays itself (no global toast). */
export const SKIP_ERROR_TOAST = new HttpContextToken<boolean>(() => false);

export function skipToast(): HttpContext {
  return new HttpContext().set(SKIP_ERROR_TOAST, true);
}
frontend/src/app/core/http/error.interceptor.ts
ts
import { HttpErrorResponse, HttpInterceptorFn } from '@angular/common/http';
import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import { catchError, throwError } from 'rxjs';
import { ProblemDetails } from '../models';
import { ToastService } from '../notifications/toast.service';
import { SKIP_ERROR_TOAST } from './http-context';

/** Converts an HTTP error (ProblemDetails from the .NET API) into a human-readable message. */
export function describeError(error: unknown): string {
  if (!(error instanceof HttpErrorResponse)) return 'Something went wrong.';
  if (error.status === 0) return 'Cannot reach the server. Check your connection.';
  if (error.status === 429) return 'Too many requests. Please wait a moment and try again.';
  const problem = error.error as ProblemDetails | null;
  if (problem?.errors) {
    const messages = Object.values(problem.errors).flat();
    if (messages.length) return messages.join(' ');
  }
  return problem?.detail || problem?.title || `Request failed (HTTP ${error.status}).`;
}

export const errorInterceptor: HttpInterceptorFn = (req, next) => {
  const toasts = inject(ToastService);
  return next(req).pipe(
    catchError((error: unknown) => {
      // 401 is handled by the auth interceptor (refresh / sign-out).
      if (error instanceof HttpErrorResponse && error.status !== 401 && !req.context.get(SKIP_ERROR_TOAST)) {
        toasts.error(describeError(error));
      }
      return throwError(() => error);
    }),
  );
};
frontend/src/app/core/notifications/toast.service.ts
ts
import { Injectable, signal } from '@angular/core';

export interface Toast {
  id: number;
  kind: 'success' | 'error' | 'info';
  message: string;
}

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class ToastService {
  private nextId = 1;
  readonly toasts = signal<Toast[]>([]);

  success(message: string): void {
    this.push('success', message, 4000);
  }

  info(message: string): void {
    this.push('info', message, 4000);
  }

  error(message: string): void {
    this.push('error', message, 7000);
  }

  dismiss(id: number): void {
    this.toasts.update((list) => list.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
  }

  private push(kind: Toast['kind'], message: string, ttlMs: number): void {
    const id = this.nextId++;
    this.toasts.update((list) => [...list, { id, kind, message }].slice(-4));
    setTimeout(() => this.dismiss(id), ttlMs);
  }
}
frontend/src/app/core/auth/auth.service.ts
ts
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { computed, inject, Injectable, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';
import { finalize, firstValueFrom, map, Observable, shareReplay, throwError } from 'rxjs';
import { skipToast } from '../http/http-context';
import { AuthResponse, RegisterTenantRequest, RoleName, UserSummary } from '../models';

const REFRESH_KEY = 'nexusrag.refreshToken';

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class AuthService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);

  private readonly currentUser = signal<UserSummary | null>(null);
  private accessToken: string | null = null;                // memory only - never persisted
  private refreshInFlight: Observable<string> | null = null;

  readonly user = this.currentUser.asReadonly();
  readonly isAuthenticated = computed(() => this.currentUser() !== null);
  readonly isAdmin = computed(() => this.hasRole('TenantAdmin', 'PlatformAdmin'));
  readonly canEdit = computed(() => this.hasRole('TenantAdmin', 'PlatformAdmin', 'Editor'));

  get token(): string | null {
    return this.accessToken;
  }

  hasRole(...roles: RoleName[]): boolean {
    const user = this.currentUser();
    return !!user && user.roles.some((r) => roles.includes(r));
  }

  login(email: string, password: string): Observable<UserSummary> {
    return this.http
      .post<AuthResponse>('/api/auth/login', { email, password }, { context: skipToast() })
      .pipe(map((response) => this.apply(response)));
  }

  registerTenant(request: RegisterTenantRequest): Observable<UserSummary> {
    return this.http
      .post<AuthResponse>('/api/auth/register-tenant', request, { context: skipToast() })
      .pipe(map((response) => this.apply(response)));
  }

  /** Single-flight: concurrent 401s share one refresh call (refresh tokens rotate and are single-use). */
  refresh(): Observable<string> {
    if (this.refreshInFlight) return this.refreshInFlight;
    const refreshToken = localStorage.getItem(REFRESH_KEY);
    if (!refreshToken) return throwError(() => new Error('No refresh token'));

    this.refreshInFlight = this.http
      .post<AuthResponse>('/api/auth/refresh', { refreshToken }, { context: skipToast() })
      .pipe(
        map((response) => {
          this.apply(response);
          return response.accessToken;
        }),
        finalize(() => (this.refreshInFlight = null)),
        shareReplay(1),
      );
    return this.refreshInFlight;
  }

  async restoreSession(): Promise<void> {
    if (!localStorage.getItem(REFRESH_KEY)) return;
    try {
      await firstValueFrom(this.refresh());
    } catch {
      this.clear();
    }
  }

  logout(): void {
    const refreshToken = localStorage.getItem(REFRESH_KEY);
    if (refreshToken) {
      this.http.post('/api/auth/logout', { refreshToken }, { context: skipToast() }).subscribe({ error: () => undefined });
    }
    this.clear();
    void this.router.navigateByUrl('/login');
  }

  clear(): void {
    this.accessToken = null;
    this.currentUser.set(null);
    localStorage.removeItem(REFRESH_KEY);
  }

  private apply(response: AuthResponse): UserSummary {
    this.accessToken = response.accessToken;
    localStorage.setItem(REFRESH_KEY, response.refreshToken);
    this.currentUser.set(response.user);
    return response.user;
  }
}
frontend/src/app/core/auth/auth.interceptor.ts
ts
import { HttpErrorResponse, HttpInterceptorFn, HttpRequest } from '@angular/common/http';
import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import { catchError, switchMap, throwError } from 'rxjs';
import { AuthService } from './auth.service';

const ANONYMOUS_ENDPOINTS = ['/api/auth/login', '/api/auth/register-tenant', '/api/auth/refresh', '/api/auth/logout'];

export const authInterceptor: HttpInterceptorFn = (req, next) => {
  if (!req.url.startsWith('/api/') || ANONYMOUS_ENDPOINTS.some((url) => req.url.startsWith(url))) {
    return next(req);
  }

  const auth = inject(AuthService);
  const withToken = (request: HttpRequest<unknown>, token: string | null) =>
    token ? request.clone({ setHeaders: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }) : request;

  return next(withToken(req, auth.token)).pipe(
    catchError((error: unknown) => {
      if (!(error instanceof HttpErrorResponse) || error.status !== 401) {
        return throwError(() => error);
      }
      // Access token expired: refresh once, then retry the original request.
      return auth.refresh().pipe(
        switchMap((token) => next(withToken(req, token))),
        catchError((refreshError: unknown) => {
          auth.logout();
          return throwError(() => refreshError);
        }),
      );
    }),
  );
};
frontend/src/app/core/auth/guards.ts
ts
import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CanActivateFn, Router } from '@angular/router';
import { RoleName } from '../models';
import { AuthService } from './auth.service';

export const authGuard: CanActivateFn = (_route, state) => {
  if (inject(AuthService).isAuthenticated()) return true;
  return inject(Router).createUrlTree(['/login'], { queryParams: { returnUrl: state.url } });
};

export const guestGuard: CanActivateFn = () =>
  inject(AuthService).isAuthenticated() ? inject(Router).createUrlTree(['/dashboard']) : true;

/** UI-level guard only. Authorization is always enforced by the API. */
export function roleGuard(...roles: RoleName[]): CanActivateFn {
  return () => (inject(AuthService).hasRole(...roles) ? true : inject(Router).createUrlTree(['/dashboard']));
}

frontend/src/app/core/api/document.api.ts
ts
import { HttpClient, HttpEvent, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { inject, Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { skipToast } from '../http/http-context';
import {
  BulkUploadItemResult,
  DocumentItem,
  DocumentStatus,
  DocumentVersion,
  PagedResult,
  UploadResult,
} from '../models';

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class DocumentApi {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);

  list(knowledgeBaseId: string, page = 1, pageSize = 25) {
    const params = new HttpParams().set('knowledgeBaseId', knowledgeBaseId).set('page', page).set('pageSize', pageSize);
    return this.http.get<PagedResult<DocumentItem>>('/api/documents', { params });
  }

  status(id: string) {
    return this.http.get<DocumentStatus>(`/api/documents/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/status`, { context: skipToast() });
  }

  versions(id: string) {
    return this.http.get<DocumentVersion[]>(`/api/documents/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/versions`);
  }

  upload(file: File, knowledgeBaseId: string, accessGroups: string, documentId?: string): Observable<HttpEvent<UploadResult>> {
    const form = new FormData();
    form.append('file', file);
    form.append('knowledgeBaseId', knowledgeBaseId);
    if (accessGroups.trim()) form.append('accessGroups', accessGroups);
    if (documentId) form.append('documentId', documentId);
    return this.http.post<UploadResult>('/api/documents/upload', form, {
      reportProgress: true,
      observe: 'events',
      context: skipToast(),
    });
  }

  bulkUpload(files: File[], knowledgeBaseId: string, accessGroups: string): Observable<HttpEvent<BulkUploadItemResult[]>> {
    const form = new FormData();
    files.forEach((file) => form.append('files', file));
    form.append('knowledgeBaseId', knowledgeBaseId);
    if (accessGroups.trim()) form.append('accessGroups', accessGroups);
    return this.http.post<BulkUploadItemResult[]>('/api/documents/bulk-upload', form, {
      reportProgress: true,
      observe: 'events',
      context: skipToast(),
    });
  }

  download(id: string, version?: number) {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (version) params = params.set('version', version);
    return this.http.get(`/api/documents/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/content`, { params, responseType: 'blob' });
  }

  reindex(id: string) {
    return this.http.post<DocumentStatus>(`/api/documents/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/reindex`, {});
  }

  delete(id: string) {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`/api/documents/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`);
  }
}
frontend/src/app/core/api/chat.api.ts

import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class ChatApi {

    return this.http.post<Session>('/api/chat/sessions', body);
  }

  listSessions() {
    return this.http.get<Session[]>('/api/chat/sessions');
  }

  getSession(id: string) {
    return this.http.get<SessionDetail>(`/api/chat/sessions/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`);
  }

  /** Errors are shown inline in the conversation, not as toasts. */
  query(body: { sessionId: string; question: string; topK: number; verify: boolean }) {
    return this.http.post<ChatAnswer>('/api/chat/query', body, { context: skipToast() });
  }
}

frontend/src/app/shared/badges.ts
ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, computed, input } from '@angular/core';
import { IngestionStatus, TERMINAL_STATUSES, VerificationStatus } from '../core/models';

const STATUS_LABELS: Record<IngestionStatus, string> = {
  UPLOADED: 'Uploaded',
  QUEUED: 'Queued',
  PARSING: 'Parsing',
  CHUNKING: 'Chunking',
  EMBEDDING: 'Embedding',
  INDEXING: 'Indexing',
  INDEXED: 'Indexed',
  FAILED: 'Failed',
  RETRYING: 'Retrying',
  CANCELLED: 'Cancelled',
};

@Component({
  selector: 'app-status-badge',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <span class="badge" [attr.data-tone]="tone()">
      @if (active()) { <span class="pulse" aria-hidden="true"></span> }
      {{ label() }}@if (active() && progress() !== null) { · {{ progress() }}% }
    </span>
  `,
})
export class StatusBadge {
  readonly status = input.required<IngestionStatus>();
  readonly progress = input<number | null>(null);

  readonly active = computed(() => !TERMINAL_STATUSES.includes(this.status()));
  readonly label = computed(() => STATUS_LABELS[this.status()]);
  readonly tone = computed(() => {
    switch (this.status()) {
      case 'INDEXED': return 'success';
      case 'FAILED': return 'danger';
      case 'RETRYING': return 'warning';
      case 'CANCELLED': return 'neutral';
      default: return 'info';
    }
  });
}

const VERIFICATION_LABELS: Record<VerificationStatus, string> = {
  SUPPORTED: 'Verified',
  UNCERTAIN: 'Partially verified',
  CONTRADICTED: 'Contradiction found',
  INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE: 'Insufficient evidence',
};

@Component({
  selector: 'app-verification-badge',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <span class="badge" [attr.data-tone]="tone()"
          title="Confidence is computed from the verification results, not self-reported by the model.">
      {{ label() }}@if (showConfidence()) { · {{ percent() }}% }
    </span>
  `,
})
export class VerificationBadge {
  readonly status = input.required<VerificationStatus>();
  readonly confidence = input<number | null>(null);
  readonly abstained = input(false);

  readonly label = computed(() => (this.abstained() ? 'Not enough evidence' : VERIFICATION_LABELS[this.status()]));
  readonly percent = computed(() => Math.round((this.confidence() ?? 0) * 100));
  readonly showConfidence = computed(() => this.confidence() !== null && !this.abstained());
  readonly tone = computed(() => {
    if (this.abstained()) return 'neutral';
    switch (this.status()) {
      case 'SUPPORTED': return 'success';
      case 'CONTRADICTED': return 'danger';
      case 'UNCERTAIN': return 'warning';
      default: return 'neutral';
    }
  });
}
frontend/src/app/shared/pipes.ts
ts
import { Pipe, PipeTransform } from '@angular/core';

@Pipe({ name: 'bytes' })
export class BytesPipe implements PipeTransform {
  transform(value: number | null | undefined): string {
    if (!value) return '—';
    const units = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
    let size = value;
    let unit = 0;
    while (size >= 1024 && unit < units.length - 1) {
      size /= 1024;
      unit++;
    }
    return `${size.toFixed(size < 10 && unit > 0 ? 1 : 0)} ${units[unit]}`;
  }
}
frontend/src/app/shared/markdown.ts
ts
import { marked } from 'marked';

marked.setOptions({ gfm: true, breaks: false });

/**
 * Renders an answer to HTML and turns [n] markers into citation chips.
 * Output is bound with [innerHTML], so Angular's sanitizer removes scripts, event handlers and
 * dangerous URLs - even if a malicious document tricked the model into emitting HTML.
 */
export function renderAnswer(markdown: string): string {
  const html = marked.parse(markdown, { async: false }) as string;
  return html.replace(/\[(\d{1,3})\]/g, '<sup class="cite" title="Show source $1">$1</sup>');
}
frontend/src/app/shared/download.ts
ts
export function saveBlob(blob: Blob, fileName: string): void {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const anchor = document.createElement('a');
  anchor.href = url;
  anchor.download = fileName;
  anchor.click();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 10_000);
}

/**
 * Shows a blob in a window opened synchronously on the user's click (avoids popup blockers).
 * PDFs jump to the cited page via the #page fragment supported by browser PDF viewers.
 */
export function showBlobIn(target: Window | null, blob: Blob, fileName: string, page?: number | null): void {
  if (!target) {
    saveBlob(blob, fileName);
    return;
  }
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  target.location.href = page && blob.type === 'application/pdf' ? `${url}#page=${page}` : url;
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 60_000);
}

import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpErrorResponse } from '@angular/common/http';
import { NonNullableFormBuilder, ReactiveFormsModule, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { ActivatedRoute, Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/auth/auth.service';
import { describeError } from '../../core/http/error.interceptor';

/** Prevents open redirects: only same-app paths are allowed. */
function safeReturnUrl(value: string | null): string {
  return value && value.startsWith('/') && !value.startsWith('//') ? value : '/dashboard';
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-login-page',
  imports: [ReactiveFormsModule, RouterLink],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <main class="auth-layout">
      <section class="auth-card card">
        <div class="brand"><span class="brand-mark">N</span><span>NexusRAG</span></div>
        <h1>Sign in</h1>
        <p class="muted">Enterprise knowledge intelligence with verified, cited answers.</p>

        <form [formGroup]="form" (ngSubmit)="submit()" novalidate>
          <label class="field">
            <span>E-mail</span>
            <input class="input" type="email" formControlName="email" autocomplete="username" />
          </label>
          <label class="field">
            <span>Password</span>
            <input class="input" type="password" formControlName="password" autocomplete="current-password" />
          </label>
          @if (error()) { <div class="alert alert-error" role="alert">{{ error() }}</div> }
          <button class="btn btn-primary btn-block" type="submit" [disabled]="form.invalid || busy()">
            {{ busy() ? 'Signing in…' : 'Sign in' }}
          </button>
        </form>

        <p class="muted small">New organization? <a routerLink="/register">Create a workspace</a></p>
      </section>
    </main>
  `,
})
export class LoginPage {
  private readonly auth = inject(AuthService);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);
  private readonly route = inject(ActivatedRoute);

  readonly busy = signal(false);
  readonly error = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly form = inject(NonNullableFormBuilder).group({
    email: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.email]],
    password: ['', Validators.required],
  });

  submit(): void {
    if (this.form.invalid) return;
    this.busy.set(true);
    this.error.set(null);
    const { email, password } = this.form.getRawValue();

    this.auth.login(email, password).subscribe({
      next: () => void this.router.navigateByUrl(safeReturnUrl(this.route.snapshot.queryParamMap.get('returnUrl'))),
      error: (e: HttpErrorResponse) => {
        this.error.set(e.status === 401 ? 'Invalid e-mail or password.' : describeError(e));
        this.busy.set(false);
      },
    });
  }
}
frontend/src/app/features/auth/register.page.ts
ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { takeUntilDestroyed } from '@angular/core/rxjs-interop';
import { HttpErrorResponse } from '@angular/common/http';
import { NonNullableFormBuilder, ReactiveFormsModule, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/auth/auth.service';
import { describeError } from '../../core/http/error.interceptor';

const STRONG_PASSWORD = /^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*\d)(?=.*[^a-zA-Z0-9]).{12,128}$/;

function slugify(value: string): string {
  return value.toLowerCase().normalize('NFKD').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '').slice(0, 50);
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-register-page',
  imports: [ReactiveFormsModule, RouterLink],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <main class="auth-layout">
      <section class="auth-card card">
        <div class="brand"><span class="brand-mark">N</span><span>NexusRAG</span></div>
        <h1>Create a workspace</h1>
        <p class="muted">Your organization gets an isolated tenant. You become its administrator.</p>

        <form [formGroup]="form" (ngSubmit)="submit()" novalidate>
          <label class="field"><span>Organization name</span>
            <input class="input" formControlName="tenantName" autocomplete="organization" />
          </label>
          <label class="field"><span>Workspace ID</span>
            <input class="input" formControlName="tenantSlug" (input)="slugEdited = true" />
            <small class="muted">3–50 lowercase letters, digits or hyphens.</small>
          </label>
          <label class="field"><span>Your name</span>
            <input class="input" formControlName="adminDisplayName" autocomplete="name" />
          </label>
          <label class="field"><span>E-mail</span>
            <input class="input" type="email" formControlName="adminEmail" autocomplete="email" />
          </label>
          <label class="field"><span>Password</span>
            <input class="input" type="password" formControlName="adminPassword" autocomplete="new-password" />
            <small class="muted">At least 12 characters with upper- and lowercase letters, a digit and a symbol.</small>
          </label>
          @if (error()) { <div class="alert alert-error" role="alert">{{ error() }}</div> }
          <button class="btn btn-primary btn-block" type="submit" [disabled]="form.invalid || busy()">
            {{ busy() ? 'Creating…' : 'Create workspace' }}
          </button>
        </form>

        <p class="muted small">Already have an account? <a routerLink="/login">Sign in</a></p>
      </section>
    </main>
  `,
})
export class RegisterPage {
  private readonly auth = inject(AuthService);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);

  slugEdited = false;
  readonly busy = signal(false);
  readonly error = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly form = inject(NonNullableFormBuilder).group({
    tenantName: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.maxLength(200)]],
    tenantSlug: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.pattern(/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{1,48}[a-z0-9]$/)]],
    adminDisplayName: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.maxLength(200)]],
    adminEmail: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.email]],
    adminPassword: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.pattern(STRONG_PASSWORD)]],
  });

  constructor() {
    this.form.controls.tenantName.valueChanges.pipe(takeUntilDestroyed()).subscribe((name) => {
      if (!this.slugEdited) this.form.controls.tenantSlug.setValue(slugify(name));
    });
  }

  submit(): void {
    if (this.form.invalid) return;
    this.busy.set(true);
    this.error.set(null);
    this.auth.registerTenant(this.form.getRawValue()).subscribe({
      next: () => void this.router.navigateByUrl('/dashboard'),
      error: (e: HttpErrorResponse) => {
        this.error.set(e.status === 403 ? 'Self-registration is disabled on this server.' : describeError(e));
        this.busy.set(false);
      },
    });
  }
}

export class DashboardPage {
  readonly auth = inject(AuthService);
  readonly knowledgeBases = signal<KnowledgeBase[]>([]);
  readonly sessions = signal<Session[]>([]);
  readonly loading = signal(true);
  readonly documentCount = computed(() => this.knowledgeBases().reduce((sum, kb) => sum + kb.documentCount, 0));
  readonly groups = computed(() => this.auth.user()?.accessGroups.join(', ') || 'none');

  constructor() {
    forkJoin({ kbs: inject(KnowledgeBaseApi).list(), sessions: inject(ChatApi).listSessions() })
      .pipe(takeUntilDestroyed())
      .subscribe({
        next: ({ kbs, sessions }) => {
          this.knowledgeBases.set(kbs);
          this.sessions.set(sessions);
          this.loading.set(false);
        },
        error: () => this.loading.set(false),
      });
  }
}
