import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { takeUntilDestroyed } from '@angular/core/rxjs-interop';
import { NonNullableFormBuilder, ReactiveFormsModule, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { KnowledgeBaseApi } from '../../core/api/knowledge-base.api';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/auth/auth.service';
import { KnowledgeBase } from '../../core/models';
import { ToastService } from '../../core/notifications/toast.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-knowledge-bases-page',
  imports: [RouterLink, ReactiveFormsModule],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <section class="page">
      <header class="page-header">
        <div>
          <h1>Knowledge bases</h1>
          <p class="muted">Group documents by department or topic. Access groups control who can search them.</p>
        </div>
        @if (auth.canEdit()) {
          <button class="btn btn-primary" (click)="showCreate.set(!showCreate())">
            {{ showCreate() ? 'Cancel' : 'New knowledge base' }}
          </button>
        }
      </header>

      @if (showCreate()) {
        <form class="card form-card" [formGroup]="form" (ngSubmit)="create()" novalidate>
          <h2>New knowledge base</h2>
          <label class="field"><span>Name</span><input class="input" formControlName="name" maxlength="200" /></label>
          <label class="field"><span>Description</span>
            <textarea class="input" rows="2" formControlName="description" maxlength="2000"></textarea>
          </label>
          <label class="field"><span>Allowed access groups</span>
            <input class="input" formControlName="groups" placeholder="e.g. hr-india, managers" />
            <small class="muted">Comma-separated. Leave empty to allow everyone in your organization.</small>
          </label>
          <div class="toolbar">
            <button class="btn btn-primary" type="submit" [disabled]="form.invalid || busy()">
              {{ busy() ? 'Creating…' : 'Create' }}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      }

      @if (loading()) {
        <div class="empty-state"><span class="spinner"></span></div>
      } @else {
        <div class="grid grid-cards">
          @for (kb of knowledgeBases(); track kb.id) {
            <a class="card kb-card" [routerLink]="['/knowledge-bases', kb.id]">
              <h3>{{ kb.name }}</h3>
              <p class="muted small clamp">{{ kb.description || 'No description' }}</p>
              <div class="chips">
                @for (g of kb.allowedGroups; track g) { <span class="chip">{{ g }}</span> }
                @empty { <span class="chip">All users</span> }
              </div>
              <div class="kb-meta muted small">{{ kb.documentCount }} documents · content v{{ kb.contentVersion }}</div>
            </a>
          } @empty {
            <div class="card empty-state">
              <p>No knowledge bases yet.</p>
              @if (auth.canEdit()) { <p class="muted small">Create one to start uploading documents.</p> }
              @else { <p class="muted small">Ask an editor or administrator to give you access.</p> }
            </div>
          }
        </div>
      }
    </section>
  `,
})
export class KnowledgeBasesPage {
  readonly auth = inject(AuthService);
  private readonly api = inject(KnowledgeBaseApi);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);
  private readonly toasts = inject(ToastService);

  readonly knowledgeBases = signal<KnowledgeBase[]>([]);
  readonly loading = signal(true);
  readonly showCreate = signal(false);
  readonly busy = signal(false);
  readonly form = inject(NonNullableFormBuilder).group({
    name: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.maxLength(200)]],
    description: [''],
    groups: [''],
  });

  constructor() {
    this.api.list().pipe(takeUntilDestroyed()).subscribe({
      next: (list) => {
        this.knowledgeBases.set(list);
        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: () => this.loading.set(false),
    });
  }

  create(): void {
    if (this.form.invalid) return;
    const { name, description, groups } = this.form.getRawValue();
    this.busy.set(true);
    this.api
      .create({
        name: name.trim(),
        description: description.trim() || null,
        allowedGroups: groups.split(',').map((g) => g.trim()).filter(Boolean),
      })
      .subscribe({
        next: (kb) => {
          this.toasts.success(`Knowledge base "${kb.name}" created.`);
          void this.router.navigate(['/knowledge-bases', kb.id]);
        },
        error: () => this.busy.set(false),
      });
  }
}
frontend/src/app/features/knowledge-bases/upload-panel.ts
ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, computed, inject, input, output, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpErrorResponse, HttpEvent, HttpEventType } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { DocumentApi } from '../../core/api/document.api';
import { describeError } from '../../core/http/error.interceptor';
import { BulkUploadItemResult } from '../../core/models';
import { BytesPipe } from '../../shared/pipes';

const ALLOWED = ['.pdf', '.docx', '.xlsx', '.pptx', '.txt', '.md', '.csv'];
const MAX_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_FILES = 50;

interface PendingFile {
  file: File;
  error: string | null;
}

function validate(file: File): string | null {
  const extension = file.name.slice(file.name.lastIndexOf('.')).toLowerCase();
  if (!ALLOWED.includes(extension)) return `Unsupported type (${extension || 'none'})`;
  if (file.size === 0) return 'File is empty';
  if (file.size > MAX_BYTES) return 'Larger than 25 MB';
  return null;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-upload-panel',
  imports: [BytesPipe],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-header"><h2>Upload documents</h2><span class="muted small">PDF, DOCX, XLSX, PPTX, TXT, MD, CSV · max 25 MB</span></div>

      <label class="dropzone" [class.dragging]="dragging()"
             (dragover)="$event.preventDefault(); dragging.set(true)"
             (dragleave)="dragging.set(false)"
             (drop)="onDrop($event)">
        <input type="file" multiple hidden [accept]="accept" (change)="onSelect($event)" />
        <strong>Drop files here</strong> <span class="muted">or click to browse</span>
      </label>

      @if (files().length) {
        <ul class="file-list">
          @for (item of files(); track $index) {
            <li [class.invalid]="item.error">
              <span class="truncate">{{ item.file.name }}</span>
              <span class="muted small">{{ item.file.size | bytes }}</span>
              @if (item.error) { <span class="error-text small">{{ item.error }}</span> }
              <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-icon" (click)="remove($index)" [disabled]="busy()" aria-label="Remove">×</button>
            </li>
          }
        </ul>

        <label class="field">
          <span>Document access groups (optional)</span>
          <input class="input" [value]="groups()" (input)="groups.set($any($event.target).value)"
                 placeholder="e.g. hr-india" [disabled]="busy()" />
          <small class="muted">Restricts these documents further. Empty = everyone with access to this knowledge base.</small>
        </label>

        <div class="toolbar">
          <button class="btn btn-primary" (click)="upload()" [disabled]="busy() || validFiles().length === 0">
            {{ busy() ? 'Uploading…' : 'Upload ' + validFiles().length + ' file(s)' }}
          </button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="files.set([])" [disabled]="busy()">Clear</button>
        </div>
      }

      @if (progress() !== null) {
        <div class="progress"><div class="progress-bar" [style.width.%]="progress()"></div></div>
      }

      @if (results().length) {
        <ul class="result-list">
          @for (r of results(); track $index) {
            <li [class.ok]="r.succeeded" [class.failed]="!r.succeeded">
              <span>{{ r.succeeded ? '✓' : '✕' }}</span>
              <span class="truncate">{{ r.fileName }}</span>
              <span class="small muted">{{ r.succeeded ? 'Queued for indexing' : r.error }}</span>
            </li>
          }
        </ul>
      }
    </div>
  `,
})
export class UploadPanel {
  private readonly documents = inject(DocumentApi);

  readonly knowledgeBaseId = input.required<string>();
  readonly uploaded = output<void>();

  readonly accept = ALLOWED.join(',');
  readonly files = signal<PendingFile[]>([]);
  readonly groups = signal('');
  readonly dragging = signal(false);
  readonly busy = signal(false);
  readonly progress = signal<number | null>(null);
  readonly results = signal<BulkUploadItemResult[]>([]);
  readonly validFiles = computed(() => this.files().filter((f) => !f.error).map((f) => f.file));

  onDrop(event: DragEvent): void {
    event.preventDefault();
    this.dragging.set(false);
    this.add(event.dataTransfer?.files ?? null);
  }

  onSelect(event: Event): void {
    const inputEl = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    this.add(inputEl.files);
    inputEl.value = '';
  }

  remove(index: number): void {
    this.files.update((list) => list.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  }

  upload(): void {
    const files = this.validFiles();
    if (!files.length) return;
    this.busy.set(true);
    this.progress.set(0);
    this.results.set([]);

    if (files.length === 1) {
      const file = files[0];
      this.track(this.documents.upload(file, this.knowledgeBaseId(), this.groups()),
        (body) => [{ fileName: file.name, succeeded: true, result: body, error: null }],
        (error) => [{ fileName: file.name, succeeded: false, result: null, error }]);
    } else {
      this.track(this.documents.bulkUpload(files, this.knowledgeBaseId(), this.groups()),
        (body) => body,
        (error) => files.map((f) => ({ fileName: f.name, succeeded: false, result: null, error })));
    }
  }

  private add(list: FileList | null): void {
    if (!list) return;
    const added = Array.from(list).map((file) => ({ file, error: validate(file) }));
    this.files.update((existing) => [...existing, ...added].slice(0, MAX_FILES));
    this.results.set([]);
  }

  private track<T>(request: Observable<HttpEvent<T>>, onSuccess: (body: T) => BulkUploadItemResult[],
                   onError: (message: string) => BulkUploadItemResult[]): void {
    request.subscribe({
      next: (event) => {
        if (event.type === HttpEventType.UploadProgress && event.total) {
          this.progress.set(Math.round((100 * event.loaded) / event.total));
        } else if (event.type === HttpEventType.Response && event.body !== null) {
          this.finish(onSuccess(event.body));
        }
      },
      error: (e: HttpErrorResponse) => this.finish(onError(describeError(e))),
    });
  }

  private finish(results: BulkUploadItemResult[]): void {
    this.results.set(results);
    this.busy.set(false);
    this.progress.set(null);
    this.files.set([]);
    if (results.some((r) => r.succeeded)) this.uploaded.emit();
  }
}
frontend/src/app/features/knowledge-bases/knowledge-base-detail.page.ts

import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, computed, effect, inject, input, signal, untracked } from '@angular/core';
import { takeUntilDestroyed } from '@angular/core/rxjs-interop';
import { DatePipe } from '@angular/common';
import { HttpEventType } from '@angular/common/http';
import { RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { catchError, EMPTY, filter, forkJoin, interval, map, Observable, of, switchMap, tap } from 'rxjs';
import { DocumentApi } from '../../core/api/document.api';
import { KnowledgeBaseApi } from '../../core/api/knowledge-base.api';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/auth/auth.service';
import { describeError } from '../../core/http/error.interceptor';
import { DocumentItem, DocumentStatus, DocumentVersion, KnowledgeBase, TERMINAL_STATUSES } from '../../core/models';
import { ToastService } from '../../core/notifications/toast.service';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../shared/badges';
import { saveBlob } from '../../shared/download';
import { BytesPipe } from '../../shared/pipes';
import { UploadPanel } from './upload-panel';

const PAGE_SIZE = 25;

@Component({
  selector: 'app-knowledge-base-detail-page',
  imports: [RouterLink, DatePipe, BytesPipe, StatusBadge, UploadPanel],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <section class="page">
      <a class="back small" routerLink="/knowledge-bases">← Knowledge bases</a>

      @if (kb(); as k) {
        <header class="page-header">
          <div>

              @for (g of k.allowedGroups; track g) { <span class="chip">{{ g }}</span> }

      @if (auth.canEdit()) {

        @if (loading() && documents().length === 0) {
          <div class="empty-state"><span class="spinner"></span></div>

          <div class="empty-state">No documents yet.</div>

              <tbody>

                  <tr>

                      @if (d.accessGroups.length) {
                        <div class="chips">@for (g of d.accessGroups; track g) { <span class="chip chip-sm">{{ g }}</span> }</div>
                      }
                    </td>
                    <td>
                      <app-status-badge [status]="statusOf(d)" [progress]="progressOf(d)" />
                      @if (errorOf(d); as err) { <div class="error-text small clamp" [title]="err">{{ err }}</div> }
                    </td>
                    <td>v{{ d.currentVersion }}</td>
                    <td>{{ d.chunkCount || '—' }}</td>
                    <td>{{ d.sizeBytes | bytes }}</td>
                    <td class="nowrap">{{ d.createdAt | date: 'medium' }}</td>
                    <td class="actions">
                      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" (click)="download(d)">Download</button>
                      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" (click)="toggleVersions(d)">{{ expanded() === d.id ? 'Hide' : 'Versions' }}</button>
                      @if (auth.canEdit()) {
                        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" (click)="versionTarget = d; versionInput.click()">New version</button>
                        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" (click)="reindex(d)" [disabled]="!isTerminal(d)">Re-index</button>
                        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm danger" (click)="remove(d)">Delete</button>
                      }
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                  @if (expanded() === d.id) {
                    <tr class="subrow">
                      <td colspan="7">
                        <table class="table table-compact">
                          <thead><tr><th>Version</th><th>Status</th><th>Chunks</th><th>Size</th><th>SHA-256</th><th>Uploaded</th><th></th></tr></thead>

                            @for (v of versions(); track v.versionNumber) {
                              <tr>
                                <td>v{{ v.versionNumber }}</td>

                                <td>{{ v.sizeBytes | bytes }}</td>

                                <td>{{ v.createdAt | date: 'medium' }}</td>
                                <td><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" (click)="download(d, v.versionNumber)">Download</button></td>
                              </tr>
                            }
                          </tbody>
                        </table>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  }
                }
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          @if (total() > pageSize) {
            <div class="pager">
              <button class="btn btn-sm" (click)="goTo(page() - 1)" [disabled]="page() === 1">Previous</button>
              <span class="muted small">Page {{ page() }} of {{ pageCount() }}</span>
              <button class="btn btn-sm" (click)="goTo(page() + 1)" [disabled]="page() >= pageCount()">Next</button>
            </div>
          }
        }
      </div>
      <input #versionInput type="file" hidden (change)="uploadVersion($event)" />
    </section>
  `,

export class KnowledgeBaseDetailPage {
  readonly id = input.required<string>();

  readonly auth = inject(AuthService);
  private readonly kbApi = inject(KnowledgeBaseApi);
  private readonly docApi = inject(DocumentApi);
  private readonly toasts = inject(ToastService);

  readonly pageSize = PAGE_SIZE;
  readonly kb = signal<KnowledgeBase | null>(null);
  readonly documents = signal<DocumentItem[]>([]);
  readonly total = signal(0);
  readonly page = signal(1);
  readonly loading = signal(true);
  readonly live = signal<Record<string, DocumentStatus>>({});
  readonly expanded = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly versions = signal<DocumentVersion[]>([]);
  versionTarget: DocumentItem | null = null;

  readonly pageCount = computed(() => Math.max(1, Math.ceil(this.total() / PAGE_SIZE)));
  readonly hasActive = computed(() => this.documents().some((d) => !TERMINAL_STATUSES.includes(this.statusOf(d))));

  constructor() {
    // Reload whenever the :id route parameter changes.
    effect(() => {
      const id = this.id();
      untracked(() => {
        this.page.set(1);
        this.kbApi.get(id).subscribe((kb) => this.kb.set(kb));
        this.reload();
      });
    });

    // Poll every 3 s, but only while at least one document is still being processed.
    interval(3000)
      .pipe(
        takeUntilDestroyed(),
        filter(() => this.hasActive()),
        switchMap(() => this.refresh$().pipe(catchError(() => EMPTY))),
      )
      .subscribe();
  }

  statusOf(d: DocumentItem) {
    return this.live()[d.id]?.status ?? d.status;
  }

  progressOf(d: DocumentItem): number | null {
    return this.live()[d.id]?.progressPercent ?? null;
  }

  errorOf(d: DocumentItem): string | null {
    const status = this.live()[d.id];
    return status && (status.status === 'FAILED' || status.status === 'RETRYING') ? status.lastError : null;
  }

  isTerminal(d: DocumentItem): boolean {
    return TERMINAL_STATUSES.includes(this.statusOf(d));
  }

  reload(): void {
    this.refresh$().subscribe();
  }

  goTo(page: number): void {
    this.page.set(page);
    this.reload();
  }

  download(d: DocumentItem, version?: number): void {
    this.docApi.download(d.id, version).subscribe((blob) => saveBlob(blob, d.fileName));
  }

  toggleVersions(d: DocumentItem): void {
    if (this.expanded() === d.id) {
      this.expanded.set(null);
      return;
    }
    this.expanded.set(d.id);
    this.versions.set([]);
    this.docApi.versions(d.id).subscribe((v) => this.versions.set(v));
  }

  reindex(d: DocumentItem): void {
    if (!confirm(`Re-index "${d.fileName}"? This uses free embedding quota.`)) return;
    this.docApi.reindex(d.id).subscribe(() => {
      this.toasts.info(`Re-indexing "${d.fileName}".`);
      this.reload();
    });
  }

  remove(d: DocumentItem): void {
    if (!confirm(`Delete "${d.fileName}"? It will no longer be searchable.`)) return;
    this.docApi.delete(d.id).subscribe(() => {
      this.toasts.success(`"${d.fileName}" deleted.`);
      this.reload();
    });
  }

  uploadVersion(event: Event): void {
    const inputEl = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    const file = inputEl.files?.[0];
    const target = this.versionTarget;
    inputEl.value = '';
    if (!file || !target) return;

    this.docApi.upload(file, this.id(), '', target.id).subscribe({
      next: (e) => {
        if (e.type === HttpEventType.Response) {
          this.toasts.success(`New version of "${target.fileName}" uploaded.`);
          this.reload();
        }
      },
      error: (e) => this.toasts.error(describeError(e)),
    });
  }

  private refresh$(): Observable<unknown> {
    return this.docApi.list(this.id(), this.page(), PAGE_SIZE).pipe(
      tap((result) => {
        this.documents.set(result.items);
        this.total.set(result.totalCount);
        this.loading.set(false);
      }),
      switchMap((result) => {
        const active = result.items.filter((d) => !TERMINAL_STATUSES.includes(d.status) || d.status === 'FAILED');
        if (!active.length) return of([] as (DocumentStatus | null)[]);
        return forkJoin(active.map((d) => this.docApi.status(d.id).pipe(catchError(() => of(null)))));
      }),
      map((statuses) => {
        const next: Record<string, DocumentStatus> = {};
        for (const s of statuses) if (s) next[s.documentId] = s;
        this.live.set(next);
        return statuses;
      }),
      catchError(() => {
        this.loading.set(false);
        return EMPTY;
      }),
    );
  }
}

frontend/src/app/features/chat/chat-models.ts
ts
import { ChatAnswer, Citation, StoredMessage, TraceStep, VerificationReport } from '../../core/models';

export type VerificationView = Pick<VerificationReport, 'status' | 'confidence' | 'correctionTriggered' | 'abstained'> &
  Partial<VerificationReport>;

export interface ChatMessageVm {
  id: string;
  role: 'user' | 'assistant';
  content: string;
  createdAt: string;
  citations: Citation[];
  verification: VerificationView | null;
  warnings: string[];
  trace: TraceStep[];
  workflow: string | null;
  latencyMs: number | null;
  cache: string | null;
  pending?: boolean;
  error?: string;
  retryQuestion?: string;
}

export function fromStored(m: StoredMessage): ChatMessageVm {
  return {
    id: m.id,
    role: m.role,
    content: m.content,
    createdAt: m.createdAt,
    citations: m.citations,
    verification: m.verificationStatus
      ? { status: m.verificationStatus, confidence: m.confidence ?? 0, correctionTriggered: m.correctionTriggered, abstained: m.abstained }
      : null,
    warnings: [],
    trace: [],
    workflow: m.workflowType,
    latencyMs: m.latencyMs,
    cache: null,
  };
}

export function fromAnswer(a: ChatAnswer): ChatMessageVm {
  return {
    id: a.messageId,
    role: 'assistant',
    content: a.answer,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    citations: a.citations,
    verification: a.verification,
    warnings: a.warnings,
    trace: a.trace,
    workflow: a.workflow,
    latencyMs: a.latencyMs,
    cache: a.cache,
  };
}

import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, computed, input, output } from '@angular/core';
import { DecimalPipe } from '@angular/common';
import { Citation } from '../../core/models';
import { VerificationBadge } from '../../shared/badges';
import { renderAnswer } from '../../shared/markdown';
import { ChatMessageVm } from './chat-models';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-answer-view',
  imports: [VerificationBadge, DecimalPipe],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    @let m = message();
    <article class="answer">
      <div class="answer-meta">
        @if (m.verification; as v) {
          <app-verification-badge [status]="v.status" [confidence]="v.confidence" [abstained]="v.abstained" />
          @if (v.correctionTriggered) {
            <span class="chip chip-info" title="Issues were detected automatically and the answer was regenerated.">
              Self-corrected{{ v.correctionAttempts ? ' ×' + v.correctionAttempts : '' }}
            </span>
          }
          @if (v.independentVerifier) { <span class="chip" title="Checked by a different AI provider than the one that wrote it">Independent check</span> }
        }
        @if (m.workflow === 'multi_agent') { <span class="chip">Multi-agent</span> }
        @if (m.cache) { <span class="chip">Cached · {{ m.cache }}</span> }
        @if (m.latencyMs) { <span class="muted small">{{ m.latencyMs / 1000 | number: '1.1-1' }} s</span> }
      </div>

      <div class="markdown" [innerHTML]="html()" (click)="onAnswerClick($event)"></div>

      @if (m.warnings.length) {
        <div class="alert alert-warning small">
          @for (w of m.warnings; track $index) { <div>{{ w }}</div> }
        </div>
      }

      @if (m.citations.length) {
        <div class="sources">
          <span class="muted small">Sources</span>
          @for (c of m.citations; track c.ordinal) {
            <button class="source" (click)="citationSelected.emit(c)" [title]="c.snippet">
              <span class="source-n">{{ c.ordinal }}</span>
              <span class="truncate">{{ c.fileName }}</span>
              @if (c.pageNumber) { <span class="muted small">p.{{ c.pageNumber }}</span> }
            </button>
          }
        </div>
      }

      @if (m.verification?.claims?.length || m.trace.length) {
        <div class="details-row">
          @if (m.verification?.claims?.length) {
            <details>
              <summary>Verification details</summary>
              <table class="table table-compact">
                <thead><tr><th>#</th><th>Claim</th><th>Status</th><th>Notes</th></tr></thead>
                <tbody>
                  @for (claim of m.verification!.claims!; track claim.id) {
                    <tr>
                      <td>{{ claim.id }}</td>
                      <td>{{ claim.claim }}</td>
                      <td><span class="badge" [attr.data-tone]="claimTone(claim.status)">{{ claim.status }}</span></td>
                      <td class="small muted">{{ claim.checks.join('; ') || claim.rationale }}</td>
                    </tr>
                  }
                </tbody>
              </table>
              @if (m.verification!.missingAspects?.length) {
                <p class="small"><strong>Not covered:</strong> {{ m.verification!.missingAspects!.join(', ') }}</p>
              }
              @if (m.verification!.sourceConflicts?.length) {
                <p class="small"><strong>Sources disagree:</strong> {{ m.verification!.sourceConflicts!.join(' · ') }}</p>
              }
              <p class="small muted">
                Verifier: {{ m.verification!.verifier || 'n/a' }} · {{ m.verification!.groundingNote }}
              </p>
            </details>
          }
          @if (m.trace.length) {
            <details>
              <summary>Agent trace ({{ m.trace.length }} steps)</summary>
              <ol class="trace">
                @for (step of m.trace; track $index) {
                  <li>
                    <span class="trace-node">{{ step.node }}</span>
                    <span class="trace-bar"><span [style.width.%]="(step.durationMs / maxDuration()) * 100"></span></span>
                    <span class="small muted">{{ step.durationMs }} ms</span>
                    <span class="small muted trace-detail">{{ step.detail }}</span>
                  </li>
                }
              </ol>
            </details>
          }
        </div>
      }
    </article>
  `,
})
export class AnswerView {
  readonly message = input.required<ChatMessageVm>();
  readonly citationSelected = output<Citation>();

  readonly html = computed(() => renderAnswer(this.message().content));
  readonly maxDuration = computed(() => Math.max(1, ...this.message().trace.map((t) => t.durationMs)));

  claimTone(status: string): string {
    return status === 'SUPPORTED' ? 'success' : status === 'CONTRADICTED' ? 'danger' : status === 'UNCERTAIN' ? 'warning' : 'neutral';
  }

  /** Clicking an inline [n] marker opens the matching source. */
  onAnswerClick(event: MouseEvent): void {
    const marker = (event.target as HTMLElement).closest('sup.cite');
    if (!marker) return;
    const ordinal = Number(marker.textContent);
    const citation = this.message().citations.find((c) => c.ordinal === ordinal);
    if (citation) this.citationSelected.emit(citation);
  }
}

ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, inject, input, output, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { DecimalPipe } from '@angular/common';
import { DocumentApi } from '../../core/api/document.api';
import { Citation } from '../../core/models';
import { saveBlob, showBlobIn } from '../../shared/download';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-source-viewer',
  imports: [DecimalPipe],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  host: { '(document:keydown.escape)': 'closed.emit()' },
  template: `
    @if (citation(); as c) {
      <div class="drawer-backdrop" (click)="closed.emit()"></div>
      <aside class="drawer" role="dialog" aria-label="Source">
        <header class="drawer-header">
          <div>
            <div class="muted small">Source [{{ c.ordinal }}]</div>
            <h2 class="truncate">{{ c.fileName }}</h2>
          </div>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-icon" (click)="closed.emit()" aria-label="Close">×</button>
        </header>
        <dl class="meta">
          @if (c.pageNumber) { <dt>Page</dt><dd>{{ c.pageNumber }}</dd> }
          @if (c.section) { <dt>Section</dt><dd>{{ c.section }}</dd> }
          <dt>Version</dt><dd>v{{ c.documentVersion }}</dd>
          <dt>Retrieval score</dt><dd>{{ c.score | number: '1.3-3' }}</dd>
        </dl>
        <h3 class="small muted">Passage used as evidence</h3>
        <blockquote class="passage">{{ c.snippet }}</blockquote>
        <div class="toolbar">
          <button class="btn btn-primary" (click)="open(c)" [disabled]="busy()">
            {{ c.pageNumber ? 'Open at page ' + c.pageNumber : 'Open document' }}
          </button>
          <button class="btn" (click)="download(c)" [disabled]="busy()">Download</button>
        </div>
      </aside>
    }
  `,
})
export class SourceViewer {
  private readonly documents = inject(DocumentApi);
  readonly citation = input<Citation | null>(null);
  readonly closed = output<void>();
  readonly busy = signal(false);

  open(c: Citation): void {
    const target = window.open('', '_blank');   // opened synchronously in the click -> not blocked
    this.busy.set(true);
    this.documents.download(c.documentId, c.documentVersion).subscribe({
      next: (blob) => showBlobIn(target, blob, c.fileName, c.pageNumber),
      error: () => {
        target?.close();
        this.busy.set(false);
      },
      complete: () => this.busy.set(false),
    });
  }

  download(c: Citation): void {
    this.busy.set(true);
    this.documents.download(c.documentId, c.documentVersion).subscribe({
      next: (blob) => saveBlob(blob, c.fileName),
      error: () => this.busy.set(false),
      complete: () => this.busy.set(false),
    });
  }
}

import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, computed, effect, ElementRef, inject, input, signal, untracked, viewChild,
} from '@angular/core';
import { DatePipe } from '@angular/common';
import { HttpErrorResponse } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { firstValueFrom } from 'rxjs';
import { ChatApi } from '../../core/api/chat.api';
import { KnowledgeBaseApi } from '../../core/api/knowledge-base.api';
import { describeError } from '../../core/http/error.interceptor';
import { Citation, KnowledgeBase, Session } from '../../core/models';
import { AnswerView } from './answer-view';
import { ChatMessageVm, fromAnswer, fromStored } from './chat-models';
import { SourceViewer } from './source-viewer';

const EXAMPLES = [
  'Summarize the key points of our leave policy.',
  'Compare India and US leave policies based on annual leave, sick leave and carry-forward rules.',
  'Which documents mention eligibility requirements?',
];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-chat-page',
  imports: [RouterLink, DatePipe, AnswerView, SourceViewer],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <div class="chat-layout">
      <aside class="chat-sessions card">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-block" (click)="showNew.set(!showNew())">+ New conversation</button>

        @if (showNew()) {
          <div class="new-session">
            <p class="small muted">Search in:</p>
            @for (kb of knowledgeBases(); track kb.id) {
              <label class="check">
                <input type="checkbox" [checked]="selectedKbs().has(kb.id)" (change)="toggleKb(kb.id)" />
                <span class="truncate">{{ kb.name }}</span>
              </label>
            } @empty {
              <p class="small muted">You have no knowledge bases yet.</p>
            }
            <p class="small muted">None selected = all knowledge bases you can access.</p>
            <button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" (click)="createSession()" [disabled]="!knowledgeBases().length">Start</button>
          </div>
        }

        <nav class="session-list">
          @for (s of sessions(); track s.id) {
            <a [routerLink]="['/chat', s.id]" [class.active]="s.id === currentId()">
              <span class="session-title truncate">{{ s.title }}</span>
              <span class="muted small">{{ s.messageCount }} messages · {{ s.createdAt | date: 'short' }}</span>
            </a>
          } @empty {
            <p class="muted small">No conversations yet.</p>
          }
        </nav>
      </aside>

      <section class="chat-main card">
        <header class="chat-header">
          <h1 class="truncate">{{ current()?.title || 'New conversation' }}</h1>
          <label class="check small" title="Turn off for faster, unverified answers (uses less AI quota)">
            <input type="checkbox" [checked]="verify()" (change)="verify.set(!verify())" /> Verify answers
          </label>
        </header>

        <div class="chat-scroller" #scroller>
          @if (loadingSession()) {
            <div class="empty-state"><span class="spinner"></span></div>
          } @else if (messages().length === 0) {
            <div class="empty-state chat-empty">
              <h2>Ask about your documents</h2>
              <p class="muted">Every answer cites its sources and is checked automatically against them.</p>
              <div class="examples">
                @for (example of examples; track example) {
                  <button class="btn btn-ghost example" (click)="draft.set(example)">{{ example }}</button>
                }
              </div>
            </div>
          }

          @for (m of messages(); track m.id) {
            @if (m.role === 'user') {
              <div class="msg msg-user"><div class="bubble">{{ m.content }}</div></div>
            } @else if (m.pending) {
              <div class="msg msg-assistant">
                <div class="thinking"><span class="spinner spinner-sm"></span>
                  Retrieving evidence, writing and verifying the answer… {{ elapsed() }} s
                </div>
              </div>
            } @else if (m.error) {
              <div class="msg msg-assistant">
                <div class="alert alert-error">
                  <div>{{ m.error }}</div>
                  @if (m.retryQuestion) { <button class="btn btn-sm" (click)="retry(m)" [disabled]="sending()">Retry</button> }
                </div>
              </div>
            } @else {
              <div class="msg msg-assistant">
                <app-answer-view [message]="m" (citationSelected)="selectedCitation.set($event)" />
              </div>
            }
          }
        </div>

        <footer class="composer">
          <textarea class="input" rows="2" maxlength="4000"
                    placeholder="Ask about your documents… (Enter to send, Shift+Enter for a new line)"
                    [value]="draft()" (input)="draft.set($any($event.target).value)"
                    (keydown.enter)="onEnter($event)" [disabled]="sending()"></textarea>
          <button class="btn btn-primary" (click)="send()" [disabled]="sending() || !draft().trim()">Send</button>
        </footer>
      </section>

      <app-source-viewer [citation]="selectedCitation()" (closed)="selectedCitation.set(null)" />
    </div>
  `,
})
export class ChatPage {
  readonly sessionId = input<string | undefined>();

  private readonly chatApi = inject(ChatApi);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);
  private readonly scroller = viewChild<ElementRef<HTMLElement>>('scroller');

  readonly examples = EXAMPLES;
  readonly sessions = signal<Session[]>([]);
  readonly knowledgeBases = signal<KnowledgeBase[]>([]);
  readonly currentId = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly messages = signal<ChatMessageVm[]>([]);
  readonly loadingSession = signal(false);
  readonly sending = signal(false);
  readonly elapsed = signal(0);
  readonly verify = signal(true);
  readonly draft = signal('');
  readonly showNew = signal(false);
  readonly selectedKbs = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());
  readonly selectedCitation = signal<Citation | null>(null);
  readonly current = computed(() => this.sessions().find((s) => s.id === this.currentId()) ?? null);

  constructor() {
    this.reloadSessions();
    inject(KnowledgeBaseApi).list().subscribe((list) => this.knowledgeBases.set(list));

    // Load a session when the route changes (but not when we just created it ourselves).
    effect(() => {
      const id = this.sessionId() ?? null;
      untracked(() => {
        if (id !== this.currentId()) this.openSession(id);
      });
    });
  }

  onEnter(event: Event): void {
    const keyboard = event as KeyboardEvent;
    if (keyboard.shiftKey) return;
    keyboard.preventDefault();
    void this.send();
  }

  toggleKb(id: string): void {
    this.selectedKbs.update((set) => {
      const next = new Set(set);
      if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
      else next.add(id);
      return next;
    });
  }

  createSession(): void {
    const ids = [...this.selectedKbs()];
    this.chatApi.createSession({ title: null, knowledgeBaseIds: ids.length ? ids : null }).subscribe((session) => {
      this.sessions.update((list) => [session, ...list]);
      this.showNew.set(false);
      this.selectedKbs.set(new Set());
      void this.router.navigate(['/chat', session.id]);
    });
  }

  async send(): Promise<void> {
    const question = this.draft().trim();
    if (!question || this.sending()) return;
    this.sending.set(true);

    let sessionId = this.currentId();
    if (!sessionId) {
      try {
        const session = await firstValueFrom(this.chatApi.createSession({ title: null, knowledgeBaseIds: null }));
        sessionId = session.id;
        this.currentId.set(session.id);           // set first so the route effect does not reload
        this.sessions.update((list) => [session, ...list]);
        void this.router.navigate(['/chat', session.id], { replaceUrl: true });
      } catch {
        this.sending.set(false);
        return;
      }
    }

    this.draft.set('');
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    this.messages.update((list) => [
      ...list,
      this.blank(crypto.randomUUID(), 'user', question, now),
      { ...this.blank('pending', 'assistant', '', now), pending: true },
    ]);
    this.scrollToBottom();

    this.elapsed.set(0);
    const timer = setInterval(() => this.elapsed.update((s) => s + 1), 1000);

    this.chatApi.query({ sessionId, question, topK: 8, verify: this.verify() }).subscribe({
      next: (answer) => {
        this.replacePending(fromAnswer(answer));
        this.reloadSessions();
      },
      error: (e: HttpErrorResponse) =>
        this.replacePending({ ...this.blank(crypto.randomUUID(), 'assistant', '', now), error: describeError(e), retryQuestion: question }),
    }).add(() => {
      clearInterval(timer);
      this.sending.set(false);
      this.scrollToBottom();
    });
  }

  retry(failed: ChatMessageVm): void {
    if (!failed.retryQuestion) return;
    // Remove the failed answer and its question, then ask again.
    this.messages.update((list) => {
      const index = list.findIndex((m) => m.id === failed.id);
      return index > 0 ? list.slice(0, index - 1) : list.filter((m) => m.id !== failed.id);
    });
    this.draft.set(failed.retryQuestion);
    void this.send();
  }

  private openSession(id: string | null): void {
    this.currentId.set(id);
    this.messages.set([]);
    this.selectedCitation.set(null);
    if (!id) return;
    this.loadingSession.set(true);
    this.chatApi.getSession(id).subscribe({
      next: (detail) => {
        this.messages.set(detail.messages.map(fromStored));
        this.loadingSession.set(false);
        this.scrollToBottom();
      },
      error: () => {
        this.loadingSession.set(false);
        void this.router.navigate(['/chat']);
      },
    });
  }

  private reloadSessions(): void {
    this.chatApi.listSessions().subscribe((list) => this.sessions.set(list));
  }

  private replacePending(message: ChatMessageVm): void {
    this.messages.update((list) => list.map((m) => (m.pending ? message : m)));
  }

  private blank(id: string, role: 'user' | 'assistant', content: string, createdAt: string): ChatMessageVm {
    return { id, role, content, createdAt, citations: [], verification: null, warnings: [], trace: [], workflow: null, latencyMs: null, cache: null };
  }

  private scrollToBottom(): void {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const element = this.scroller()?.nativeElement;
      if (element) element.scrollTop = element.scrollHeight;
    });
  }
}

Known limitations (part 1)
Refresh token in localStorage. An XSS bug could steal it. Mitigations already in place: Angular's sanitization, no bypassSecurityTrust* anywhere, and short-lived access tokens kept in memory. The stronger fix is an httpOnly, SameSite cookie for the refresh token, which needs a small API change. I'll include it in Phase 15 (security).
Status polling runs every 3 seconds, only while documents are processing, and only fetches status for active documents. Server-sent events would be more efficient but add infrastructure; polling is adequate at this scale.
Native confirm() dialogs for delete and re-index. They're functional; a custom dialog would be a cosmetic upgrade.
Older messages show a verification summary only (status, confidence, correction flag, abstained). Per-claim details and the agent trace appear only for answers given during the current page session, because those details aren't returned when a session is reloaded.

Vitest tests for the auth service, interceptors, and key components.
A production Dockerfile and nginx config for the frontend (static hosting plus an /api reverse proxy and security headers).

All analytics endpoints require the TenantAdmin (or PlatformAdmin) role. Tenant-specific numbers come from tenant-filtered tables. Provider quota numbers are platform-wide, because the free quotas are shared by every tenant; the screen labels this clearly.

Part A — Python: live quota endpoint
Edit ai-services/app/providers/quota.py

There are four changes.

1. Add this import at the top:

python
from app.messaging.contracts import CamelModel

2. Add the model below the QuotaLimits dataclass:

python
class QuotaStatus(CamelModel):
    provider: str
    requests_today: int
    daily_limit: int | None = None
    requests_per_minute: int | None = None
    tokens_per_minute: int | None = None
    usage_ratio: float | None = None
    status: str = "ok"            # ok | warning | exhausted

3. Add get_int to the CounterStore protocol and to both stores:

python
# in class CounterStore(Protocol):
    async def get_int(self, key: str) -> int: ...

# in class RedisCounterStore:
    async def get_int(self, key: str) -> int:
        value = await self._redis.get(key)
        return int(value) if value is not None else 0

# in class InMemoryCounterStore:
    async def get_int(self, key: str) -> int:
        return self.values.get(key, 0)

4. Add this method to QuotaGuard:

python
    async def snapshot(self) -> list[QuotaStatus]:
        """Today's usage per provider vs. its configured free daily limit (counters reset 00:00 UTC)."""
        day = time.strftime("%Y%m%d", time.gmtime(self._clock()))
        statuses: list[QuotaStatus] = []
        for provider, limits in self._limits.items():
            try:
                used = await self._store.get_int(f"nx:quota:{provider}:rpd:{day}")
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
                used = 0
            ratio = used / limits.rpd if limits.rpd else None
            if ratio is not None and ratio >= 1:
                status = "exhausted"
            elif ratio is not None and ratio >= self._warning_ratio:
                status = "warning"
            else:
                status = "ok"
            statuses.append(QuotaStatus(provider=provider, requests_today=used, daily_limit=limits.rpd,
                                        requests_per_minute=limits.rpm, tokens_per_minute=limits.tpm,
                                        usage_ratio=round(ratio, 3) if ratio is not None else None, status=status))
        return statuses
ai-services/app/api/internal.py (replace)
python
"""Internal endpoints - callable only by the .NET backend (service token required)."""

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Request

from app.core.security import require_service_token
from app.providers.quota import QuotaStatus

router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1", tags=["internal"], dependencies=[Depends(require_service_token)])


@router.get("/providers")
async def provider_status(request: Request) -> dict[str, object]:
    return request.app.state.settings.provider_summary()


@router.get("/providers/quota", response_model=list[QuotaStatus])
async def provider_quota(request: Request) -> list[QuotaStatus]:
    bundle = getattr(request.app.state, "providers", None)
    return [] if bundle is None else await bundle.quota.snapshot()

Add to ai-services/tests/test_quota.py
python
def test_snapshot_reports_warning_and_exhausted():
    store = InMemoryCounterStore()
    guard = QuotaGuard(store, {"warn": QuotaLimits(rpd=10), "full": QuotaLimits(rpd=2), "free": QuotaLimits()},
                       warning_ratio=0.8, clock=lambda: 1_000_000.0)

    async def scenario():
        for _ in range(8):
            await guard.acquire("warn")
        for _ in range(2):
            await guard.acquire("full")
        return {s.provider: s for s in await guard.snapshot()}

    result = asyncio.run(scenario())
    assert result["warn"].status == "warning" and result["warn"].requests_today == 8
    assert result["full"].status == "exhausted"
    assert result["free"].usage_ratio is None and result["free"].status == "ok"

csharp
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<AiQuotaStatus>?> GetQuotaAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        try
        {
            return await httpClient.GetFromJsonAsync<List<AiQuotaStatus>>("internal/v1/providers/quota", JsonOptions, cancellationToken);
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            throw;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            logger.LogWarning(ex, "Could not read live provider quota");
            return null;
        }
    }

backend/NexusRAG.Application/Analytics/AnalyticsContracts.cs
csharp
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Analytics;

public sealed record DailyUsagePoint(DateOnly Day, int Queries, int Ingestions, long InputTokens, long OutputTokens,
    long EmbeddingTokens, int CacheHits);

public sealed record ProviderModelUsage(string Provider, string Model, string Operation, int Operations,
    long InputTokens, long OutputTokens, long EmbeddingTokens);

public sealed record UsageSummaryDto(int Days, int TotalQueries, int CacheHits, long InputTokens, long OutputTokens,
    long EmbeddingTokens, decimal EstimatedCostUsd, IReadOnlyList<DailyUsagePoint> Daily, IReadOnlyList<ProviderModelUsage> ByModel);

public sealed record ProviderCost(string Provider, long Requests, decimal CostUsd);

public sealed record CostSummaryDto(int Days, decimal TotalUsd, IReadOnlyList<ProviderCost> ByProvider, string Note);

public sealed record ProviderDailyPoint(DateOnly Day, string Provider, string Model, int Requests, int Failures,
    int RateLimited, long InputTokens, long OutputTokens);

public sealed record ProviderAnalyticsDto(IReadOnlyList<AiQuotaStatus>? LiveQuota, IReadOnlyList<ProviderDailyPoint> History, string Note);

public sealed record KeyCount(string Key, int Count);

public sealed record QualityDto(int Days, int Answers, int Verified, double SupportedRate, double AbstentionRate,
    double CorrectionRate, double AverageConfidence, int? LatencyP50Ms, int? LatencyP95Ms, int? LatencyP99Ms,
    IReadOnlyList<KeyCount> ByStatus, IReadOnlyList<KeyCount> ByWorkflow);

public sealed record DailyCachePoint(DateOnly Day, int Answers, int Hits);

public sealed record CacheAnalyticsDto(int Days, int Answers, int CacheHits, int ExactHits, int SemanticHits, double HitRate,
    IReadOnlyList<DailyCachePoint> Daily);

public sealed record VerificationHistoryItem(Guid MessageId, Guid ConversationId, string ConversationTitle, string AnswerExcerpt,
    string Status, double Confidence, int CorrectionAttempts, bool Abstained, string? Workflow, int? LatencyMs,
    DateTimeOffset CreatedAt, bool OwnedByCurrentUser);

public interface IAnalyticsService
{
    Task<UsageSummaryDto> GetUsageAsync(int days, CancellationToken ct);
    Task<CostSummaryDto> GetCostsAsync(int days, CancellationToken ct);
    Task<ProviderAnalyticsDto> GetProvidersAsync(int days, CancellationToken ct);
    Task<QualityDto> GetQualityAsync(int days, CancellationToken ct);
    Task<CacheAnalyticsDto> GetCacheAsync(int days, CancellationToken ct);
    Task<PagedResult<VerificationHistoryItem>> GetVerificationHistoryAsync(string? status, int page, int pageSize, CancellationToken ct);
}
backend/NexusRAG.Application/Analytics/AnalyticsService.cs
csharp
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Chat;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Ingestion;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Analytics;

/// <summary>
/// Read-only analytics. Rows for the period are fetched with narrow projections and aggregated in memory
/// (provider-agnostic, testable). Bounded by MaxRows; Phase 18 moves heavy aggregation into SQL if benchmarks need it.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class AnalyticsService(
    IApplicationDbContext db,
    ICurrentUser currentUser,
    IAiServiceClient ai,
    TimeProvider time) : IAnalyticsService
{
    private const int MaxRows = 50_000;
    private static readonly HashSet<string> HistoryFilters =
        ["SUPPORTED", "UNCERTAIN", "CONTRADICTED", "INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE", "ABSTAINED"];

    public async Task<UsageSummaryDto> GetUsageAsync(int days, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var (effectiveDays, from, fromDay, today) = Period(days);

        var usage = await db.UsageRecords.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(u => u.CreatedAt >= from).OrderByDescending(u => u.CreatedAt).Take(MaxRows)
            .Select(u => new { u.CreatedAt, u.Provider, u.Model, u.Operation, u.InputTokens, u.OutputTokens, u.EmbeddingTokens, u.CacheHit })
            .ToListAsync(ct);
        var answers = await AnswerTimesAsync(from, ct);
        var ingestions = await db.IngestionJobs.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(j => j.Status == IngestionStatus.Indexed && j.CompletedAt >= from)
            .Select(j => j.CompletedAt!.Value).Take(MaxRows).ToListAsync(ct);

        var aiUsage = usage.Where(u => !u.CacheHit).ToList();
        var aiByDay = aiUsage.ToLookup(u => Day(u.CreatedAt));
        var cacheByDay = usage.Where(u => u.CacheHit).ToLookup(u => Day(u.CreatedAt));
        var answersByDay = answers.ToLookup(Day);
        var ingestByDay = ingestions.ToLookup(Day);

        var daily = DaysBetween(fromDay, today).Select(d => new DailyUsagePoint(
            d, answersByDay[d].Count(), ingestByDay[d].Count(),
            aiByDay[d].Sum(u => (long)u.InputTokens), aiByDay[d].Sum(u => (long)u.OutputTokens),
            aiByDay[d].Sum(u => (long)u.EmbeddingTokens), cacheByDay[d].Count())).ToList();

        var byModel = aiUsage
            .GroupBy(u => new { u.Provider, u.Model, u.Operation })
            .Select(g => new ProviderModelUsage(g.Key.Provider, g.Key.Model, g.Key.Operation, g.Count(),
                g.Sum(u => (long)u.InputTokens), g.Sum(u => (long)u.OutputTokens), g.Sum(u => (long)u.EmbeddingTokens)))
            .OrderByDescending(m => m.Operations).ToList();

        return new UsageSummaryDto(effectiveDays, answers.Count, usage.Count(u => u.CacheHit),
            aiUsage.Sum(u => (long)u.InputTokens), aiUsage.Sum(u => (long)u.OutputTokens),
            aiUsage.Sum(u => (long)u.EmbeddingTokens), 0m, daily, byModel);
    }

    public async Task<CostSummaryDto> GetCostsAsync(int days, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var (effectiveDays, _, fromDay, _) = Period(days);
        var byProvider = await db.ProviderUsages.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(p => p.Day >= fromDay)
            .GroupBy(p => p.Provider)
            .Select(g => new { Provider = g.Key, Requests = g.Sum(p => (long)p.Requests) })
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        return new CostSummaryDto(effectiveDays, 0m,
            byProvider.Select(p => new ProviderCost(p.Provider, p.Requests, 0m)).ToList(),
            "All configured AI providers are free tiers and NexusRAG refuses paid providers, so AI cost is $0. "
            + "Shown for transparency; infrastructure hosting costs are not included.");
    }

    public async Task<ProviderAnalyticsDto> GetProvidersAsync(int days, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var (_, _, fromDay, _) = Period(days);
        var history = await db.ProviderUsages.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(p => p.Day >= fromDay)
            .OrderBy(p => p.Day).ThenBy(p => p.Provider)
            .Select(p => new ProviderDailyPoint(p.Day, p.Provider, p.Model, p.Requests, p.Failures,
                p.RateLimitedCount, p.InputTokens, p.OutputTokens))
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        IReadOnlyList<AiQuotaStatus>? live;
        using var timeout = CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(ct);
        timeout.CancelAfter(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));
        try
        {
            live = await ai.GetQuotaAsync(timeout.Token);
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (!ct.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            live = null;
        }

        return new ProviderAnalyticsDto(live, history,
            "Provider usage is platform-wide because free quotas are shared by all tenants. Live counters reset at "
            + "00:00 UTC; Google's Gemini free quota resets at midnight Pacific time.");
    }

    public async Task<QualityDto> GetQualityAsync(int days, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var (effectiveDays, from, _, _) = Period(days);
        var answers = await db.Messages.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(m => m.Role == MessageRole.Assistant && m.CreatedAt >= from)
            .Select(m => new
            {
                m.WorkflowType,
                m.LatencyMs,
                m.CorrectionTriggered,
                Status = m.Verification != null ? m.Verification.OverallStatus : null,
                Confidence = m.Verification != null ? (double?)m.Verification.Confidence : null,
                Abstained = m.Verification != null && m.Verification.Abstained,
            })
            .Take(MaxRows).ToListAsync(ct);

        var verified = answers.Where(a => a.Status != null).ToList();
        var latencies = answers.Where(a => a.LatencyMs is > 0).Select(a => a.LatencyMs!.Value).Order().ToList();

        return new QualityDto(
            effectiveDays, answers.Count, verified.Count,
            Ratio(verified.Count(a => a.Status == "SUPPORTED" && !a.Abstained), verified.Count),
            Ratio(verified.Count(a => a.Abstained), verified.Count),
            Ratio(answers.Count(a => a.CorrectionTriggered), answers.Count),
            verified.Count == 0 ? 0 : Math.Round(verified.Average(a => a.Confidence ?? 0), 3),
            Percentile(latencies, 50), Percentile(latencies, 95), Percentile(latencies, 99),
            verified.GroupBy(a => a.Abstained ? "ABSTAINED" : a.Status!).Select(g => new KeyCount(g.Key, g.Count()))
                .OrderByDescending(k => k.Count).ToList(),
            answers.GroupBy(a => a.WorkflowType ?? "unknown").Select(g => new KeyCount(g.Key, g.Count()))
                .OrderByDescending(k => k.Count).ToList());
    }

    public async Task<CacheAnalyticsDto> GetCacheAsync(int days, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var (effectiveDays, from, fromDay, today) = Period(days);
        var answers = await AnswerTimesAsync(from, ct);
        var hits = await db.UsageRecords.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(u => u.CacheHit && u.CreatedAt >= from)
            .Select(u => new { u.CreatedAt, u.Model }).Take(MaxRows).ToListAsync(ct);

        var answersByDay = answers.ToLookup(Day);
        var hitsByDay = hits.ToLookup(h => Day(h.CreatedAt));
        return new CacheAnalyticsDto(effectiveDays, answers.Count, hits.Count,
            hits.Count(h => h.Model == "exact"), hits.Count(h => h.Model == "semantic"),
            Ratio(hits.Count, answers.Count),
            DaysBetween(fromDay, today).Select(d => new DailyCachePoint(d, answersByDay[d].Count(), hitsByDay[d].Count())).ToList());
    }

    public async Task<PagedResult<VerificationHistoryItem>> GetVerificationHistoryAsync(string? status, int page, int pageSize,
        CancellationToken ct)
    {
        page = Math.Max(1, page);
        pageSize = Math.Clamp(pageSize, 1, 50);

        var query =
            from v in db.VerificationResults.AsNoTracking()
            join m in db.Messages.AsNoTracking() on v.MessageId equals m.Id
            join c in db.Conversations.AsNoTracking() on m.ConversationId equals c.Id
            select new
            {
                v.MessageId, v.OverallStatus, v.Confidence, v.CorrectionAttempts, v.Abstained,
                m.Content, m.WorkflowType, m.LatencyMs, m.CreatedAt,
                ConversationId = c.Id, ConversationTitle = c.Title, OwnerId = c.UserId,
            };

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(status))
        {
            var filter = status.Trim().ToUpperInvariant();
            if (!HistoryFilters.Contains(filter)) throw new FluentValidation.ValidationException($"Unknown status filter '{status}'.");
            query = filter == "ABSTAINED" ? query.Where(x => x.Abstained) : query.Where(x => x.OverallStatus == filter && !x.Abstained);
        }

        var total = await query.CountAsync(ct);
        var rows = await query.OrderByDescending(x => x.CreatedAt).Skip((page - 1) * pageSize).Take(pageSize).ToListAsync(ct);
        var userId = currentUser.UserId;

        var items = rows.Select(r => new VerificationHistoryItem(
            r.MessageId, r.ConversationId, r.ConversationTitle,
            r.Content.Length > 220 ? r.Content[..220] + "…" : r.Content,
            r.OverallStatus, r.Confidence, r.CorrectionAttempts, r.Abstained, r.WorkflowType, r.LatencyMs,
            r.CreatedAt, r.OwnerId == userId)).ToList();
        return new PagedResult<VerificationHistoryItem>(items, page, pageSize, total);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers
    private Task<List<DateTimeOffset>> AnswerTimesAsync(DateTimeOffset from, CancellationToken ct) =>
        db.Messages.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(m => m.Role == MessageRole.Assistant && m.CreatedAt >= from)
            .Select(m => m.CreatedAt).Take(MaxRows).ToListAsync(ct);

    private (int Days, DateTimeOffset From, DateOnly FromDay, DateOnly Today) Period(int days)
    {
        days = Math.Clamp(days, 1, 90);
        var today = DateOnly.FromDateTime(time.GetUtcNow().UtcDateTime);
        var fromDay = today.AddDays(-(days - 1));
        return (days, new DateTimeOffset(fromDay.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue), TimeSpan.Zero), fromDay, today);
    }

    private static IEnumerable<DateOnly> DaysBetween(DateOnly from, DateOnly to)
    {
        for (var day = from; day <= to; day = day.AddDays(1)) yield return day;
    }

    private static DateOnly Day(DateTimeOffset value) => DateOnly.FromDateTime(value.UtcDateTime);

    private static double Ratio(int part, int whole) => whole == 0 ? 0 : Math.Round((double)part / whole, 4);

    /// <summary>Nearest-rank percentile on a sorted list.</summary>
    private static int? Percentile(List<int> sorted, double percentile) =>
        sorted.Count == 0
            ? null
            : sorted[Math.Clamp((int)Math.Ceiling(percentile / 100.0 * sorted.Count) - 1, 0, sorted.Count - 1)];
}
backend/NexusRAG.Application/Tenants/TenantService.cs
csharp
using FluentValidation;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Common;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Operations;

namespace NexusRAG.Application.Tenants;

public sealed record TenantDto(Guid Id, string Name, string Slug, bool IsActive, DateTimeOffset CreatedAt,
    int Users, int KnowledgeBases, int Documents);

public sealed record UpdateTenantRequest(string Name);

public interface ITenantService
{
    Task<TenantDto> GetCurrentAsync(CancellationToken ct);
    Task<TenantDto> UpdateCurrentAsync(UpdateTenantRequest request, CancellationToken ct);
}

internal sealed class UpdateTenantRequestValidator : AbstractValidator<UpdateTenantRequest>
{
    public UpdateTenantRequestValidator() => RuleFor(x => x.Name).NotEmpty().MaximumLength(200);
}

internal sealed class TenantService(
    IApplicationDbContext db,
    ICurrentUser currentUser,
    IValidator<UpdateTenantRequest> validator) : ITenantService
{
    public async Task<TenantDto> GetCurrentAsync(CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var tenant = await db.Tenants.AsNoTracking().FirstOrDefaultAsync(t => t.Id == currentUser.TenantId, ct)
                     ?? throw new NotFoundException("Tenant", currentUser.TenantId);
        // Users / KnowledgeBases / Documents are tenant-filtered automatically.
        return new TenantDto(tenant.Id, tenant.Name, tenant.Slug, tenant.IsActive, tenant.CreatedAt,
            await db.Users.CountAsync(ct), await db.KnowledgeBases.CountAsync(ct), await db.Documents.CountAsync(ct));
    }

    public async Task<TenantDto> UpdateCurrentAsync(UpdateTenantRequest request, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        await validator.ValidateAndThrowAsync(request, ct);
        var tenant = await db.Tenants.FirstOrDefaultAsync(t => t.Id == currentUser.TenantId, ct)
                     ?? throw new NotFoundException("Tenant", currentUser.TenantId);
        tenant.Name = request.Name.Trim();
        db.AuditLogs.Add(AuditLog.Create(tenant.Id, currentUser.UserId, "tenant.renamed", "Tenant", tenant.Id.ToString()));
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        return await GetCurrentAsync(ct);
    }
}

Edit backend/NexusRAG.API/Program.cs (running behind nginx)

Without this change, every request would appear to come from nginx's IP, and the per-IP rate limits would effectively apply to all users at once.

csharp
// add at the top
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.HttpOverrides;

// add with the other service registrations
builder.Services.Configure<ForwardedHeadersOptions>(options =>
{
    options.ForwardedHeaders = ForwardedHeaders.XForwardedFor | ForwardedHeaders.XForwardedProto;
    options.ForwardLimit = 1;              // exactly one trusted hop: nginx
    options.KnownNetworks.Clear();         // nginx's Docker IP is dynamic. Only safe if the API is NOT publicly reachable
    options.KnownProxies.Clear();          // directly in production (expose it through nginx only).
});

// make this the FIRST middleware, right after `var app = builder.Build();` and the initializer
app.UseForwardedHeaders();

On .NET 10, KnownNetworks may produce an obsolete warning that suggests KnownIPNetworks. If it does, use KnownIPNetworks.Clear() instead; the behavior is identical.

backend/NexusRAG.Tests/Analytics/AnalyticsServiceTests.cs
csharp
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Moq;
using NexusRAG.Application.Abstractions;
using NexusRAG.Application.Analytics;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Chat;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Identity;
using NexusRAG.Domain.Operations;
using NexusRAG.Infrastructure.Persistence;

namespace NexusRAG.Tests.Analytics;

public class AnalyticsServiceTests
{
    private sealed class FixedTenant(Guid tenantId) : ITenantContext
    {
        public Guid? TenantId => tenantId;
        public bool BypassTenantFilter => false;
    }

    private static readonly Guid Tenant = Guid.NewGuid();
    private static readonly Guid User = Guid.NewGuid();

    private static NexusRagDbContext NewContext(string name) =>
        new(new DbContextOptionsBuilder<NexusRagDbContext>().UseInMemoryDatabase(name).Options,
            new FixedTenant(Tenant), TimeProvider.System);

    private static ICurrentUser Admin()
    {
        var user = new Mock<ICurrentUser>();
        user.SetupGet(u => u.TenantId).Returns(Tenant);
        user.SetupGet(u => u.UserId).Returns(User);
        user.Setup(u => u.IsInRole(RoleNames.TenantAdmin)).Returns(true);
        return user.Object;
    }

    private static async Task<string> SeedAsync()
    {
        var name = Guid.NewGuid().ToString();
        await using var db = NewContext(name);
        var conversation = new Conversation { TenantId = Tenant, UserId = User, Title = "Leave" };
        db.Conversations.Add(conversation);

        void Answer(string status, bool abstained, bool corrected, int latency, double confidence, string workflow)
        {
            var message = new Message
            {
                TenantId = Tenant, ConversationId = conversation.Id, Role = MessageRole.Assistant, Content = "answer",
                CorrectionTriggered = corrected, LatencyMs = latency, WorkflowType = workflow,
            };
            message.Verification = new VerificationResult
            {
                TenantId = Tenant, MessageId = message.Id, OverallStatus = status, Confidence = confidence, Abstained = abstained,
            };
            db.Messages.Add(message);
        }

        Answer("SUPPORTED", false, false, 1000, 0.9, "simple");
        Answer("SUPPORTED", false, true, 2000, 0.8, "multi_agent");
        Answer("UNCERTAIN", false, false, 3000, 0.5, "simple");
        Answer("INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE", true, true, 9000, 0.0, "simple");

        db.UsageRecords.AddRange(
            new UsageRecord { TenantId = Tenant, Operation = "chat", Provider = "groq", Model = "llama", InputTokens = 900, OutputTokens = 100 },
            new UsageRecord { TenantId = Tenant, Operation = "query", Provider = "cache", Model = "exact", CacheHit = true });
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        return name;
    }

    private static AnalyticsService Service(NexusRagDbContext db, IAiServiceClient? ai = null) =>
        new(db, Admin(), ai ?? Mock.Of<IAiServiceClient>(), TimeProvider.System);

    [Fact]
    public async Task Quality_ComputesRatesAndLatencyPercentiles()
    {
        await using var db = NewContext(await SeedAsync());
        var quality = await Service(db).GetQualityAsync(30, default);

        Assert.Equal(4, quality.Answers);
        Assert.Equal(0.5, quality.SupportedRate);          // 2 of 4 verified answers
        Assert.Equal(0.25, quality.AbstentionRate);
        Assert.Equal(0.5, quality.CorrectionRate);
        Assert.Equal(2000, quality.LatencyP50Ms);
        Assert.Equal(9000, quality.LatencyP95Ms);
        Assert.Contains(quality.ByStatus, k => k.Key == "ABSTAINED" && k.Count == 1);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Usage_SeparatesCacheHitsFromAiTokens()
    {
        await using var db = NewContext(await SeedAsync());
        var usage = await Service(db).GetUsageAsync(7, default);

        Assert.Equal(7, usage.Daily.Count);
        Assert.Equal(4, usage.TotalQueries);
        Assert.Equal(1, usage.CacheHits);
        Assert.Equal(900, usage.InputTokens);
        Assert.Equal(0m, usage.EstimatedCostUsd);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(usage.ByModel, m => m.Provider == "cache");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Cache_ReportsHitRate()
    {
        await using var db = NewContext(await SeedAsync());
        var cache = await Service(db).GetCacheAsync(14, default);
        Assert.Equal(1, cache.ExactHits);
        Assert.Equal(0.25, cache.HitRate);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Providers_ReturnsHistory_WhenLiveQuotaIsUnavailable()
    {
        await using var db = NewContext(await SeedAsync());
        var ai = new Mock<IAiServiceClient>();
        ai.Setup(a => a.GetQuotaAsync(It.IsAny<CancellationToken>())).ReturnsAsync((IReadOnlyList<AiQuotaStatus>?)null);

        var providers = await Service(db, ai.Object).GetProvidersAsync(14, default);

        Assert.Null(providers.LiveQuota);
        Assert.NotNull(providers.History);
    }
}

import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { inject, Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import {
  CacheAnalytics, PagedResult, ProviderAnalytics, QualityMetrics, UsageSummary, VerificationHistoryItem,
} from '../models';

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class AnalyticsApi {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);

  usage(days: number) {
    return this.http.get<UsageSummary>('/api/analytics/usage', { params: { days } });
  }

  providers(days: number) {
    return this.http.get<ProviderAnalytics>('/api/analytics/providers', { params: { days } });
  }

  cache(days: number) {
    return this.http.get<CacheAnalytics>('/api/analytics/cache', { params: { days } });
  }

  quality(days: number) {
    return this.http.get<QualityMetrics>('/api/analytics/quality', { params: { days } });
  }

  verificationHistory(status: string, page: number, pageSize = 25) {
    const params: Record<string, string | number> = { page, pageSize };
    if (status) params['status'] = status;
    return this.http.get<PagedResult<VerificationHistoryItem>>('/api/analytics/verification-history', { params });
  }
}

    return this.http.get<PagedResult<UserDto>>('/api/users', { params: { page, pageSize } });
  }

    return this.http.post<UserDto>('/api/users', request);
  }

    return this.http.get<TenantInfo>('/api/tenants/current');
  }

  renameTenant(name: string) {
    return this.http.put<TenantInfo>('/api/tenants/current', { name });
  }
